Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that, while retaining only about 52--65\% of the original teacher--student discrepancy as the optimization signal, Cal-OPD consistently outperforms standard OPD and its variants across model scales.

</details>


### [35] [One Prompt Does Not Fit All: Self-Meta-Evolve for Personalized Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21626)
*Hongliang Li,Lu Wang,Yong Xu,Hanyang Chen,Zhitao Hou,Xiaoting Qin,Song Ge,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将企业信息抽取重定义为基于交互反馈的"按用户提示适配"问题，提出双层框架 Self-Meta-Evolve：内层依据角色条件反馈编辑结构化提示，外层通过蒸馏成功编辑模式进化元提示本身；并发布基于 O*NET 的292个模拟企业用户基准。该方法成功率达74.58%，超出最强基线13.56个百分点，两轮迭代即达52.54%，20名真实从业者的双盲研究中71%两两比较胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业场景中同一文档需针对不同用户重新组织，用户异质性显著；现有提示优化方法仅针对全局目标优化单一提示，与实际工作场所的个性化需求相错配。

Method: 提出 Self-Meta-Evolve 层次化框架，为每个用户维护专属提示，并通过双循环持续精化：内循环基于角色条件（persona-conditioned）反馈编辑结构化提示，外循环通过蒸馏成功编辑模式来进化元提示。同时发布292个模拟企业用户、基于 O*NET 职业分类体系的可复现角色生成管线所构建的角色驱动 IE 基准。

Result: 在该基准上成功率达74.58%，较最强提示优化基线高出13.56个绝对百分点，仅两轮迭代即达52.54%；20名真实从业者的双盲人类研究中，本框架适配的提示在71%的两两比较中优于静态基线。

Conclusion: 按用户进行提示适配并引入元提示自我进化，能有效应对企业信息抽取中的用户异质性，显著优于单提示全局优化范式，且经真实用户验证具备实用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for enterprise information extraction (IE), where the same document must be reorganized differently for each user. Existing prompt optimization methods, however, rely on a single prompt optimized against a global objective, which is misaligned with the inherent user heterogeneity of real workplaces. We formulate enterprise IE as per-user prompt adaptation under interaction feedback and propose Self-Meta-Evolve, a hierarchical framework that maintains a dedicated prompt for each user and continuously refines it through a dual-loop process: an inner loop that edits structured prompts based on persona-conditioned feedback, and an outer loop that evolves the meta-prompt itself by distilling successful editing patterns. To enable scalable training and evaluation, we release a persona-driven IE benchmark of 292 simulated enterprise users, paired with a reproducible persona-generation pipeline grounded in O*NET occupational taxonomies. On this benchmark, Self-Meta-Evolve achieves a 74.58% success rate, outperforming the strongest prompt-optimization baseline by 13.56 absolute points, and reaches 52.54\% within only two iterations. A double-blind human study with twenty real professionals further confirms that prompts adapted by our framework win against static baselines in 71% of pairwise comparisons.

</details>


### [36] [Accelerating Dense LLMs via L0-regularized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21672)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Jiudong Yang,Zhaowen Tao,Meng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: L0-MoE：一种轻量级MoE方法，通过L0正则化加速稠密LLM，在几乎无性能损失下实现最高2.5倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理慢、成本高；现有加速方法导致明显性能下降，而MoE模型需要大量计算资源。因此需要一种轻量级、低损失的加速方案。

Method: 提出L0-MoE，利用L0正则化的轻量级MoE方法；引入聚类混淆矩阵进行领域感知数据集构建，并采用动态批处理实现高效训练。

Result: 相比稠密模型最高2.5倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的性能，优于现有LLM加速基线。

Conclusion: L0-MoE在几乎不损失性能的前提下有效加速稠密LLM，是一种优于现有基线的轻量级加速方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance but suffer from slow and costly inference. Existing acceleration methods often lead to noticeable performance degradation, while Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models require extensive computational resources. In this paper, we propose L0-MoE, a lightweight MoE approach using L0-regularization to accelerate dense LLMs nearly without performance loss. Our method introduces a cluster confusion matrix for domain-aware dataset curation and applies dynamic batching for efficient training. Experiments show that L0-MoE achieves up to 2.5x speedup over dense models while maintaining competitive performance, outperforming existing LLM acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [37] [GUARD: Natural Forgetting in Large Reasoning Models via Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21677)
*Zeyu Yan,Guanghao Zhou,Minghui Qiu,Ming Gao,Cen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GUARD 方法，通过引导式答案-推理蒸馏，将大推理模型的不安全披露转化为"安全退出"轨迹，并引入 NFRS 指标评估遗忘替代质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRM）在生成最终答案前会产生中间思维链（CoT），受保护事实或不安全推理可能已在 CoT 中泄露，使传统遗忘方法失效。现有遗忘目标仅压制目标内容或重定向内部表示，却未规定遗忘后的轨迹应如何延续，导致幻觉替代、边界畸形或重复输出。

Method: 提出 Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation（GUARD）：1) 将模型自身生成的不安全披露转化为"安全退出"轨迹（连贯的非披露 CoT + 稳定拒绝式答案）；2) 通过引导 token 对齐冻结的 LRM；3) 将引导行为蒸馏进模型参数。同时引入 Natural Forgetting Reasoning Score（NFRS）指标，从结构稳定性、流畅性、无支撑替代三方面衡量替代质量。

Result: 在 R-TOFU 及基于 STAR-1 的有害意图设定上，跨两种主流蒸馏 LRM 的实验表明：GUARD 大幅降低不安全与隐私披露，同时保持推理效用。

Conclusion: LRM 遗忘应学习一条"自然遗忘轨迹"（非披露 CoT + 拒绝式答案），而非单纯压制内容；GUARD 与 NFRS 为该方向提供了可行方法与评估工具。

Abstract: Recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) have made machine unlearning more challenging, as protected facts or unsafe rationales may surface in intermediate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before the final answer is produced. Existing unlearning objectives typically suppress the target content or redirect internal representations, but they never specify how the post-forgetting trajectory should continue, which can lead to hallucinated substitutes, malformed boundaries, or repetitive outputs. We argue that LRM unlearning should instead learn a natural forgetting trajectory: a coherent non-disclosing CoT followed by a stable refusal-style answer that replace the original disclosure. To this end, we propose Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation (GUARD), which converts model-generated unsafe disclosures into safe-exit trajectories, aligns a frozen LRM via guidance tokens, and distills the guided behavior into model parameters.To address the lack of metrics for replacement quality beyond leakage, we further introduce Natural Forgetting Reasoning Score (NFRS), which captures structural stability, fluency, and unsupported substitutes in forgotten outputs. Extensive experiments on R-TOFU and a STAR-1-derived harmful-intent setting show that GUARD substantially reduces unsafe and privacy disclosures across two widely adopted distilled LRMs while preserving reasoning utility. Codes are available at https://github.com/zeyu-Yan/GUARD

</details>


### [38] [Listen Before You Speak: Response Planning from Listener Facial Reactions for Conversational Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21683)
*Yunji Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ReACT-TTS 两阶段框架，利用听者约一秒的前反应面部序列预测下一话语的情绪与韵律，再驱动 Grad-TTS 实现语音生成；在 MELD 双人对话协议下，时间条件优于纯文本条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会话语音的生成不仅依赖对话上下文，还依赖听者在对方说话时的即时反应行为；现有方法多仅使用文本上下文，忽略了听者前反应这一非语言线索对下一话语情绪与韵律规划的潜在价值。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段以约 1 秒的听者前反应面部序列为输入，结合对话上下文预测下一话语的情绪与韵律（含 VAD 维度）风格；第二阶段将预测的响应风格接入 Grad-TTS 骨干网络，完成端到端的语音实现。在严格的双人 MELD 协议下与 Text-only 基线对比，并进行视觉变体消融与端到端差分实验。另开展 20 名语音研究者的语境恰当性主观评测。

Result: 10 个随机种子下，Temporal 条件在平均宏 F1 与 VAD 一致性上均高于 Text-only，而准确率基本持平。消融显示：在各类视觉变体中时间建模表现最佳，且显式的早—晚差分并无必要；正确听者反应平均优于循环错配反应。主观评测中 76% 判断偏好 Temporal，9% 偏好 Text-only，15% 无偏好。风格预测已成功接入 Grad-TTS 实现端到端语音生成。

Conclusion: 听者前反应动态可作为会话响应规划的补充线索，在情绪与韵律预测及语音实现中带来增益。代码已开源。

Abstract: Conversational speech depends on dialogue context and the listener's immediately preceding behavior. We propose ReACT-TTS, a two-stage framework that uses a one-second pre-response listener facial sequence to plan the next utterance's emotion and prosody before speech realization. On a strict dyadic MELD protocol, Temporal conditioning yields higher mean macro-F1 and VAD concordance than Text-only across ten seeds, while accuracy remains essentially unchanged. Ablations show that temporal modeling performs best among the visual variants and that an explicit early-to-late difference is unnecessary; correct listener reactions also outperform cyclic mismatches on average. In a contextual-appropriateness study with 20 speech researchers, 76% of judgments prefer Temporal, 9% Text-only, and 15% report no preference. We further connect the predicted response style to a Grad-TTS backbone for end-to-end speech realization. Overall, the results support pre-response listener dynamics as complementary cues for conversational response planning. The source code is available at https://github.com/CYJ1/ReACT-TTS_public.

</details>


### [39] [World Modeling in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21748)
*Pierre Beckmann,Matthieu Queloz,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 以TaxiGPT为对象，通过机制分析与因果干预证明：其行为失败并非缺少世界模型，而是内部地图中叠加的交叉口特征相互干扰导致定位错误；可用机制指标比较模型并刻画世界建模能力的训练阶段涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为层面的失败容易被解读为模型缺乏世界模型，但行为失败可能与内部表征是否忠实无关。本文旨在厘清“表现失败”与“缺乏世界模型”之间的区别，并把研究问题从“模型是否有世界模型”转向对其世界建模机制的分解与因果刻画。

Method: 在TaxiGPT（在曼哈顿随机游走轨迹上训练的transformer）上开展机制分析与因果干预：识别交叉口与街道表征、位置追踪表征以及目标罗盘表征；定位失败来源为叠加（superposed）交叉口特征之间的干扰，并评估“affordance packing”（将具有相同合法动作的交叉口表征分组）的作用；提出一组机制指标用于模型间比较及训练各阶段的能力涌现分析。

Result: 模型确实表征了交叉口与街道、追踪自身位置，并使用目标罗盘进行导航；其失败可追溯到叠加交叉口特征间的干扰，从而破坏内部地图中的定位。affordance packing能限制此类错误的后果。所提出的机制指标可区分不同模型，并显示世界建模各项能力在训练的不同阶段依次涌现。

Conclusion: 应超越“是否具备世界模型”的二元判断，转向机制层面研究世界建模：即模型表征环境并以其引导行为的多个相互作用能力。行为失败本身不足以否定忠实内部表征的存在。

Abstract: Behavioral failures can make a transformer appear to lack a world model even when it has learned faithful representations of its environment. We demonstrate this in TaxiGPT, a transformer trained on random walks through Manhattan whose failures have been interpreted as evidence of an incoherent internal map. Through mechanistic analysis and causal interventions, we show that the model represents intersections and streets, tracks its position, and uses a goal compass to navigate. We trace its failures to interference between superposed intersection features, which disrupts localization within the internal map. Affordance packing, which groups representations of intersections with the same legal moves, helps limit the consequences of these errors. Finally, we propose mechanistic indicators that we use to compare models and show that world-modeling capacities emerge at different stages of training. Our findings motivate a shift from asking whether a model has a world model to mechanistically studying its world modeling: the interacting capacities through which it represents its environment and uses those representations to guide behavior.

</details>


### [40] [ECG Mirage: Revealing and Mitigating the Underutilisation of ECGs in Vision-Language Models for Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21755)
*Jinning Liang,Mingcheng Zhu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示多模态视觉语言模型在急诊决策中的"ECG Mirage"失效模式，并提出视觉提示微调作为缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊决策依赖病史、生命体征、检验和心电图(ECG)等异构信息，VLM可联合处理这些模态，但强预测性能并不代表模型真正利用了正确的患者ECG信息。作者将这一失效模式称为"ECG Mirage"，并区分出ECG忽视与ECG混淆两种形式，旨在评估并解决该问题。

Method: 在MDS-ED数据集上，固定临床文本与预测目标，对比匹配ECG、结果不一致的错配ECG以及无图像输入三种条件下的预测；评估四个VLM在ICU入住和临床恶化预测上的表现。随后在冻结VLM主干的前提下，用监督学习结合条件直接偏好优化(conditional DPO)训练四个受限视觉提示。

Result: 四个VLM在两种任务上匹配ECG均无一致优势；经视觉提示微调后，模型在ICU入住预测上达到70.6%平衡准确率，恶化预测达67.5%，并将匹配与错配的性能差距分别扩大至约16.5和5.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究识别了多模态临床预测中的ECG Mirage现象，并证明视觉提示微调是一种高效、无需更新主干参数的缓解策略，可促使模型真正依赖患者特异性ECG信息。

Abstract: Emergency department (ED) decision-making relies on heterogeneous clinical information, including patient history, vital signs, laboratory results, and electrocardiograms (ECGs). Vision--language models (VLMs) can jointly process these modalities, but strong predictive performance does not necessarily imply meaningful use of the correct patient's ECG. We term this failure mode ECG Mirage: apparent multimodal capability without useful dependence on patient-specific ECG information. We distinguish two forms: ECG neglect, where ECGs provide little predictive benefit, and ECG confusion, where matched ECGs outperform no-image inputs but not mismatched ECGs. To evaluate these behaviours, we compare predictions obtained with matched ECGs, outcome-discordant mismatched ECGs, and no-image inputs while holding the clinical text and prediction targets fixed. Across four VLMs on MDS-ED, matched ECGs provide no consistent advantage for either ICU admission or clinical deterioration prediction. We then train four restricted visual prompts using supervised learning followed by conditional direct preference optimisation, while keeping the VLM backbone frozen. The resulting models achieve balanced accuracies of 70.6% for ICU admission and 67.5% for deterioration and increase the matched-versus-mismatched performance gap to approximately 16.5 and 5.5 percentage points, respectively. Overall, our study identifies ECG Mirage in multimodal clinical prediction and introduces visual prompt tuning as an efficient mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [41] [LLM-Generated Feature Pools for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21801)
*Youssef Attia El Hili,Malik Tiomoko,Corinne Ancourt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在严格选择协议下，一个仅用滑窗统计特征与MAD稳健评分的简单流水线，在TSB-AD-U单变量时间序列异常检测上达到0.529 per-series VUS-PR，超过最佳神经与统计基线；消融表明候选特征池决定性能上限，而搜索策略仅为二阶因素。用多模态LLM按域生成候选池并与手工池取并集，可将性能提升至0.588，追平排行榜最佳条目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望探究：在无/弱监督的设定下，简单的统计方法在单变量时间序列异常检测上究竟能走多远，以及性能瓶颈主要来自特征候选池本身还是来自后续的特征选择与聚合搜索。

Method: 方法为一条轻量流水线：在滑动窗口上提取一小组统计量，用transductive鲁棒（MAD）模型对每个窗口打分，并在留出的调参划分上按域选择特征子集。随后通过消融对比三种选择策略、事后oracle、聚合网格变化以及候选池变化对分数的影响，并进一步用多模态LLM结合该域的in-context示例窗口生成候选池，最后在手工池与生成池的并集上做选择。

Result: 在TSB-AD-U上达到0.529 per-series VUS-PR，高于公开排行榜最佳神经（0.45）与统计（0.44）条目，距最强预训练基础模型仅差0.06且后者使用更多监督。消融显示：三种选择策略与事后oracle之间分数波动0.031，聚合网格波动0.096，而候选池变化带来0.226的波动。生成的候选池在匹配选择下与手工池相当，且二者覆盖不同域；在十二个生成器-种子对上，并集选择均优于生成池，流水线提升至0.588，追平排行榜最佳条目。

Conclusion: 候选池设定性能上限，对候选池的搜索仅为二阶因素。用LLM按域生成候选池可作为一种有效且互补的特征来源，其与手工池的并集能进一步突破上限并达到排行榜最佳水平，说明简单统计流水线在合适候选池支持下仍具竞争力。

Abstract: We study how far a simple statistical pipeline can go on univariate time series anomaly detection under a strict selection protocol. The method extracts a small pool of statistics over sliding windows, scores each window with a transductive robust (MAD) model, and selects a feature subset per domain on a held-out tuning split. On TSB-AD-U it reaches $0.529$ per-series VUS-PR, above the best neural ($0.45$) and statistical ($0.44$) entries on the public leaderboard and within $0.06$ of the strongest pretrained foundation model, several of which use more supervision than ours. Ablations locate the cause: across three selection strategies and a hindsight oracle the score moves by $0.031$, and across the aggregation grid by $0.096$, while changing the candidate pool moves it by $0.226$. The candidate pool sets the ceiling; the search over it is second-order. We therefore generate a pool per domain by prompting a multimodal LLM with in-context example windows from that domain. The generated pools match the hand-crafted one under matched selection, and the two cover different domains: selecting over their union improves on the generated pool in all twelve generator-seed pairs and lifts the pipeline to $0.588$, matching the performance of the best entry on the leaderboard.

</details>


### [42] [MIST: Multimodal Survival Prediction with Genomic-Guided Histology Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21811)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Richard R. Chen,Jana Lipkova,Benedikt Wiestler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIST：以基因组特征为查询、病理基础模型token为上下文的注意力融合，实现紧凑且抗分布偏移的多模态生存预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像（WSI）与基因组可提供互补预后信息，但多模态融合面临外部队列分布偏移与计算复杂度高的双重挑战；现有"晚期融合"仅在最终阶段拼接独立编码的模态，未充分利用跨模态交互。

Method: 提出MIST（genomic-guided histology attention）：将基因组特征表示为token，作为query去查询由病理基础模型导出的紧凑组织学上下文token，在生存预测前完成信息整合；训练目标结合离散时间生存预测、基因组特征掩码（genomic feature masking）、WSI dropout，以及配对WSI-基因组对比对齐损失。

Result: 在结肠、肾脏、肺和胶质母细胞瘤四个外部队列评估中，MIST在主要对比中相较标准融合基线取得更高的外部C-index。

Conclusion: 基因组引导的组织学注意力是一种紧凑且有效的多模态肿瘤结局预测策略；代码已开源（https://github.com/samiyavuuz/MIST）。

Abstract: Multimodal survival models can combine complementary prognostic information from whole-slide images and genomic profiles, but effective fusion remains challenging amid external cohort shift and computational complexity. To address these challenges, we propose MIST, multimodal survival prediction with genomic-guided histology attention. MIST represents genomic features as tokens and allows them to query compact foundation-model-derived histology context tokens before survival prediction. This design enriches molecular information with histology context rather than merging separately encoded modalities only at the final stage. Training combines discrete-time survival prediction with genomic feature masking, WSI dropout, and paired WSI-genomics contrastive alignment. Across four external evaluations in colon, renal, lung, and glioblastoma cohorts, MIST improves external C-index over standard fusion baselines in the primary comparisons. These results support genomic-guided histology attention as a compact and effective strategy for multimodal oncology outcome prediction. Our code is available at https://github.com/samiyavuuz/MIST .

</details>


### [43] [AutoRecLab: Describe the Experiment, Get the Code!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21863)
*Moritz Baumgart,Philipp Meister,Justus Krell,Michael Schmidt,Bela Gipp,Joeran Beel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoRecLab是一个基于Python的自主推荐系统实验平台,可将自然语言研究想法自动转化为可执行实验代码,在演示中自主完成显式到隐式反馈转换研究,6种算法3个数据集共9次运行中8次成功,单次成本约1美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统研究中实证评估至关重要,但从实验设计到可执行代码的转化仍是手工且易出错的过程,作者希望自动化这一流程。

Method: 给定自然语言研究想法后,AutoRecLab先提取显式实验需求,构建并验证原型,再迭代扩展为完整实验。其工作流结合了用于文档检索的检索增强生成(RAG)、静态类型校验以及执行引导的树搜索(execution-steered tree search)。

Result: 在演示中,AutoRecLab自主实现了一项显式到隐式反馈转换研究。在涵盖6种算法、3个数据集的基线对比中,9次运行有8次成功,使用GPT-5.4-mini时平均每次运行成本约1美元。

Conclusion: AutoRecLab展示了以自然语言驱动、自动化完成推荐系统实验的可行性,在成功率与成本上具备实用潜力,可减少手工编码负担与错误。

Abstract: Empirical evaluation is central to recommender-systems (RecSys) research, but turning experimental designs into executable code remains a manual and error-prone task. We present AutoRecLab, a Python-based autonomous RecSys lab that automates RecSys experiments from natural-language prompts. Given a research idea, AutoRecLab derives explicit experiment requirements, builds and validates a prototype, and iteratively expands it into the requested full experiment. The workflow combines retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for documentation lookup, static type verification, and execution-steered tree search. In our demonstration, AutoRecLab autonomously implements an explicit-to-implicit feedback conversion study. In a baseline comparison across six algorithms and three datasets, 8 of 9 runs succeed at an average cost of approx- imately $1 per run with GPT-5.4-mini.

</details>


### [44] [What Should We Ask Next? Retrieval-Aware Question Learning under Partial Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21924)
*Lyucheng Qian,John Yuehan Zhang,Pingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出RAVEL，一个面向交互式行人重识别的检索感知在线强化学习框架，通过问答-检索全循环的排序反馈优化提问策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分证据下的交互式检索本质上是序贯信息获取问题：智能体需判断哪个问题能为下一轮检索更新提供最有用的证据。然而现有系统依赖模仿离线候选问答对的排序来训练该决策，但问题价值实际取决于其引发的回答及对检索的下游影响。作者指出候选区分度与主观感知有用性对该目标仅能提供弱监督。

Method: RAVEL从监督式问题生成初始化，直接观测当前Top-4候选，并利用完整问答-检索循环中的排序反馈，通过在线强化学习优化提问策略。

Result: 在Interactive-PEDES数据集上，RAVEL在五轮交互中实现了逐步增强的检索性能。进一步分析表明，RAVEL将提问预算重新分配给局部化开放式属性，这类问题提供更有用的检索证据，并在初始困难的查询上取得最大提升。

Conclusion: 以检索排序反馈为奖励的在线强化学习能有效提升交互式检索中的提问决策质量，且开放式局部属性问答是获取高价值证据的关键方向。

Abstract: Interactive retrieval under partial evidence is a sequential information-acquisition problem: an agent must decide which question will create the most useful evidence for the next retrieval update. Existing systems train this decision by imitating an offline ordering of candidate QA pairs, although question value is determined by the response it elicits and its downstream effect on retrieval. We establish that candidate discriminativeness and perceived usefulness provide weak supervision for this objective, then introduce RAVEL, a retrieval-aware online reinforcement learning framework for interactive person re-identification. RAVEL initializes from supervised question generation, observes the current Top-4 candidates directly, and optimizes the question policy with rank feedback from the full question-answer-retrieval loop. Experiments on Interactive-PEDES show that RAVEL delivers progressively stronger retrieval performance across five interaction rounds. Further analysis shows that RAVEL reallocates the questioning budget toward localized open-ended attributes, which provide more useful retrieval evidence and yield the largest gains on initially difficult queries.

</details>


### [45] [AutoViewMem: Self-Configuring Orthogonal Views for Conversational Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21940)
*Zijie Cao,Xijun Qu,Zhicheng Gu,Xiaoshu Chen,Duanyang Yuan,Yanning Hou,Sihang Zhou,Jianxing Gong,Jian Huang,Yang Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoViewMem是一个数据驱动框架，在写入阶段将长期对话记忆组织为自配置、低重叠的语义视图，从而在标准top-K检索下实现更聚焦的证据召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统依赖固定粒度或静态schema，难以处理偏好、事件、约束、时序更新等异构信息混于单一表示中的情况，导致语义干扰使top-K检索对噪声敏感、相关证据排名靠后。

Method: AutoViewMem从交互轨迹中发现候选视图，选择紧凑的互补视图集合，并用这些视图指导写入时的结构化抽取，生成带来源依据（provenance-grounded）的记忆；将语义解耦从检索时前移到写入时，使标准top-K相似度检索即可召回聚焦证据，无需显式路由或迭代检索；并进一步采用离线整合提升记忆紧凑性与一致性。

Result: 在LoCoMo和PersonaMem基准上，基于Qwen3-8B与Qwen3-14B骨干，AutoViewMem在长程问答与个性化任务上优于强记忆基线，同时保持简单的推理流程。

Conclusion: 以表示为中心、在写入时进行语义解耦的设计可有效缓解异构记忆的语义干扰，在保持简单推理管线的同时提升长程交互的一致性与个性化能力。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for large language model (LLM) agents to maintain consistency and personalization over extended interactions. Existing memory systems typically rely on fixed granularities or static schemas, but these designs struggle when heterogeneous information, such as preferences, events, constraints, and temporal updates, is embedded in a single mixed representation. The resulting semantic interference makes top-K retrieval sensitive to noise and often leaves relevant evidence poorly ranked. We present AutoViewMem, a data-driven framework that organizes long-term conversational memory into self-configuring, low-overlap semantic views before indexing. AutoViewMem discovers candidate views from interaction traces, selects a compact complementary view set, and uses these views to guide write-time structured extraction of provenance-grounded memories. This representation-first design moves semantic disentanglement from retrieval time to write time, allowing standard top-K similarity search to retrieve focused evidence without explicit routing or iterative retrieval. We further apply offline consolidation to improve memory compactness and consistency. Experiments on the LoCoMo and PersonaMem benchmarks, under both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B backbones, show that AutoViewMem improves long-horizon question answering and personalization over strong memory baselines while preserving a simple inference pipeline.

</details>


### [46] [Learning Cardiac Features: ECG Biometrics Across Time and~Exercise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21962)
*Luca Thiebaud,Paul Chauchat,Mustapha Ouladsine,Stéphane Delliaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对心电生物识别在运动应激与跨会话变化下缺乏评估的问题，训练了晚期多导联融合的Siamese ResNet，并在运动-时间感知协议及公开基准上评估。结果表明：会话内静息-峰值EER为1.7%，CYBHi数据集上达到3.9%的当前最优水平，支持存在对生理与时间漂移具有鲁棒性的内在心脏特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电信号包含个体特异模式，可用于身份识别，并在保护敏感心脏数据及自监督学习预文本任务方面具有潜力。但现有研究多局限于单会话静息状态，对时间与生理变化下的鲁棒性缺乏验证。

Method: 采用Siamese ResNet架构，结合晚期多导联融合策略；在来自心肺运动试验的大规模心电数据集上训练，并使用运动-时间感知评估协议，同时在公开基准上进行评测。

Result: 在生理与时间变化联合条件下，会话内静息-峰值EER为1.7%；在CYBHi数据集上达到3.9%的最新最优性能。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统评估了生理与时间变化联合条件下的心电生物识别，结果支持存在一种对生理与时间漂移具有鲁棒性的内在心脏特征。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) carry subject-specific patterns enabling reliable individual discrimination, forming the basis of ECG biometrics. Beyond authentication, this paradigm holds significant potential to secure sensitive cardiac data and to serve as a pretext task in self-supervised learning. Yet, most studies remain confined to singlesession, resting data, leaving robustness to temporal and physiological variations largely untested. We address this gap by evaluating ECG biometrics under realistic conditions involving exercise-induced stress and cross-session variability. A Siamese ResNet with late multi-lead fusion strategy is trained on a large ECG dataset extracted from cardiopulmonary exercise tests and evaluated with a exercise-and time-aware protocol, as well as on public benchmarks. This first extensive assessment of ECG biometrics under combined physiological and temporal variability achieves an intra-session rest-to-peak EER of 1.7% and stateof-the-art 3.9% on the CYBHi dataset. Findings support the presence of an intrinsic cardiac signature resilient to physiological and temporal drift.

</details>


### [47] [A Lie Detector Test for Language Models: Reading Knowledge a Model Won't Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21996)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出无参考探针方法 PIR，通过读取模型内部状态识别其"认出"的正确答案，从而区分模型是"不愿回答"还是"不知道"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型可能隐藏已知知识：在能力评估中故意放水（sandbagging），或输出与内部认知相悖的答案。仅凭输出无法判断模型是隐藏答案还是本就不具备该知识，这给安全审计和遗忘验证带来困难。

Method: 借鉴法医学的隐藏信息测试（CIT）——向嫌疑人混入真实细节与干扰项并测量其对识别项的更强反应。PIR 在模型内部做同样的事：给定问题及其候选答案，从模型内部状态读取它把哪个候选识别为正确。该方法无需诚实参考模型，也无需带标注的真值语料（reference-free）。

Result: 在来自 5 个家族（Gemma、Qwen、Llama、Mistral、Phi）的 8 个模型上，PIR 以 0.70–0.87 的平衡准确率恢复被识别的答案，远高于未知题目基线（0.28–0.40）和随机水平（0.25）。在各类隐藏形式下（提示欺骗、训练出的 sandbagging、外部密码锁定与电路破坏检查点）识别率保持 0.85–0.93。模型隐藏已知答案时识别仍高；遗忘移除知识后识别降至从未知晓题目的水平。

Conclusion: PIR 能区分"不愿回答"与"无法回答"的模型，支持 sandbagging 审计与遗忘验证。该信号具因果性，提供超越黑盒行为线索的信息，并从多选扩展到自由生成。

Abstract: Large language models can hold knowledge they do not report. A model may sandbag on a capability evaluation, or answer against what it internally knows, and its outputs alone cannot tell whether it is hiding an answer or simply does not have one. We borrow the Concealed Information Test, a forensic method that identifies guilty knowledge by presenting a suspect with the true detail among plausible decoys and measuring a stronger response to the item they recognize. Our method, Probe of Internal Recognition (PIR), does the same inside a model. It presents a question with its candidate answers and reads, from the model's internal states, which candidate the model recognizes as correct. PIR is reference-free, needing no honest reference model and no labeled truth corpus. Across eight models from five families (Gemma, Qwen, Llama, Mistral, and Phi), PIR recovers the recognized answer at 0.70 to 0.87 balanced accuracy, well above the 0.28 to 0.40 unknown-item baseline and the 0.25 chance rate. It stays readable across every form of concealment we test, from prompted deception and trained sandbagging to external password-locked and circuit-broken checkpoints, with recognition between 0.85 and 0.93. When the model hides a known answer, recognition stays high. When unlearning removes the knowledge, recognition drops to the level of a question the model never knew. PIR therefore separates a model that will not answer from one that cannot, which supports sandbagging audits and unlearning verification. The signal is causal, adds information beyond black-box behavioral cues, and extends from multiple-choice questions to free-form generation.

</details>


### [48] [CodeMidas: Scaling Agentic Coding RL Environments from Code Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22068)
*Bowen Ye,Lei Li,Shicheng Li,Zihao Yue,Linghao Zhang,Hanglong Lv,Yuanxin Liu,Wenhan Ma,Hao Tian,Rang Li,Jinhao Dong,Yikai Zhao,Xiangwei Deng,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Qi Liu,Lingpeng Kong,Tong Yang,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CodeMidas，一种仅以源码为任务特定输入的智能体式流水线，将现有代码库中已实现的功能转化为可执行的 RL 环境，构建了覆盖 3,185 个代码库、23 种语言、15 个领域的 5,545 个训练任务，训练后模型在五类基准上全面提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用强化学习训练编码智能体需要大量带可靠验证器的多样化任务。开源代码库是丰富来源，但现有方法多依赖 issue、commit 等开发过程产物来挖掘任务，导致可提取任务的范围受限，难以规模化扩展 RL 环境。

Method: CodeMidas 将智能体算力分配到环境构建的每个阶段：智能体探索已实现功能以形成行为规格说明；基于原始代码的实际执行构造测试；再通过执行检查与多次解 rollout 对候选任务进行验证与过滤。整个过程仅需源码作为任务特定输入。

Result: 产出数据集含 5,545 个训练任务，来自 3,185 个开源代码库，覆盖 23 种编程语言与 15 个技术领域。用 GRPO 训练 MiMo-V2.5 后，在五个基准上均提升：DeepSWE（issue 修复）+11.7%，ProgramBench（整程序构建）+17%，Terminal-Bench v2.1（终端任务）+8.5%。消融表明高质量训练任务数量增加可提升性能；轨迹分析显示 RL 训练后的智能体代码库探索更充分、自验证更多样。

Conclusion: 源码可作为构建 RL 环境的可扩展基础，能够跨多样软件任务提升编码智能体能力。

Abstract: Training capable coding agents via reinforcement learning (RL) requires diverse tasks with reliable verifiers. Open-source codebases offer a rich source of such tasks, while existing methods typically rely on development artifacts such as issues and commits, limiting the range of tasks that can be extracted. To better scale RL environments, we present CodeMidas, an agentic pipeline that turns implemented functionality in existing codebases into executable RL environments using source code as its only task-specific input. CodeMidas allocates agentic compute to every stage of environment construction: agents explore implemented functionality to formulate behavioral specifications, construct tests grounded in execution of the original code, and validate and filter candidate tasks through execution checks and repeated solution rollouts. The resulting dataset has 5,545 training tasks from 3,185 open-source codebases spanning 23 programming languages and 15 technical domains. Training MiMo-V2.5 on these tasks with GRPO improves performance on all five diverse benchmarks, covering issue repair (DeepSWE + 11.7%), whole-program construction (ProgramBench +17%), and terminal work (Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%). Ablations show that increasing the number of high-quality training tasks improves performance. Trajectory analysis shows the RL-trained agent demonstrates better behaviors like increasing codebase exploration and more diverse self-verification. These results establish source code as a scalable foundation for constructing RL environments that improve coding agents across diverse software tasks.

</details>


### [49] [Designer-RSI: Evolving Procedural Memory from User Traffic for Agentic Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22086)
*Hongyang Du,Lan Yan,Christian Flores,Asim Kadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种持续适应框架，让冻结的前沿模型通过230余个工具操作专业设计软件，并借助外部自然语言技能的程序性记忆从经验中积累与精炼可复用设计流程。在1,406份真实用户简报上经五轮迭代且无权重更新、无人工标注，技能库由76增至139，GenEval2执行成功率从72.7%升至99.3%，并在多个设计基准上显著优于无技能智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业平面设计是长时程智能体任务，产出为结构化、可编辑的工件，且结果缺乏可靠的程序化评判标准（oracle），因此无法依赖传统监督信号进行持续优化，需要一种在噪声大、不可验证反馈下仍能持续自适应的机制。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：1）冻结的前沿模型通过超过230个工具操作专业设计软件；2）外部程序性记忆，以自然语言技能形式存储可复用设计流程，通过“拓宽”（为反复出现且未被覆盖的子任务新增技能）与“深化”（基于自身成功与失败执行修订已有技能）两种机制演化；3）匹配重放门控（matched replay gate），仅接纳能修复失败且不使已观测成功退化的修改。整个过程无权重更新、无人工标签。

Result: 在1,406份真实用户简报、1,869条自动评分轨迹上进行五轮迭代：技能库从76条（源自文档）扩展到139条；Claude-Sonnet-4上GenEval2执行成功率由72.7%提升至99.3%，生成质量提升11.99个百分点；在四个专业设计基准上对无技能智能体取得61.8%（Sonnet-4）与67.6%（Opus-4.6）的胜率。消融方面，在用户流量基准的200份留出简报上，仅拓宽或仅深化分别达到49.4%/48.6%胜率，二者结合达58.5%（p=0.025）。

Conclusion: 程序性记忆为智能体在噪声大、不可验证反馈下实现持续适应提供了一条可行路径；拓宽与深化两种机制需协同使用才能取得最佳效果。

Abstract: Professional graphic design is a long-horizon agentic task in which structured, editable artifacts emerge from many interdependent actions, yet outcomes admit no reliable programmatic oracle. We introduce a continual adaptation framework in which a frozen frontier model operates professional design software through more than 230 tools, while an external procedural memory of natural-language skills accumulates and refines reusable design procedures from experience. The memory widens by acquiring procedures for recurring uncovered subtasks and deepens by revising existing procedures against their own successful and failed executions, while a matched replay gate admits only changes that repair failures without regressing observed successes. Five rounds over 1,406 real user briefs and 1,869 automatically graded trajectories, with no weight updates and no human labels, grow the bank from 76 documentation-derived skills to 139 and raise GenEval2 execution success on Claude-Sonnet-4 from 72.7% to 99.3% (+11.99 points in generation quality), with 61.8% and 67.6% win rates against the no-skill agent across four specialized design benchmarks on Claude-Sonnet-4 and Claude-Opus-4.6. We further show the two mechanisms are effective in combination: on 200 held-out briefs from user-traffic benchmark, widening or deepening alone reaches a 49.4% / 48.6% win rate over the no-skill agent, while their combination reaches 58.5% (p = 0.025). Procedural memory offers a practical route to continual adaptation of agents under noisy, unverifiable feedback.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [50] [Training-Adaptive Convolutional Sparse Coding via Information Bottleneck for Robust Visual Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19122)
*Meng'en Qin,Yinchen Liu,Mingxuan Cui,Youlu Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种训练自适应的卷积稀疏编码(CSC)框架，将稀疏系数作为可微变量与网络参数联合学习，并从信息瓶颈视角解释其控制信息保留与压缩的权衡，同时引入无标签后训练策略以提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉信号需要紧凑且充分的表示以支撑稳健的下游预测。卷积稀疏编码能显式抑制冗余成分并保留信号内容，但其稀疏系数通常固定且需人工选择，限制了表示的适应性和鲁棒性。

Method: 用FISTA展开CSC优化过程，将稀疏系数设为可微变量并与网络参数联合学习；从信息瓶颈角度，稀疏项促进紧凑表示，重建项与任务损失共同保留任务相关信号；进一步提出无标签后训练策略，在主网络参数固定下针对受损输入调整压缩强度。

Result: 在CIFAR和ImageNet上的实验表明，该方法在干净数据识别上具有竞争力，并在不同输入扰动下大幅提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将稀疏系数自适应可学习化并引入无标签后训练，能够在保持竞争性识别性能的同时显著增强视觉表示的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Visual signals require compact yet sufficient representations for robust downstream prediction. Convolutional sparse coding (CSC) provides an explicit mechanism for suppressing redundant components while preserving signal content, but its sparsity coefficient is typically fixed and manually selected. We propose a training-adaptive convolutional sparse coding framework for robust visual signal representation. Specifically, we unfold the CSC optimization with the Fast Iterative Shrinkage-Thresholding Algorithm (FISTA) and treat the sparsity coefficient as a differentiable variable jointly learned with the network parameters. From the information bottleneck perspective, this coefficient controls the trade-off between information retention and compression: the sparsity term promotes compact representations, while the reconstruction term together with task loss preserves task-relevant signal content. We further introduce a label-free post-training strategy that adjusts the compression strength for corrupted inputs with the main network parameters fixed. Experiments on CIFAR and ImageNet demonstrate competitive clean-data recognition and greatly improved robustness under different input perturbations.

</details>


### [51] [TAPe+ML: A Compact Structured Representation for Multi-Task Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20869)
*Sergey Kurinov,Alexey Upatov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TAPe+ML v3紧凑视觉系统，基于TAPe结构化表征（识别前编码感知元素关系），参数不足10万，在检测、分割、分类多任务上取得有竞争力的性能，并验证了视频场景检测与工业分布迁移场景下的紧凑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算机视觉直接在像素张量上运算，建模负担集中于网络参数，导致数据、内存与算力需求高。作者希望把部分建模负担从网络参数转移到结构化输入表征上，从而实现紧凑的多任务视觉系统。

Method: 构建TAPe+ML v3系统，采用共享的TAPe结构化表征替代直接像素张量操作，配合模块化识别架构：背景与轮廓处理、局部目标定位、基于原型的分类，以及协调各专用子模型的协调器。参数总量少于100,000。在COCO检测/分割、Imagenette与ImageNet-Real分类、视频场景检测及工业pilots分布迁移上评估。

Result: COCO检测：84.7 mAP50、65.3 mAP50-95；COCO实例分割：80.7 mask mAP50、58.4 mask mAP50-95；分类：Imagenette同训练对比下92%验证精度（对比原始像素基线），ImageNet-Real 89.9% Top-1。参数少于10万。

Conclusion: 将部分建模负担从网络参数转移到结构化输入表征，可支撑紧凑的多任务视觉系统，并降低数据、内存与算力需求。

Abstract: We present TAPe+ML v3, a compact computer vision system based on TAPe (Theory of Active Perception), a structured representation that encodes relations among perceptual elements before recognition. Instead of operating directly on pixel tensors, the system uses a shared TAPe representation and a modular recognition architecture for image classification, object detection, and instance segmentation.
  TAPe+ML v3 combines background and contour processing, local object localization, prototype-based classification, and a coordinator for specialized submodels. Across the reported experiments, it uses fewer than 100,000 parameters. On COCO object detection, it obtains 84.7 mAP50 and 65.3 mAP50-95. On COCO instance segmentation, it obtains 80.7 mask mAP50 and 58.4 mask mAP50-95. In classification experiments, it reaches 92 percent validation accuracy on Imagenette under an identical-training comparison with a raw-pixel baseline, and 89.9 percent Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-Real. We also evaluate compactness in video scene detection and adaptation under distribution shift in an industrial pilot. The results suggest that shifting part of the modeling burden from network parameters to a structured input representation can support compact multi-task vision systems with reduced data, memory, and compute requirements.

</details>


### [52] [MemeTAG: Keyword-Driven Meme Classification through Tag Embedding Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20962)
*Akshit Sharma,Prashant W. Patil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MemeTAG双目标框架，通过关键词感知与标签推理网络实现有害表情包分类，在三个数据集上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有害网络表情包泛滥构成社会威胁，但其内容具有细微的多模态特性，自动化分类仍是严峻的算法挑战。

Method: 核心为两部分语义引导机制：1）利用预训练视觉-语言模型生成捕捉高层语义的描述性关键词；2）提出聚合标签推理网络（ATIN），基于注意力将关键词蒸馏为单一丰富语义嵌入，作为新颖辅助重构损失的目标，促使模型学习深度对齐的视觉与文本特征；并配合高效的三阶段训练策略。

Result: 在HarMeme、Hateful Memes Challenge（HMC）和PrideMM数据集上刷新SOTA，决定性地超越现有最优方法。

Conclusion: MemeTAG以关键词感知的双目标框架有效提升多模态有害表情包分类性能，验证了语义引导与辅助重构损失的有效性。

Abstract: The proliferation of harmful internet memes poses a significant societal threat, yet their automated classification remains a formidable algorithmic challenge due to the nuanced, multimodal nature of their content. To address this, we introduce MemeTAG, a novel dual-objective framework that pioneers a keyword-aware approach to meme classification. Our core innovation is a two-part semantic guidance mechanism: first, we leverage a pretrained Vision-Language Model to generate a set of descriptive keywords, that capture the high-level semantics. Second, we introduce the Aggregated Tag Inference Network (ATIN), an attention-based module that distills these keywords into a single, rich semantic embedding. This embedding serves as a target for a novel auxiliary reconstruction loss, which compels the model to learn deeply aligned visual and textual features. This approach, combined with an efficient three-stage training strategy, establishes a new state-of-the-art on the HarMeme, Hateful Memes Challenge (HMC), and PrideMM datasets, decisively outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [53] [Image-Derived PM10 Estimation in Cattle Feedlot Using Machine Learning: Addressing Concentration Ranges Beyond Existing Digital Imaging Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20975)
*Sirapoom Peanusaha,Greg B. Ferguson,K. Jack Bush,Peiyang Li,Brent W. Auvermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究面向牛饲养场粉尘监测需求，利用对比面板特征与机器学习，从灰度图像估计PM10浓度（小时均值250–1000 µg/m³，瞬时可达5000–20000 µg/m³），XGBoost取得最优性能（R²=0.792，中位绝对误差103 µg/m³）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛饲养场行业亟需低成本粉尘监测手段；基于摄像头的PM估计虽在城市空气质量研究中日益增多，但尚未在集约化畜牧作业特有的高浓度区间得到验证。

Method: 在商业牛饲养场采集傍晚粉尘高峰期的灰度图像，提取对比面板特征（面板对比度、黑/白面板像素值、整体亮度），并引入前序图像的历史值和太阳天顶角作为预测变量，采用多种机器学习模型进行PM10估计并比较性能，开展特征重要性分析。

Result: XGBoost预测性能最佳，R²=0.792，中位绝对误差103 µg/m³。特征重要性显示：距相机最远的面板贡献最大；黑面板像素值比白面板像素值对PM10变化更敏感。日落过渡期（与饲养场傍晚粉尘峰值起始重合）的预测精度仍需改进。

Conclusion: 基于图像的PM10估计在远超以往城市研究报道的浓度范围内具有可行性，并为饲养场环境的未来部署提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Affordable dust monitoring remains a pressing need for the cattle feedlot industry, yet camera-based PM estimation, despite its growing body of research in urban air quality settings, has not been evaluated under the extended concentration ranges characteristic of intensive livestock operations. This study developed an image-based approach using contrast panel features and machine learning to estimate PM10 concentrations in a commercial cattle feedlot, where hourly average PM10 ranged from 250 to 1,000 ug/m^-3 and instantaneous concentrations reached 5,000 to 20,000 ug/m^-3. Grayscale images were captured during the evening dust peak period, and features including panel contrast, black and white panel pixel values, and overall image brightness were extracted. The model also incorporated recent past values from preceding images and solar zenith angle as predictors. Among the candidate models evaluated, XGBoost achieved the highest predictive performance, with an R^2 of 0.792 and a median absolute error of 103 ug/m^-3. Feature importance analysis revealed that (a) panels positioned farthest from the camera contributed most strongly to predictions and (b) that black panel pixel values were more sensitive than white panel values to changes in PM10 concentration. Prediction accuracy during the sunset transition, which coincides with the onset of the feedlot evening dust peak, remains an area for further refinement. These findings demonstrate the feasibility of image-based PM10 estimation across PM concentration ranges substantially exceeding those reported in prior urban studies and provide practical guidelines for future deployment in feedlot environments.

</details>


### [54] [Fragment-Aware Vision Transformers for Fresco-Fragment Style Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21012)
*Sara Miketek,Biagio Barchielli,Nadeem Iqbal Kajla,Sinem Aslan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出渐进式Transformer框架用于壁画残片艺术风格分类，含前景引导掩码、修复几何正则化与分布级对比目标，并以简单logit集成融合分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有艺术风格分类多基于完整艺术品，可依赖全局构图与图像志结构；考古场景中作品常仅存残片，视觉证据不完整、不规则且缺乏上下文，因此需研究碎片化的风格识别。

Method: 以ViT-B/16为基线，逐步引入：(1) 前景引导掩码以抑制纯背景token；(2) 基于修复的几何正则化，使不规则残片支撑区域对齐ViT的patch网格；(3) 监督对比目标，在预测分布上通过KL相似度计算，可稳定提升各分支；最后用刻意简单的可学习logit集成融合各分支，并对比更复杂的图融合变体。

Result: 在CLEOPATRA与POMPAAF上，碎片感知建模优于标准ViT基线；集成将CLEOPATRA准确率从0.604提升至0.656，macro-F1从0.596提升至0.648；在POMPAAF的六种碎片设置中有四种超过最佳单分支。复杂图融合变体在POMPAAF与简单集成持平，仅在CLEOPATRA上有小幅数据集特定增益，不足以证明其额外复杂度合理。

Conclusion: 贡献有两点：一是分布级对比目标可持续锐化单分支识别性能；二是可解释性分析验证模型确实利用真实绘画证据，同时量化出基于修复的分支部分归因来自合成周边区域。

Abstract: Artistic style classification is usually studied on complete artworks, where models can exploit global composition, spatial organisation, and iconographic structure. In archaeological settings, however, artworks often survive only as fragmented remains, forcing recognition from incomplete, irregular, and context-limited visual evidence. We study fresco-fragment style classification using a progressive transformer-based framework. Starting from a ViT-B/16 baseline, we introduce foreground-guided masking to suppress background-only tokens, inpainting-based geometric regularisation to align irregular fragment supports with the ViT patch grid, and a supervised contrastive objective that operates on predictive distributions through a Kullback-Leibler similarity and consistently improves every branch. We combine the branches with a deliberately simple learnable logit ensemble. Experiments on CLEOPATRA and POMPAAF show that fragment-aware modelling improves over the standard ViT baseline, with the ensemble increasing accuracy from 0.604 to 0.656 and macro-F1 from 0.596 to 0.648 on CLEOPATRA, and outperforming the best single branch in four of six fragmentation settings on POMPAAF. We additionally evaluate a more complex graph-fusion variant and find that it matches the simple ensemble on POMPAAF while offering only a small, dataset-specific gain on CLEOPATRA, which does not justify its added complexity. Beyond these empirical gains, our contribution is twofold: a distribution-level contrastive objective that consistently sharpens single-branch recognition, and an interpretability analysis that verifies the models exploit genuine painted evidence, while quantifying that the inpainting-based branch draws part of its attribution from the synthesised surround.

</details>


### [55] [MAGIC: Marginal-Guided Compression with Optimal Transport for Efficient Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21018)
*Xu Yuan,Hua Liu,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练后处理压缩器 MAGIC，通过最优传输的双边际公式对齐后期交互检索中稀疏、非均匀的 patch 使用，在激进压缩下显著优于现有压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量页面嵌入（如 ColPali）虽能实现细粒度证据匹配，但带来巨大的索引存储和 MaxSim 评分开销。事后合并可在不重训练检索器的情况下降低成本，但其统一重建目标与后期交互检索诱导的稀疏、非均匀 patch 使用不匹配：激进压缩时会保留很少使用的 patch，同时把检索活动集中在过少的保留代表上。

Method: 提出 MAGIC（Marginal-Guided Compression with Optimal Transport），一种面向冻结多向量嵌入的免训练事后压缩器。方法推导 MaxSim 诱导的压缩代理目标，并通过双边际熵最优传输形式进行优化：检索需求源边际优先考虑高使用率 patch，平衡的目标边际则正则化保留 facet 的使用。

Result: 在 ViDoRe 基准、多种保留比例和检索骨干网络上，MAGIC 持续优于强事后压缩器，尤其在激进压缩区间增益显著；组件消融验证了两个边际的互补效果。

Conclusion: MAGIC 通过将压缩目标与后期交互检索的实际 patch 使用分布对齐，为高效视觉文档检索提供了一种有效的免训练后处理压缩方案。代码已开源于 https://github.com/xandery-geek/MAGIC。

Abstract: Recent visual document retrieval (VDR) systems such as ColPali use multi-vector page embeddings, in which patch-level vectors enable fine-grained evidence matching but incur substantial index storage and MaxSim scoring overhead. Post-hoc merging offers a practical route to efficient VDR by reducing this cost without retraining the retriever, but its uniform reconstruction objectives are poorly aligned with the sparse, non-uniform patch usage induced by late-interaction retrieval. Under aggressive compression, this misalignment can preserve rarely used patches while concentrating retrieval activity on too few retained representatives. To address this misalignment, we propose Marginal-Guided Compression with Optimal Transport (MAGIC), a training-free post-hoc compressor for efficient retrieval with frozen multi-vector embeddings. MAGIC derives a MaxSim-induced compression surrogate and optimizes it through a two-marginal entropic optimal-transport formulation, where a retrieval-demand source marginal prioritizes high-use patches and a balanced target marginal regularizes retained-facet usage. Across ViDoRe benchmarks, keep ratios, and retrieval backbones, MAGIC consistently outperforms strong post-hoc compressors, with particularly large gains in the aggressive-compression regime; component ablations verify the complementary effects of its two marginals. We release the code at: https://github.com/xandery-geek/MAGIC.

</details>


### [56] [MarsFM: Shading-Regularized Flow Matching for Martian Relief Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21095)
*Marius F. R. Juston*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MarsFM：一种图像条件潜空间流匹配模型，从单波段HiRISE RED正射影像估计局部火星地形起伏，融合生成先验、立体几何监督与可微Lunar-Lambert着色目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星局部地形（relief）高分辨率重建困难：单波段光学影像缺乏立体信息，且缺少可靠的高分辨率参考数据；作者希望利用学习到的地形先验与影像约束结合，在少量推理步数下实现地形估计。

Method: 以预训练生成先验为基础，构建图像条件潜空间流匹配模型；结合立体（stereo）导出的几何监督与可微Lunar-Lambert着色目标。损失包含relief、法线、梯度、曲率与序数（ordinal）项，从互补角度约束地形结构；并采用正仿射不变（positive-affine-invariant）影像比较约束渲染外观。

Result: 在每步数配置2024个patch记录上评估，1至20个Euler步的归一化signed-log relief空间中，仿射对齐RMSE均值为0.0935–0.0957，区间很窄，支持低步数推理。空间、微分与谱诊断显示地形整体对应良好，但存在平滑、振幅压缩与边界不匹配问题。

Conclusion: MarsFM提供了将学习地形先验与影像约束结合的框架；但当前分数仅衡量与VAE重建参考在正参考支撑上的一致性，要确立真正改进的物理地形分辨率，仍需匹配基线与独立高分辨率参考数据。

Abstract: We present MarsFM, an image-conditioned latent flow-matching model for local Martian relief estimation from single-band HiRISE RED orthoimagery. The method combines a pretrained generative prior with stereo-derived geometric supervision and a differentiable Lunar--Lambert shading objective. Relief, normal, gradient, curvature, and ordinal terms constrain complementary aspects of terrain structure, while a positive-affine-invariant image comparison constrains rendered appearance. An evaluation comprising 2024 gathered patch records per integration-step count yields mean affine-aligned RMSE between 0.0935 and 0.0957 in normalized signed-log relief space for one to twenty Euler steps. These scores measure agreement with VAE-reconstructed references on positive-reference support. Their narrow range supports low-step inference under this protocol. Spatial, differential, and spectral diagnostics show broad terrain correspondence alongside smoothing, amplitude compression, and boundary mismatch. MarsFM provides a framework for combining learned terrain priors with image-based constraints; establishing improved physical terrain resolution requires matched baselines and independent high-resolution reference data. Data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/SuperComputer/mars_hirise_dtm_processed-6aa9b66ba461e07f; code: https://github.com/Marius-Juston/MarsRecon.

</details>


### [57] [4DGS-Fixer: Generative Sparse-View 4D Gaussian Splatting with Iterative Refinement Guided by Video Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21176)
*Haitao Huang,Shenghao Zhao,Boyuan Tian,Shin-Fang Chng,Songlin Yang,Sheila Lim,Huangying Zhan,Yi Xu,Anyi Rao,Frank Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于视频扩散模型的迭代细化框架，解决稀疏视角视频动态场景合成中观测不足导致的病态问题与几何初始化不佳问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏视角4D高斯建模方法（几何先验、自适应优化、密度控制）无法从根本上解决观测不足和场景信息缺失导致的病态问题；且COLMAP在少视角下重建的点云稀疏不完整，大面积区域缺乏高斯支撑，后续优化难以恢复。

Method: 1) 估计多视角深度图并融合为稠密点云，为动态4DGS表示提供更完整的几何初始化；2) 使用预训练视频修复模型对沿新相机轨迹、不同时间步渲染的序列进行细化；3) 将恢复后的序列作为伪监督，正则化并迭代细化4DGS表示。

Result: 在广泛使用的基准数据集上大幅超越现有基线，相较此前最优方法PSNR提升近2 dB。

Conclusion: 通过稠密几何初始化与视频扩散模型伪监督的迭代细化，可有效提升稀疏视角动态4D场景的完整性与一致性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenges of dynamic scene synthesis from sparse-view videos. Existing methods employ geometric priors, adaptive optimization, or density-control strategies to improve 4D Gaussian modeling under sparse observations. However, they cannot fundamentally resolve the ill-posed problem caused by insufficient observations and missing scene information. Moreover, sparse-view 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) often suffers from poor geometric initialization: with only a few input views, COLMAP typically reconstructs sparse and incomplete point clouds, leaving large scene regions without sufficient Gaussian support and making them difficult to recover through subsequent optimization. To address these limitations, we propose a novel iterative refinement framework based on a video diffusion model to improve the completeness and consistency of dynamic 4D scenes. Specifically, we first estimate multi-view depth maps and fuse them into dense point clouds to provide more complete geometric initialization for a dynamic 4DGS representation. We then employ a pretrained video restoration model to refine sequences rendered along novel camera trajectories at different time steps. The restored sequences serve as pseudo-supervision to regularize and iteratively refine the 4DGS representation. Experiments on a widely used benchmark dataset demonstrate that our method substantially outperforms existing baselines, achieving nearly a 2 dB PSNR improvement over the previous best-performing method.

</details>


### [58] [OnomatoBridge: Onomatopoeia Translation and Rendering Pipeline in Manga](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21199)
*Takara Taniguchi,Wataru Shimoda,Kota Yamaguchi,Hideki Nakayama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OnomatoBridge，一种用于漫画视觉拟声词翻译的过滤式流水线，实现日语到英语的拟声词替换并保持视觉风格，在 Manga109 数据集上优于基线图像编辑模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漫画中的拟声词以其独特的视觉风格传达声音、动作与情感，其翻译需在保留视觉风格的同时用目标语言拟声词干净地替换日语拟声词。现有方法在移除日语拟声词和渲染风格化英语拟声词时，常产生残留伪影或风格不一致的问题。

Method: 提出 OnomatoBridge——一个用于视觉拟声词翻译的过滤式流水线（filtering pipeline），针对日语到英语的翻译任务，在 Manga109 拟声词数据集上进行评估，并与基线图像编辑模型进行对比。

Result: 实验表明，经所提方法过滤后的输出优于传统方法：英语文本正确率提升约 10 至 25 个百分点，日语文本残留相对减少约 20% 至 50%。

Conclusion: OnomatoBridge 能有效改善视觉拟声词翻译中的文本正确性与残留问题，验证了过滤式流水线在漫画拟声词翻译任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Manga is a comic drawn by black and white paints gaining popularity around the world. Onomatopoeia in Manga specifically appeals to the audience with its unique visual styles, which convey sound, motion, and emotion. Visual onomatopoeia translation requires the clean replacement of Japanese onomatopoeia with onomatopoeia in the other language while preserving their visual style. Existing approaches often produce residual artifacts or style inconsistency when removing the Japanese onomatopoeia and rendering stylized English onomatopoeia. To approach these problems, we present OnomatoBridge, a filtering pipeline for visual onomatopoeia translation. We evaluate OnomatoBridge from Japanese to English on the Manga109 onomatopoeia dataset and compare it with baseline image editing models. Experimental results show that the filtered outputs by the proposed method outperform those of conventional methods. OnomatoBridge improves English text correctness by roughly 10 to 25 points and reduces residual Japanese text by about 20 to 50% in relative terms.

</details>


### [59] [Hand-Aware Transition Modeling for Bimanual Procedural Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21207)
*Di Wen,Jimmy Weissert,Luc Maria Scherrer,Cedric Zöllner,Kailun Yang,Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Jiale Wei,Junwei Zheng,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HACT，一种基于预测双手事件转移模型的双臂装配流程异常检测方法，兼顾语义与时序surprisal，在两项电动工具流程中取得最高AUPRC/F1并减少恢复误报，且可零重训练跨装配顺序迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂装配中的流程异常检测需将每只手的动作置于已有执行上下文中判断：纠正性动作单独看似异常，而视觉合理的动作可能违反工序顺序，因此单一时刻的视觉判断不足，需要建模动作间的转移与双手并行职责。

Method: HACT构建在预测的逐手事件之上的转移模型：采用保留角色的历史表示以维持双手并发职责；用带标记的时间点过程为每个观测转移分配语义与时间surprisal；再由监督证据头与两状态滤波器将这些surprisal转化为逐手异常后验。评估采用基于预测事件的恢复感知协议与参与者不相交折，报告在验证参与者上选定操作点的恢复假阳性率。

Result: 在两项双臂电动工具流程上，HACT在所比较方法中取得最高AUPRC与F1，且恢复告警最少；在无需重训练的情况下迁移到同产品不同装配顺序时，仍保持最高AUPRC与F1。

Conclusion: 基于预测事件转移与双重surprisal的建模能有效刻画双臂流程异常，兼具准确性与低恢复误报，并具备跨装配顺序的泛化能力；源码已开源。

Abstract: Procedural anomaly detection in bimanual assembly requires judging each hand action against the execution so far. A corrective action may look unusual in isolation, while a visually plausible action can violate the order of the procedure. We present HACT, a transition model over predicted per-hand events. A role-preserving history keeps the concurrent responsibilities of both hands, and a marked temporal point process assigns each observed transition a semantic and temporal surprisal. A supervised evidence head and a two-state filter convert these surprisals into per-hand anomaly posteriors. A recovery-aware protocol on predicted events and participant-disjoint folds reports the recovery false-positive rate at an operating point selected on validation participants. On two bimanual power-tool procedures HACT has the highest AUPRC and F1 among the compared methods and the fewest recovery alarms. Applied without retraining to a different assembly order of the same product, it retains the highest AUPRC and F1. The source code is available at https://github.com/Kratos-Wen/HACT.

</details>


### [60] [Multi-viewpoint Geo-localization with Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21219)
*Adam D. Hines,Michael Milford,Tobias Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出事件相机视觉位置识别系统MegaEvent，通过I2E转换大规模数据集并微调事件视觉Transformer，实现视点鲁棒定位，并发布新数据集Springfield-Event-VPR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人定位需容忍视点变化，事件相机虽受关注，但现有事件定位器对视点变化研究不足，且缺乏强调视点变化的挑战性数据集。

Method: 将5个大规模地理标记数据集经Image-to-Event转换为合成事件流；用多损失函数微调预训练事件视觉Transformer主干，得到MegaEvent；并录制多相机朝向的Springfield-Event-VPR数据集。

Result: 在3个现有事件定位数据集上平均Recall@1达82%，分别领先次优事件方法20个召回点和基于帧的VPR模型8-26个召回点；在新数据集上领先最强基线9个召回点。

Conclusion: MegaEvent在视点变化下具有鲁棒的事件视觉位置识别性能，并提供了具有视点变化挑战的新基准数据集与开源代码。

Abstract: Robot localization is an ongoing challenge that demands mapping and positioning systems that are tolerant to viewpoint change. Event cameras are attracting increasing interest and adoption in robotics; however, dealing with viewpoint variance is an under-investigated problem in existing event-based localizers. In addition, event-based datasets that emphasize viewpoint variance for challenging localization situations are scarce. Here, we introduce an event-based visual place recognition (VPR) system that performs robustly under viewpoint changes. We converted five large-scale geo-tagged datasets, conventionally used to train frame-based localization systems, into synthetic event streams using Image-to-Event (I2E) conversion, and used them to fine-tune a pre-trained event-based vision transformer backbone with a multi-loss function, yielding a system we call MegaEvent that learns viewpoint-robust features for place recognition. We achieved an average Recall@1 of 82% across three existing event-based localization datasets, leading the next best event-based method by 20 recall points, and frame-based VPR models applied directly to event frames by 8 to 26 recall points. We introduce a new, challenging dataset - Springfield-Event-VPR - which features a 3.7km walking route recorded in three camera orientations for a total of 11.1km, which MegaEvent outperforms the strongest baseline by 9 recall points. The code for MegaEvent is available at https://github.com/AdamDHines/megaevent.

</details>


### [61] [VGGT-CAD: Reconstructing Parametric CAD 3D Model with Geometric Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21225)
*Chunan Yu,Tianrun Chen,Fu Shen,Cheng Chen,Lanyun Zhu,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGGT-CAD：将预训练3D几何先验引入参数化CAD重建，通过相机条件token、变视角跨视图聚合与免训练视角选择，实现单/多视图CAD命令序列的非自回归生成，并构建多视图基准VideoCAD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉观测恢复参数化CAD需同时还原精确几何与可编辑建模操作，在视角有限且存在歧义时尤为困难；现有方法主要依赖2D外观线索，缺乏强多视图几何先验。

Method: 1) 将预训练3D几何先验迁移至CAD重建，把相机参数编码为条件token并与图像token联合建模；2) 提出变视角跨视图上下文聚合模块，自适应融合任意数量视图特征；3) 设计免训练的几何感知视角选择策略，在推理时挑选互补且可靠的帧；4) 使用非自回归解码器将表示解码为CAD命令序列；5) 基于已有CAD数据经多视图重渲染构建大规模多视图视频基准VideoCAD。

Result: 大量实验表明，在不同观测配置（单视图与多视图）下，VGGT-CAD对视觉CAD重建均具有有效性。

Conclusion: 引入几何先验与多视图自适应融合可显著提升参数化CAD重建在受限、歧义视角下的表现，并为该任务提供新的多视图基准。

Abstract: Parametric CAD reconstruction requires recovering both precise geometry and editable modeling operations from visual observations, making it challenging under limited and ambiguous views. Existing methods mainly rely on 2D appearance cues and lack strong multi-view geometric priors. In this work, we present VGGT-CAD, a geometry-aware framework for parametric CAD reconstruction from single- and multi-view observations. We transfer pretrained 3D geometric priors into CAD reconstruction by encoding camera parameters as condition tokens and jointly modeling them with image tokens. To handle varying numbers of viewpoints, we introduce a variable-view cross-view context aggregation module that adaptively fuses multi-view features. We further develop a training-free geometry-aware view selection strategy to select complementary and reliable frames during inference. The resulting representation is decoded into CAD command sequences using a non-autoregressive decoder. We also develop VideoCAD, a large-scale multi-view video benchmark derived from existing CAD data through multi-view re-rendering. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of VGGT-CAD for visual CAD reconstruction under different observation configurations.

</details>


### [62] [Multiclass Semantic Segmentation of Wildland Fire Images Using Context-Aware Centralized Copy-Paste Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21241)
*Joon Tai Kim,Nishanth Kunchala,Vishv Patel,Tianle Chen,Ziyu Dong,Daniel Ospina Acero,Roger Williams,Mrinal Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文感知的CCPDA数据增强策略，通过限制火焰粘贴到语义有效区域并匹配Ash-Vegetation组成，提升野火图像分割精度与场景真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习图像分割的精确标注成本高、耗时长，野火场景动态性强导致标注数据集稀缺；前期CCPDA方法随机粘贴火焰簇会产生不合理场景（如火焰出现在沥青上）。

Method: 提出上下文感知的CCPDA策略：将火焰粘贴位置限制在语义有效的目标区域，选择Ash-Vegetation组成与源上下文最匹配的位置；保留目标图像中已有火焰区域，避免不合理放置，确保增强样本上下文合理。通过数值分析及加权和多目标优化(MOO)与其他增强方法对比评估。

Result: 上下文感知数据增强策略提升了分割性能与上下文真实感，优于其他增强方法。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的CCPDA能有效改善小样本多类野火数据集的数据质量与真实感，从而提升分割性能，为标注稀缺场景下的数据增强提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Producing accurate annotations for deep learning based image segmentation is both costly and labor intensive. This challenge is especially evident in wildland fire applications, where accurately labeled datasets are scarce due to the difficulty of collecting and annotating dynamic fire scenes. To address this problem, our previous work introduced the Centralized Copy-Paste Data Augmentation (CCPDA) method for semantic segmentation of wildland fire imagery, which generates artificial training samples by randomly pasting fire clusters from source images onto target images. However, random placement can produce contextually unrealistic scenes, such as fire burning on asphalt. In this paper, we present a context-aware strategy designed specifically to improve data quality and realism in small multiclass wildland fire datasets, ensuring that augmented samples remain contextually meaningful. The proposed method restricts fire placement to semantically valid target regions and selects the location whose Ash-Vegetation composition most closely matches the source context. This approach preserves existing fire regions in the target image, prevents unrealistic placements, and maintains contextual accuracy by generating images that resemble real wildland fire scenes. We evaluate the Context-Aware CCPDA strategy through numerical analysis and comparisons with other augmentation methods by a weighted sum-based multi-objective optimization (MOO) approach. The results confirm that the context-aware data augmentation strategy leads to improved segmentation performance and contextual realism, outperforming other augmentation procedures.

</details>


### [63] [SafeStyle: Calibrated Style Residual Injection for Controllable Style-Leakage Trade-off in Diffusion Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21242)
*Zhangping Yang,Min Li,Song Yan,Rong Gao,Xinliang Bi,Guanye Xiong,Yujie He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 SafeStyle，通过在冻结扩散模型中校准风格残差注入，解耦风格与内容并平衡风格保真度与内容泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考图引导的扩散风格化中，图像条件化会把可迁移的风格线索与参考图特有内容纠缠在一起，形成固有权衡：条件化越强风格越保真但内容泄漏越多，强力抑制泄漏又会削弱风格表达；同时纹理主导与几何主导的风格在空间组织方式上差异明显，使问题更复杂。

Method: SafeStyle 是一个免训练框架，在冻结扩散模型中做校准式风格残差注入。首先从小规模校准集估计“风格支撑子空间”与“内容关联子空间”，在保留二者有用重叠的同时抑制无用的内容变化；随后将纯化后的风格证据按自适应空间粒度进行迁移，并通过显式的残差范数预算约束其有效影响。

Result: 在纹理主导与几何主导风格上，SafeStyle 达到 0.432 的 DINO 风格相似度并保持有竞争力的文本对齐；在语义不相交的泄漏压力基准上，达到 0.474 的 DINO 风格相似度，同时语义泄漏仅 0.8%。

Conclusion: 结果表明 SafeStyle 能在风格保真度与参考内容抑制之间取得有效平衡，验证了基于子空间纯化与残差范数预算的校准式注入策略的有效性。

Abstract: Reference-guided diffusion stylization aims to transfer visual style from a reference image while preserving the semantics specified by a text prompt. However, image conditioning often entangles transferable style cues with reference-specific content, leading to an inherent trade-off: stronger conditioning improves style fidelity but increases content leakage, whereas aggressive suppression reduces leakage at the cost of style expression. This challenge is further complicated by the distinct spatial organization of texture- and geometry-dominant styles. To address these issues, we propose SafeStyle, a training-free framework for calibrated style residual injection in frozen diffusion models. SafeStyle first estimates style-supported and content-associated subspaces from compact calibration sets, preserving their informative overlap while suppressing useless content variations. It then transports the purified style evidence over adaptive spatial granularity and constrains its effective influence through an explicit residual-norm budget. Experiments across texture- and geometry-dominant styles show that SafeStyle achieves a DINO style similarity of 0.432 while maintaining competitive text alignment. On a semantically disjoint leakage-stress benchmark, it further achieves a DINO style similarity of 0.474 with only 0.8\% semantic leakage, demonstrating an effective balance between style fidelity and reference-content suppression.

</details>


### [64] [Geometry-Aware Diffusion Guidance via Curvature-Adaptive Tubular Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21251)
*Enze Jiang,Jinwei He,Zheng Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的曲率自适应管状校正（CAT）插件，将引导梯度分解为法向与切向分量，按一阶位移与方向曲率联合优化校正幅度，稳定扩散采样轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引导会使扩散采样轨迹进入学习分数支撑不足的区域。现有切向投影策略仅限制相对等密度面的一阶偏离，丢弃了可能有用的法向运动，也忽视了切向运动在弯曲曲面上引起的二阶偏离。

Method: CAT将引导梯度分解为法向与切向分量，法向位移按一阶计费，切向位移按方向曲率计费，在共享的、依赖噪声的几何预算下通过一维对偶方程求解二者联合最优幅度；用Armijo回溯校准有限步长，并用无矩阵方向导数避免构造完整分数雅可比。

Result: 在FFHQ和ImageNet的七个逆问题上，CAT改进了像素空间与隐空间的主干采样器，感知指标提升尤为一致；改进了InverseBench上的黑洞重建，在所有测试的无分类器引导尺度下取得最低FID，且保持稳定的饱和度与对比度。

Conclusion: 结果支持曲率感知的管状控制作为一种可复用的机制，用于稳定扩散引导；理论上建立了管状近似的局部保证、校正的唯一性以及充分的目标下降。

Abstract: Gradient-guided diffusion samplers provide flexible priors for inverse problems and conditional generation, but strong guidance can move the sampling trajectory into regions where the learned score is poorly supported. Existing tangent-projection strategies limit first-order departure from an iso-density surface, yet discard potentially useful normal motion and overlook the second-order departure induced by tangent motion on a curved surface. We introduce curvature-adaptive tubular correction (CAT), a training-free plugin that regulates both effects within a shared, noise-dependent geometric budget. CAT decomposes the guidance gradient into normal and tangent components, charges normal displacement at first order and tangent displacement according to directional curvature, and obtains their jointly optimal magnitudes from a one-dimensional dual equation. Armijo backtracking calibrates the resulting finite step against the actual guidance objective, while matrix-free directional derivatives avoid constructing the full score Jacobian. We establish local guarantees for the tubular approximation, uniqueness of the correction, and sufficient objective decrease. Across seven inverse problems on FFHQ and ImageNet, CAT improves the evaluated pixel- and latent-space host samplers, with particularly consistent gains in perceptual metrics. It also improves black hole reconstruction on InverseBench and yields the lowest FID among the compared methods at every tested classifier-free guidance scale, while maintaining stable saturation and contrast. These results support curvature-aware tubular control as a reusable mechanism for stabilizing diffusion guidance.

</details>


### [65] [Edit-VAR: Taming Visual Autoregressive Model for Precise Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21268)
*Chongbo Zhao,Jiangming Wang,Xilai Wang,Xinyu Wang,Jingyi Tang,Chunjie Hao,Pengjie Song,Yue Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Edit-VAR是首个基于预训练视觉自回归视频模型的免训练、免反演文本引导视频编辑框架，通过概率引导的条件token替换、注意力引导的token级与尺度感知调制等机制实现编辑保真与源内容保持的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导视频编辑需在修改目标内容的同时保持未编辑区域的外观与时序一致性。现有方法存在两难：基于训练的方法控制力强但需大量数据与算力；免训练方法中，免反演范式受源保持引导限制导致编辑强度不足、语义修改不完整，而基于反演的范式存在近似误差累积，引起源内容漂移与时序不一致。

Method: 提出Edit-VAR框架：将源视频直接编码为多尺度离散token，进行概率引导的条件token替换以保持源内容；采用注意力引导的token级与尺度感知调制，在编辑相关位置与生成阶段选择性地放松源约束；通过"尺度解耦生成"（后期尺度约束释放）重建运动一致细节并减少纹理破碎；并利用残差引导token剪枝，在最后两个高分辨率尺度上利用冗余降低推理成本。

Result: 大量实验与盲测用户研究表明，Edit-VAR在编辑保真度、源内容保持、时序一致性与推理效率方面总体优于现有免训练视频编辑方法。

Conclusion: Edit-VAR验证了免训练、免反演范式结合自回归视频模型进行视频编辑的可行性，在多个维度上超越现有免训练方法，同时兼顾效率。

Abstract: Text-guided video editing modifies target content while preserving the appearance and temporal coherence of unedited regions. Training-based approaches provide strong control but demand substantial data and computation. Training-free methods fall into inversion-free and inversion-based paradigms. Inversion-free approaches avoid trajectory recovery, but their source-preserving guidance can limit editing strength and leave semantic changes incomplete. Inversion-based approaches recover a latent trajectory before regeneration, where approximation errors can accumulate and cause source-content drift and temporal inconsistency. We introduce Edit-VAR, the first training-free and inversion-free framework for text-guided video editing with a pretrained visual autoregressive video model. Edit-VAR directly encodes the source video into multi-scale discrete tokens and performs probability-guided conditional token replacement for source preservation. Attention-guided token-wise and scale-aware modulation selectively relaxes source constraints over edit-relevant positions and generation stages. Scale-Decoupled Generation, implemented as late-scale constraint release, regenerates motion-consistent details and reduces texture fragmentation. Residual-guided token pruning further exploits redundancy at the final two high-resolution scales to reduce inference cost. Extensive experiments and a blind user study demonstrate that Edit-VAR outperforms existing training-free video editing methods overall in editing fidelity, source preservation, temporal coherence, and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [66] [Beyond Exact Match: Task-Aware GRPO for Cross-Domain PCBA Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21276)
*Jia Li,Li Dai,Peng Jia,Zhenzhen Hu,Chee Seng Chan,Bingkun Bao,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向跨域PCBA视觉问答的多模态推理框架，通过统一指令数据构建、任务感知GRPO奖励设计与鲁棒推理策略，在官方PCBA Standard-to-Real Grand Challenge榜单上取得83.24的总分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化PCBA检测中的标准引导决策需要系统联合推理细粒度视觉线索、元件语义与制造知识。现有大型视觉语言模型虽具潜力，但面临标准样本与真实产线图像之间的域偏移，以及选择型与计数型任务异构输出空间的挑战，难以直接部署。

Method: 将标准来源、真实场景及辅助PCB域数据转换为统一指令格式，构建与视觉证据、问题语义、候选选项和真值答案对齐的已验证推理轨迹；提出任务感知组相对策略优化（Task-Aware GRPO），用多组件语义奖励（选择题）、距离感知奖励（计数题）和格式奖励替代精确匹配监督；推理阶段结合答案-选项语义一致性校正、自一致性投票与多模型仲裁。

Result: 在官方PCBA Standard-to-Real Grand Challenge排行榜上取得83.24的总分，验证了任务感知奖励设计与鲁棒推理对跨域PCBA视觉问答的有效性。

Conclusion: 任务感知奖励设计与鲁棒推理策略能够有效缓解域偏移和异构输出空间问题，为跨域PCBA视觉问答提供可行方案。

Abstract: In automated Printed Circuit Board Assembly (PCBA) inspection, standards-guided decisions require systems to jointly reason over fine-grained visual cues, component semantics, and manufacturing knowledge. Although large vision-language models (VLMs) provide a promising foundation, their deployment is hindered by the domain shift between standards-derived samples and real-world production-line imagery, together with heterogeneous output spaces spanning choice-based and numerical counting tasks. To address these challenges, we propose a multimodal reasoning framework for cross-domain PCBA visual question answering. The framework converts standards-derived, real-world, and auxiliary PCB-domain data into a unified instruction format and constructs verified reasoning traces aligned with visual evidence, question semantics, candidate options, and ground-truth answers. We further introduce Task-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), which moves beyond exact-match supervision by integrating multi-component semantic rewards for choice-based questions, distance-aware rewards for counting questions, and an auxiliary format reward for valid outputs. During inference, answer-option semantic consistency correction, self-consistency voting, and multi-model arbitration are combined to improve prediction robustness. The proposed system achieves an Overall Score of 83.24 on the official PCBA Standard-to-Real Grand Challenge leaderboard, demonstrating the effectiveness of task-aware reward design and robust inference for cross-domain PCBA visual question answering.

</details>


### [67] [Combining Object Detection with Geometry-Aware Clustering to Distinguish Overlapping Plants in UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21304)
*Ik Jae Lee,Hieu D. Nguyen,Mahbubur Meenar,Carlos Morrison Martinez,Cameron Connelly*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向RGB无人机影像的几何感知后检测框架，通过质心与径向交点几何特征聚类，解决密集冠层中相邻植株被检测为单一目标的重叠植株拆分问题，实现无额外传感器、无需重训练的株级信息提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集作物冠层中相邻植株频繁重叠，检测器常将其识别为单个目标，导致株级测量（如计数、表型参数）可靠性下降，而依赖深度传感器、像素级分割或三维重建的方案成本高、工程复杂度大。

Method: 在灌木级检测区域基础上，对检测到的叶片/枝条提取两类互补几何特征：部件质心与由植株结构推导的径向交点（RIPs）；采用K-means与高斯混合模型判别区域内为单株或两株重叠植株；引入密度滤波抑制虚假径向交点，并通过后处理集成融合空间与方向几何信息。

Result: 在田间茄子与番茄无人机影像上评估：质心聚类对茄子F1为0.89；质心-RIP组合方法对番茄表现最佳，K-means下准确率0.80、精确率1.00、F1为0.75；密度滤波显著提升番茄RIP聚类效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为轻量、模块化工程方案，可无缝接入现有RGB无人机监测流程，无需深度传感器、像素级分割、三维重建或重训练主检测器，表明几何推理可补充深度学习检测，提升密集冠层下的株级解析能力。

Abstract: Reliable plant-level information from unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) imagery is important for automated crop monitoring. However, in dense crop canopies, adjacent plants frequently overlap and are detected as a single object, reducing the reliability of plant-level measurements. This study presents a geometry-aware post-detection framework for resolving overlapping plant instances using standard RGB UAV imagery.
  The framework combines object detection with geometric clustering of plant components. Leaves or branches detected within each bush-level region are represented using two complementary geometric features: component centroids and radial intersection points (RIPs) derived from detected plant structures. K-means and Gaussian mixture models determine whether a detected region contains a single plant or two overlapping plants. Density filtering suppresses spurious radial intersections, and a post-pipeline ensemble combines spatial and directional geometric information.
  The framework was evaluated using UAV imagery of eggplant and tomato crops under field conditions. Centroid-based clustering achieved an F1-score of 0.89 for eggplant, while the combined centroid-RIP approach achieved the best tomato performance, with an accuracy of 0.80, precision of 1.00, and F1-score of 0.75 using K-means. Density filtering substantially improved RIP-based clustering for tomato.
  The proposed approach provides a lightweight, modular engineering solution that can be integrated with existing RGB UAV monitoring pipelines without additional depth sensors, pixel-level segmentation, three-dimensional reconstruction, or retraining of the primary bush detector. The results demonstrate that geometric reasoning applied to existing detector outputs can complement deep-learning-based object detection and improve plant-level interpretation in dense agricultural canopies.

</details>


### [68] [S3VD: Semantic-Guidance Spatio-Temporal Scanning for Video Deraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21322)
*Kui Jiang,Yiang Chen,Yan Luo,Zhaocheng Yu,Junjun Jiang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S3VD视频去雨框架，结合多尺度语义融合与时空扫描融合，缓解Mamba在语义保持和时空建模上的不足，在基准上取得SOTA，PSNR平均提升0.84 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强降雨会破坏视频高频细节并引入运动模糊，严重影响下游视觉任务可靠性。现有Mamba等状态空间模型虽具线性复杂度与长程依赖建模能力，但在雨天视频的低质量表征下，难以保持2D空间语义完整性并有效建模3D时空相关性。

Method: 提出S3VD语义引导时空扫描框架，包含两个模块：1) 多尺度语义融合（MSSF）模块，引入DINOv2的时序语义先验指导特征表示，缓解Mamba一维展平操作导致的局部语义上下文丢失；2) 时空扫描融合（STSF）模块，设计时空扫描机制并提出解耦门控Mamba（DG-Mamba）层，用两个独立门控自适应控制输入片段中的前序与后续上下文信息，优化帧内与帧间相关性建模。

Result: 在视频去雨基准上验证了S3VD的优越性，取得SOTA性能，相较Mamba类基线平均PSNR提升0.84 dB。

Conclusion: 通过语义引导与时空扫描融合，S3VD有效克服了Mamba在视频去雨中的语义丢失与时空建模局限，为恶劣天气视频复原提供了高效且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Heavy rainfall severely degrades outdoor videos by corrupting high-frequency details and introducing motion blur, critically undermining the reliability of visual tasks. Recently, State Space Models (SSMs), particularly Mamba, have emerged as efficient alternatives for vision tasks with their linear complexity and ability to model long-range dependencies. However, when confronted with the poor visual representations in rainy videos, Mamba still faces difficulties in preserving the integrity of 2D spatial semantics and modeling 3D spatio-temporal correlations. To break these limitations, we introduce S3VD, a Semantic-Guidance Spatio-Temporal Scanning framework for video deraining, featuring two key innovations: Multi-Scale Semantic Fusion (MSSF) Module and Spatio-Temporal Scanning Fusion (STSF) Module. The former integrates temporal semantic priors from DINOv2 to guide precise feature representation and counteract the loss of local semantic context inherent to Mamba's 1D flatten operation, enhancing robustness against extreme degradation. The latter introduces a spatio-temporal scanning mechanism and devises a Decoupled-Gating Mamba (DG-Mamba) layer, which employs two independent gates to adaptively control preceding and subsequent contextual information within the input clip, optimizing intra-frame and inter-frame correlation modeling. Experiments on video deraining benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of S3VD, achieving state-of-the-art performance with an average 0.84 dB PSNR improvement over Mamba-based baselines.

</details>


### [69] [VeriFuse: Bounded Vision-Language Arbitration and Reason-Guided Refinement for Cooperative 3D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21323)
*Hongyi Lin,Yiyao Liu,Qi Kang,Heye Huang,Yang Liu,Haris Koutsopoulos,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VeriFuse是一个面向车路协同3D检测的有界仲裁框架，让冻结的视觉语言模型在受限动作空间内对跨源候选进行语义选择，几何由确定性约束决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）具备场景理解与语义判断能力，但在协同感知中的角色不明确：直接回归3D检测不可靠且计算昂贵，仅用于挑选单一源输出又会丢失其他智能体的信息。因此需要为VLM在协同感知中设定清晰且受限的职责。

Method: 各智能体先独立产生检测结果。围绕每个车端与路侧提议，生成源条件化的几何候选，将原始检测、其扰动及跨源假设合并为统一候选池。冻结的VLM在三种可行动作中选择：SELECT（选取合适候选）、REFINE（对象受支持但所有候选几何均不合适时对已有锚点细化）、REJECT（拒绝不受支持的路侧独有提议）。

Result: 在DAIR-V2X数据集上，VeriFuse取得0.494/0.357的协同3D AP50/AP70，并将300 ms延迟下车辆侧BEV AP50的相对下降限制在1.7%。

Conclusion: VeriFuse为VLM在协同感知中赋予明确且受约束的角色：语义推理用于消解跨智能体假设间的歧义，而最终3D几何由确定性约束确定。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong scene understanding and semantic judgment across diverse tasks, but their appropriate role in cooperative perception remains unclear. Directly asking a VLM to regress 3D detections is unreliable and computationally expensive, whereas using it to select the output of a single source discards useful information from other agents. We introduce VeriFuse, a bounded arbitration framework for vehicle-infrastructure cooperative 3D detection. Each agent first produces detections independently. Around each vehicle and roadside proposal, VeriFuse generates source-conditioned geometric candidates and combines the original detections, their perturbations, and cross-source hypotheses into a unified candidate pool. A frozen VLM then chooses among three admissible actions: SELECT an adequate candidate; REFINE an existing anchor when an object is supported but all candidates are geometrically inadequate; or REJECT an unsupported infrastructure-only proposal. Experiments on the DAIR-V2X dataset show that VeriFuse achieves 0.494/0.357 cooperative 3D AP50/AP70 and limits the relative vehicle-side BEV AP50 drop under a 300 ms delay to 1.7%. Overall, VeriFuse assigns the VLM a clear and constrained role in cooperative perception: semantic reasoning resolves ambiguity among cross-agent hypotheses, while deterministic constraints determine the final 3D geometry.

</details>


### [70] [Cube-Splat: High-Fidelity 360° Gaussian Splatting SLAM via Cubemap Factorization and Adjoint-Consistent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21347)
*Xiangfei Guo,Hao Shi,Yufan Zhang,Zhonghua Yi,Yongqi Mao,Xiaoting Yin,Kaiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个全景3DGS-SLAM框架Cube-Splat，将360°帧分解为共享光心的四虚拟针孔立方体贴图，以前面为主位姿状态并用伴随映射聚合各面梯度，实现单状态一致更新；同时搭建映射模块稠密优化各向异性高斯，并发布合成数据集SynPano。在PALVIO、OmniBlender与SynPano上跟踪精度与重建保真度均达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS-SLAM虽实现针孔相机稠密视觉SLAM，但针对全景图像的设计缺失；全景与针孔成像模型差异大，直接沿用难以保证跨视角几何一致性，同时全景SLAM缺乏多样且具有高保真、多模态真值的评测基准。

Method: 1）将每帧360°图像分解为四个固定朝向、共享同一光心的虚拟针孔视图组成的立方体贴图；2）指定前面（front face）为主位姿状态，通过伴随映射（adjoint mapping）将各面梯度累积到同一状态，实现多面观测对单一状态的一致更新并严格保持跨视角几何一致性；3）映射模块利用聚合的立方体光线稠密化并优化各向异性高斯，实现高保真稠密重建；4）构建可扩展、照片级真实的合成数据集SynPano，具有参数化复杂轨迹与多模态真值。

Result: 在PALVIO、OmniBlender两个公开基准及自建SynPano数据集（覆盖室内外场景）上的大量实验表明，Cube-Splat在跟踪精度与重建保真度两方面均达到SOTA。代码与SynPano数据集已开源。

Conclusion: Cube-Splat验证了将3DGS-SLAM拓展至全景成像的可行性与优越性：单光心立方体贴图参数化配合伴随梯度聚合，可在保持跨视角几何一致性的同时实现统一位姿优化与稠密重建；所提SynPano为全景SLAM提供可扩展、多模态的评测基准。

Abstract: Recent progress in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has enabled dense visual SLAM with pinhole cameras, yet most pipelines are not designed for panoramic imagery. We present Cube-Splat, the first panoramic GS-SLAM framework that factorizes each 360° frame into a cubemap of four fixed-orientation virtual pinhole views sharing a single optical center. By designating the front face as the primary pose state, we accumulate gradients from all faces via an adjoint mapping, thereby enabling multi-face observations to coherently update a single state while strictly preserving cross-view geometric consistency. Concurrently, our mapping module densifies and optimizes anisotropic Gaussians using aggregated cubemap rays for high-fidelity, dense reconstruction.
