An exploratory study with 12 Deaf ASL signers assesses an LLM-mediated sign-to-sign response pipeline, highlighting the potential of unified modeling to support sign language conversation (SLC).

</details>


### [106] [ZYT-World: A Real-Time Controllable World Model for Closed-Loop Autonomous-Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21712)
*Boni Hu,Xiong Wei,Haoming Huang,Yong Huang,Chenbo Wang,Yi Yang,Jiancheng Wang,Ruicheng Zhu,Zhimin Yang,Guanglai Liu,Qiaowan Jin,Dongzhuo Wang,Haiwei Kuang,Jiajun Fan,Yue Wu,Jiaxin Wei,Hao Sun,Feihong Yan,Wei Bi,Kaixuan Wang,Zichao Guo,Xiaozhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZYT-World 提出统一生成式世界模型架构，原生支持四路鱼眼与三路针孔混合相机 rig 的高保真可控闭环仿真，并通过蒸馏、量化与推理引擎实现一步流式生成，兼顾长期稳定性、低延迟与地点记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向端到端与视觉-语言-动作驾驶策略的生产级部署，现有生成式世界模型存在三项未满足要求：(1) 在原生分辨率下忠实复现鱼眼-针孔混合 rig；(2) 在因果、逐时间步交互与长期稳定性、低延迟之间取得平衡；(3) 在重访同一地点时保持场景身份一致性。

Method: 提出 ZYT-World 单一架构：用投影特定的 Plucker 适配器编码相机几何；自运动自适应层归一化提供全局运动控制；轻量像素对齐布局以实例级框、朝向与颜色条件化参与者与信号；异构训练结合全 rig 几何覆盖与高分辨率细节；通过教师强制、因果一致性蒸馏、自 rollout 分布匹配蒸馏及 RigCritic（联合评估七视图 rig）将 40 步双向教师蒸馏为一步、逐 latent 流式生成器；采用 19M 参数 TinyVAE 解码器、W8A8 量化与自有推理引擎降低解码、骨干与增量执行开销；最后用真实采集的跨轨迹对训练即插即用隐式记忆模块以保留地点特定证据。

Result: 在内部多视图测试集上，一步模型保留教师模型 90% 以上的 PSNR 与 SSIM，FID、FVD、LPIPS 与教师的差距在 11% 以内；在仅生成器计时下比 40 步双向教师快 107.7 倍；TinyVAE 解码比 Wan 快 59.8 倍；30 秒 rollout 与跨轨迹重访展示了预期的长期与记忆行为。

Conclusion: ZYT-World 以单一架构同时解决混合相机 rig 高保真原生生成、因果流式低延迟长期稳定生成以及地点身份保持三大生产级需求，验证了蒸馏、量化、专用推理引擎与隐式记忆模块组合在驾驶世界模型部署中的有效性。

Abstract: Generative world models offer controllable and repeatable closed-loop simulation for end-to-end and vision-language-action driving policies, but production deployment exposes three unresolved requirements: faithfully reproducing a mixed fisheye-pinhole rig at native resolutions; reconciling causal, per-timestep interaction with long-horizon stability and low latency; and preserving scene identity when a location is revisited. We present ZYT-World, a single architecture that natively generates four fisheye views with field of view > 180° and three pinhole views. Projection-specific Plucker adapters encode camera geometry, ego-motion adaptive layer normalization provides global motion control, and a lightweight pixel-aligned layout conditions traffic participants and signals through instance-level boxes, headings and colors. Heterogeneous training combines full-rig geometric coverage with high-resolution detail. Teacher forcing, causal consistency distillation, self-rollout distribution matching distillation, and RigCritic transform a 40-step bidirectional teacher into a one-step, per-latent streaming generator, with RigCritic evaluating the seven-view rig jointly. A 19M-parameter variational autoencoder decoder (TinyVAE), W8A8 quantization, and our inference engine reduce decoding, backbone, and incremental-execution costs, respectively. Finally, cross-trajectory pairs derived from real captures train a plug-in implicit-memory module that preserves place-specific evidence. On the internal multi-view test set, the one-step model retains more than 90% of the teacher's PSNR and SSIM, while FID, FVD, and LPIPS stay within 11% of the teacher. Under the generator-only timing in Figure 2, it is 107.7 times faster than the 40-step bidirectional teacher. TinyVAE decodes 59.8 times faster than Wan. 30s rollouts and cross-trajectory revisits show the intended long-horizon and memory behavior.

</details>


### [107] [Balanced Prompt Adaptation against Entropy-Induced Collapse for Test-Time Binary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21743)
*Zhengshan Wang,Joshua Charles Webster-Ford,Yifei Tian,Xinxin Wang,Long Chen,Weiping Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对不平衡二值分割中熵最小化导致预测崩塌的问题，提出平衡锚点提示适配方法BAPA，在四个域实验中取得最高平均Dice。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵最小化是测试时适配的常用目标，但在不平衡二值分割中容易失效。与图像分类不同，密集分割聚合数千个像素预测，较大的预测类别会主导更新，将少数类预测拉向自身，随预测饱和、熵梯度消失而产生退化掩码。

Method: 在共享偏移模型下从理论上证明上述崩塌现象。据此提出BAPA，包含两个互补模块：类别平衡锚点（CBA）模块从每个预测类别分别选取高置信度锚点，并使前景与背景获得相等的总损失权重，防止较大区域主导更新；动态提示适配（DPA）模块在每次预测更新后刷新锚点，仅优化文本侧提示残差，冻结视觉-语言编码器，从而在改变预训练密集视觉表示的前提下细化前景-背景决策边界。

Result: 在来自四个域的实验中所评估方法里，BAPA取得最高的平均Dice。分解消融实验进一步验证了CBA与DPA的互补作用。

Conclusion: 平衡提示适配是替代熵最小化、用于测试时二值分割的一种有效方案。

Abstract: Entropy minimization is a standard objective for test-time adaptation (TTA), but it can fail in imbalanced binary segmentation. Unlike image classification, dense segmentation aggregates thousands of pixel predictions, allowing the larger predicted class to dominate the update, pull minority predictions toward itself, and produce a degenerate mask as predictions saturate and their entropy gradients vanish. We theoretically establish this collapse in a shared-shift model. This analysis motivates Balanced-Anchor Prompt Adaptation (BAPA), which combines two complementary modules. The Class-Balanced Anchors (CBA) module selects high-confidence anchors separately from each predicted class and gives foreground and background equal total loss weight, preventing the larger region from dominating the update. Dynamic Prompt Adaptation (DPA) refreshes these anchors after each prediction update and optimizes only text-side prompt residuals while keeping the vision-language encoders frozen. This prompt-only update refines the foreground-background decision boundary without altering the pretrained dense visual representation. Across experiments from four domains, BAPA achieves the highest mean Dice among the evaluated methods. Factorized ablations further validate the complementary roles of CBA and DPA, supporting balanced prompt adaptation as an effective alternative to entropy minimization for test-time binary segmentation.

</details>


### [108] [SFVO: Decoupled Confidence-Guided Stereo-Flow Visual Odometry with Bidirectional PnP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21754)
*Kai Zhang,Guoyang Zhao,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SFVO是一种对应关系驱动的双目视觉里程计框架，基于预训练立体匹配与光流模型构建几何约束并预测置信度，实现稳健精确的位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习VO多聚焦单目，存在尺度模糊；双目VO虽天然具备真实度量尺度，但因计算开销大、建模复杂而研究不足。同时，立体匹配与光流估计虽使稠密对应更准确可靠，其互补的几何信息尚未在VO中被充分利用。

Method: SFVO直接建立在预训练的立体匹配与光流模型之上，利用它们估计立体对应与时间对应；不直接从图像学习位姿，而是将学习到的对应关系映射为几何约束，并预测哪些点可信。为提升基于对应的几何约束可靠性，引入解耦的旋转与平移置信度图，以更好匹配视觉对应与6自由度变换的特性。

Result: 在室外与室内数据集上的大量实验表明，SFVO实现了稳健、准确的位姿估计，并具有强泛化能力。

Conclusion: SFVO通过复用预训练对应模型、以几何约束替代直接位姿回归并引入解耦置信度，为双目深度学习VO提供了有效且泛化性强的方案。代码将开源。

Abstract: Deep learning-based visual odometry (VO) has achieved significant progress, yet most existing methods focus on a monocular approach, which suffers from scale ambiguity. Stereo VO provides real metric by its nature, but remains less studied in deep learning VO due to its high computational cost and modeling complexity. Recent advances in stereo matching and optical flow estimation have made dense visual correspondence increasingly accurate and reliable, but their complementary geometric information has not been fully exploited for VO. In this paper, we present SFVO, a correspondence-driven stereo VO framework that directly builds upon pretrained stereo matching and optical flow models. SFVO exploits pretrained stereo matching and optical flow models to estimate stereo and temporal correspondences. Instead of learning pose directly from images, SFVO maps learned correspondences into geometric constraints and predicts which points are trustworthy. To improve the reliability of visual correspondence-based geometric constraints, we introduce decoupled confidence maps for rotation and translation. This design better aligns the characteristics of visual correspondence and 6-DoF transformations. Extensive experiments on outdoor and indoor datasets demonstrate that SFVO achieves robust and accurate pose estimation with strong generalization capability. The code will be released.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond Benchmark Scores: Auditing Medical Vision-Language Models for Chest X-Ray Tuberculosis Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21763)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer,Mohd. Arshad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究对3个医学视觉语言模型与1个通用对照模型在4个数据集、12,200例胸片、5类提示家族（244,000条评分）上进行多维度稳健性审计，发现排名、可靠性、阈值保持与筛查性能均不具一致的跨设定可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数并不能保证结论在改变评估设定后仍成立，需检验模型排名、分数可靠性与筛查性能对队列、提示、负样本谱、患病率设定和操作阈值的敏感性。

Method: 审计BioMedCLIP、CheXficient、MedSigLIP及通用域OpenCLIP对照；数据来自Montgomery、Shenzhen、TBX11K、VinDr-CXR共12,200条胸片记录；采用5个固定提示家族生成244,000条模型-图像-提示分数；进行多重性控制比较，并系统变动队列、负样本谱（健康对照 vs 患病的非结核对照）、指定患病率与操作阈值。

Result: 无模型在所有队列与可靠性标准上领先；提示家族变化在48项多重性控制比较中有21项改变AUROC；用患病非结核对照替换健康对照使四个模型AUROC下降0.075–0.306；VinDr-CXR上医学模型对结核 vs 无异常对照的区分显著优于对肺炎或肺肿瘤，且对两种具名疾病的AUROC点估计均低于0.5；CheXficient存在VinDr-CXR预训练暴露，限制其结果解释；为TBX11K训练集95%灵敏度选定的阈值在16项目标评估中仅4项按点估计保持该约束；五种子监督源模型在TBX11K验证集AUROC达0.999，但在两个外部队列均仅0.629，保守排除感知重叠候选可缩小但未消除差距。

Conclusion: 这些回顾性、单任务结果表明区分能力、分数可靠性与阈值保持支持不同的可迁移性主张；胸片结核筛查证据应完整报告评估设定，而非将临床可迁移性单独归因于某一检查点权重。

Abstract: A medical model's benchmark score does not establish that the same conclusion holds under a different evaluation. This study tests whether claims about model ranking, score reliability and screening performance survive changes in cohort, prompt, negative spectrum, specified prevalence and operating threshold. We audit three medical vision-language models (BioMedCLIP, CheXficient, and MedSigLIP) and a general-domain OpenCLIP comparator on 12,200 chest radiograph records from four datasets (Montgomery, Shenzhen, TBX11K, and VinDr-CXR). Five fixed prompt families yield 244,000 model--image--prompt scores. No model leads every cohort and reliability criterion. Prompt-family changes alter AUROC in 21 of 48 multiplicity-controlled comparisons. Replacing healthy controls with sick non-tuberculosis controls reduces AUROC by 0.075--0.306 across all four models. On VinDr-CXR, the three medical models distinguish tuberculosis from no-finding controls substantially better than from pneumonia or lung tumor; their AUROC point estimates for both named diseases fall below 0.5. CheXficient has documented VinDr-CXR pretraining exposure, which limits the interpretation of its results. Thresholds chosen for 95\% sensitivity on TBX11K training retain that constraint by point estimate in only four of sixteen target evaluations. A five-seed supervised source model reaches 0.999 AUROC on TBX11K validation but 0.629 on each of two external cohorts. Conservative exclusion of perceptual-overlap candidates narrows this gap without closing it. These retrospective, single-task results show that discrimination, score reliability and threshold retention support different portability claims. Evidence for chest X-ray tuberculosis screening should identify the complete evaluation specification rather than attribute clinical portability to a checkpoint alone.

</details>


### [110] [XCalib Depth-Guided Geometric Optimization for Dense Thermal-Visible Video Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21770)
*Aurelien Godet,Gabriel Jobert,Mauro Dalla Mura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XCalib，一种无监督的稠密热-可见光图像配准框架，将虚拟针孔相机参数化作为几何约束空间，联合优化相对位姿、内参与单目度量深度，并引入归一化边缘相关性（NEC）相似度度量，在ADAS数据集上取得优于无约束稠密光流基线的时间稳定性与对齐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态感知（如图像融合、目标检测、语义分割）依赖图像配准作为预处理。ADAS中可见光与红外相机因光轴不重合及视场差异产生非均匀视差与视觉重影。经典关键点方法局限于全局单应，难以应对动态深度；无约束稠密光流缺乏结构正则化，导致时间不稳定。

Method: 提出XCalib无监督稠密热-可见光配准框架。不做绝对度量标定，而是将虚拟针孔相机参数化严格用作几何约束空间：通过联合优化有效相对位姿、内参与预测的单目度量深度，将空间位移搜索空间限制在物理有效的投影几何内；并提出面向跨光谱对齐的鲁棒结构相似度度量——归一化边缘相关性（NEC）。

Result: 在多个公开ADAS数据集上进行定量与定性评估，结果显示XCalib在时间稳定性和对齐精度上均优于无约束稠密光流基线。

Conclusion: XCalib以相机参数化作为稠密跨模态形变的隐式正则化，弥合了全局单应与无约束稠密光流之间的空白，为热-可见光配准提供了一种物理合理且时间稳定的方案。

Abstract: Image registration is a vital preprocessing step in multimodal perception tasks, including image fusion, object detection, and semantic segmentation. In Advanced Driver- Assistance Systems (ADAS), spatial misalignment between visible (RGB) and infrared (IR) cameras -caused by non-coincident optical axes and field-of-view differences- introduces non-uniform parallax and visual ghosting. Classical keypoint-based methods are restricted to global homographies that fail under dynamic depth, while unconstrained dense flow algorithms lack structural regularization and suffer from temporal instability. In this paper, we propose XCalib, an unsupervised dense thermal-visible registration framework that bridges this gap. Rather than serving as an absolute metric calibration tool, XCalib leverages virtual pinhole camera parameterization strictly as a geometric constraint space. By optimizing effective relative pose and intrinsics alongside predicted monocular metric depth, XCalib restricts the search space of spatial displacements to physically valid projection geometries. Our key contributions are: (1) a novel registration paradigm that uses camera parameterization as an implicit regularizer for dense cross-modal warping; (2) Normalized Edges Correlation (NEC), a robust structural similarity metric tailored to cross- spectral alignment; and (3) extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations across public ADAS datasets, demonstrating superior temporal stability and alignment accuracy over unconstrained dense flow baselines.

</details>


### [111] [PointLAM: Local Attentive Mamba for Efficient Point-based 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21780)
*Xuanming Shang,Weijia Zhang,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PointLAM高效点云3D检测架构，通过Laplacian Point Sampler与Local Hadamard Aggregator两大创新解决点基方法的降采样与局部建模瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR 3D目标检测存在根本性两难：体素方法高效但引入几何量化误差，点基方法保真度高却受限于严重的计算瓶颈。具体而言，点基架构受困于缓慢的降采样策略（如FPS）、昂贵的动态邻域查询（如k-NN）以及高开销的连续特征交互。因此需要一种既高效又保留几何保真度的点基架构。

Method: PointLAM由两项协同创新驱动：1）Laplacian Point Sampler（LPS）：采用隐式离散拉普拉斯高通滤波器与双重排序采样（Doubly Sorted Sampling），实现快速、结构感知的前景保留，解决降采样瓶颈；2）Local Hadamard Aggregator（LHA）：利用瞬态网格（transient grids）将空间索引与特征表示解耦，并以Hadamard门控机制替代复杂连续交互，实现拓扑感知的注意力调制。将局部门控与Bi-Directional Mamba（BDM）层耦合用于全局序列建模，构成Local Attentive Mamba（LAM）模块。

Result: PointLAM在nuScenes与Waymo数据集上取得点基检测器的竞争性性能，可与高度优化的体素方法相媲美，同时仅需其一小部分计算开销，并在小目标检测与极端稀疏场景下展现出显著优势。

Conclusion: PointLAM通过LPS与LHA两项协同设计，有效缓解了点基3D检测在降采样和局部建模上的系统性问题，在保持几何保真度的同时实现了高效率与强性能，证明点基架构可在精度与计算成本之间取得优于体素方法的平衡。

Abstract: 3D object detection from LiDAR point clouds faces a fundamental dilemma: voxel-based methods achieve efficiency at the cost of geometric quantization, while point-based methods preserve fidelity but suffer from prohibitive computational bottlenecks. Specifically, point-based architectures are crippled by slow downsampling strategies (e.g., FPS) and expensive dynamic neighbor queries (e.g., k-NN) coupled with costly continuous interactions. To tackle these systemic inefficiencies, we propose PointLAM, a highly efficient and powerful point-based architecture driven by two synergistic innovations. First, to resolve the downsampling bottleneck, we develop the Laplacian Point Sampler (LPS). LPS employs an implicit discrete Laplacian high-pass filter and Doubly Sorted Sampling to achieve fast, structure-aware foreground preservation. Second, to overcome local modeling latency, we design the Local Hadamard Aggregator (LHA). LHA decouples spatial indexing from feature representation using transient grids, and replaces complex continuous interactions with a Hadamard Gating mechanism for topology-aware, attentive modulation. By coupling this local gating with Bi-Directional Mamba (BDM) layers for global sequence modeling, we formulate the Local Attentive Mamba (LAM) block. Powered by this architecture, PointLAM achieves competitive performance on nuScenes and Waymo for point-based detectors. It rivals highly optimized voxel competitors while requiring a fraction of the computational footprint, demonstrating marked superiority in detecting small instances and handling extreme sparsity. Project page: https://pointlam.github.io/.

</details>


### [112] [A Principled Approach to Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21800)
*James Myles,Matthew Baugh,Johanna P. Müller,Bernhard Kainz,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将无监督异常检测(UAD)重构为贝叶斯逆问题，通过推断最可能的损坏机制实现异常检测并给出概率性异常分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统UAD方法仅标记或定位偏离正常分布的样本，忽略了异常的生成机制，而异常的性质往往与其存在同样重要。

Method: 将UAD重构为贝叶斯逆问题，目标是推断每次观测最可能的损坏；以推断出的损坏参数的能量作为概率性异常分数，并将多种现有方法推导为该框架在不同建模选择下的实例。

Result: 在受控环境下研究框架组件，通过调整底层损坏模型将MVTec AD数据集上的物体类别AUROC提升2.3%；在脑部MRI基准上验证，取得强检测性能并同时估计病理强度、偏置和几何。

Conclusion: 该框架为开发新UAD算法提供了原则性配方，统一了现有方法，并能推断异常的生成机制，代码已开源。

Abstract: Traditional unsupervised anomaly detection (UAD) methods are designed to flag or localise deviations from a normative distribution, ignoring the underlying generative mechanisms of the anomalies. Yet the nature of an anomaly is often as important as its presence. We reformulate UAD as a Bayesian inverse problem, in which the objective is to infer the most probable corruption responsible for each observation. Our framework yields a probabilistic anomaly score as the energy of the inferred corruption parameters, and serves as a principled recipe for developing new UAD algorithms. We derive several existing methods as instances of the general framework, each corresponding to the same energy score under different modelling choices. Experimentally, we study the framework's components in a controlled setting, and improve object-class AUROC on the MVTec AD dataset by 2.3% by adapting the underlying corruption model. Finally, we validate the framework on a brain MRI benchmark, achieving strong detection performance while producing estimates of pathology intensity, bias, and geometry. Code is available at https://github.com/jgmyles/inverse-uad.

</details>


### [113] [VideoReloc: Long-Term Indoor Video Relocalization against a Kilobyte-Scale Semantic Scene Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21804)
*Qianru Li,Xuyang Chen,Xuqin Wang,Zhenghao Zhang,Hongyi Luo,Tao Wu,Daniel Cremers,Lu Liu,Yanfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoReloc 将稀疏语义地图下的室内长时视频重定位重构为"空间延展视频查询的验证"问题，通过自适应片段、运行级决策与假设优先配准，在 100 kB 类别框地图上显著超越基于外观的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时室内视频重定位需应对光照与家具变动，纯视觉方法依赖外观因而失效；而逐帧仅凭物体类别与几何定位又会得到稀疏且歧义的证据，二者难以兼顾鲁棒性与精度。

Method: 提出 VideoReloc：1) 自适应片段利用里程计累积空间证据，直至满足物体与运动判据，使查询长度随场景自适应；2) 运行级决策基于连通片段累积的证据复查冲突位姿，稳定轨迹；3) 假设优先配准由物体三元组提出候选位姿，并用片段级物体中心与框表面验证；4) 朝向感知优化利用框面、重力与墙方向消解相机朝向歧义并精化全位姿。

Result: 在 RIO10 与 ReplicaCAD 上，1 m/10° 的全帧定位成功率在因果评估下为 73.5% 与 61.1%，加入片段闭合后升至 90.6% 与 74.8%；对比的逐帧场景坐标回归器仅为 47.6% 与 49.8%，且需 12.6–42 MB 地图。

Conclusion: 将判别性支撑从存储外观转移到时序上下文，使 100 kB 的类别标注框地图即可实现稳定长时重定位，验证了视频查询验证范式的有效性。

Abstract: Given a compact semantic scene graph, long-term indoor video relocalization estimates a map-frame trajectory after lighting and furniture changes. Visual methods rely on appearance and become unreliable under these changes; localizing one frame at a time from object classes and geometry instead leaves sparse, ambiguous evidence. We introduce VideoReloc, whose adaptive clips use odometry to gather spatial evidence until object and motion criteria are met, adapting query length to the observed scene. Its run-level decision rechecks conflicting placements using evidence accumulated across connected clips, stabilizing the trajectory beyond adjacent-clip tracking. Hypothesis-first registration proposes poses from object triplets and verifies each using clip-wide object centers and box surfaces. Orientation-aware refinement uses box faces, gravity and wall directions to resolve ambiguity in camera orientation and refine the full pose. This reframes sparse-map relocalization as verification of spatially extended video queries, moving discriminative support from stored appearance to temporal context and permitting a 100 kB map of class-labelled boxes. On RIO10 and ReplicaCAD, the all-frame localization success rate at 1 m/10$^\circ$ is 73.5% and 61.1% under causal evaluation, rising to 90.6% and 74.8% with clip closure. The evaluated per-frame scene coordinate regressors reach up to 47.6% and 49.8%, respectively, with maps of 12.6-42 MB. Project page: https://videoreloc.github.io

</details>


### [114] [Object Detection Benchmarks are Incomplete: The Role of Label Errors and Annotation Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21822)
*Sarina Penquitt,Jonathan Klees,Antonia van Betteray,Parssa Jashnieh,Peter Stehr,Matthias Rottmann,Lars Schmarje*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出目标检测基准的质量瓶颈在于标注不完整，通过重标注揭示大量漏标，并提出高召回、软标签的可扩展标注流程与两个新基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测虽在架构与开放词汇模型上不断进步，但基准质量受限于标注不完整（漏标）。作者希望量化漏标问题的严重程度，并推动评估范式从确定性标注转向高召回、不确定性感知的评价。

Method: 在 COCO、Pascal VOC、Cityscapes、KITTI 四个数据集上进行重标注，分析标注增益与错误来源；提出强调高召回的可扩展标注流程，对每个目标聚合至少 11 位标注者的软标签以捕捉歧义；构建两个大规模基准：不确定性感知目标检测基准与基于真实标注错误的标签错误检测基准。

Result: 重标注后标注目标显著增加（KITTI 最高 +60%，COCO +40%），主要来自此前未标注的小目标、遮挡目标或密集目标；漏标是所有数据集标签错误的主要来源。基准性能对标注质量高度敏感，但模型排名大体稳定。现有检测器强依赖标注质量且与人类感知不一致；在合成噪声上表现良好的标签错误检测方法在真实标签错误上难以取得高召回与高精度。

Conclusion: 未来目标检测基准应超越确定性标注，转向高召回、不确定性感知的评估方式，以最大化有效实例并更好反映现实世界的歧义性。

Abstract: While object detection has advanced through improved architectures and open-vocabulary models, we provide strong evidence that benchmark quality is limited by annotation incompleteness. Across four widely used datasets (COCO, Pascal VOC, Cityscapes, KITTI), re-annotation reveals substantial increases in annotated objects (e.g., up to +60% on KITTI and +40% on COCO), driven primarily by previously unlabeled small, occluded, or densely packed instances. While some differences arise from dataset-specific annotation conventions, we consistently find that missing annotations are the main source of label errors across all datasets. To achieve high data quality, we introduce a scalable annotation pipeline that emphasizes high recall and captures ambiguity through soft labels aggregated from at least 11 annotators per object. The resulting annotations improve coverage and align well with human calibration. We show that benchmark performance is highly sensitive to annotation quality, although model rankings remain largely stable. We introduce two large-scale benchmarks: (i) an uncertainty-aware object detection benchmark, and (ii) a label error detection benchmark grounded in real label errors. We show that current detectors are strongly depended on annotation quality and are misaligned with human perception. Current label error detection methods, which have been shown to perform well on synthetic noise, struggle to achieve high recall and precision on real label errors. Our results highlight the need for future object detection benchmarks to move beyond deterministic annotations toward high-recall, uncertainty-aware evaluation that maximizes valid instances and better reflects real-world ambiguity.

</details>


### [115] [Morphology-Aware Ambiguity Learning for Wafer Defect Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21866)
*Seungjun Chu,Seokhyun Chung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出形态学感知的模糊性学习框架，将晶圆图缺陷识别从固定单标签分类扩展为三种诊断动作，并在WM-811K上验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晶圆图缺陷识别被表述为固定分类任务，将每片晶圆强制归为单一缺陷类别；但部分晶圆形态处于类别边界，强行给出单一预测信息量不足，不如提供合理的备选诊断。

Method: 基于训练晶圆图的径向、角度和几何特征，构建类级模糊矩阵，表示形态相似、可互为合理诊断备选的缺陷类对；用该矩阵引导模型学习合理的备选类，而非同等对待所有错误类；推理时由矩阵判断不确定预测能否用有意义的两类诊断集表示，否则升级为全复核。支持三种诊断动作：自动单类诊断、两类备选辅助诊断、全复核。

Result: 在WM-811K数据集上，该框架在缺陷识别和诊断决策支持上优于常规方法，能给出有意义的两类备选，并将全复核保留给模糊性未解决的案例；示例性成本分析显示该路由策略具有潜在成本优势；诊断行为在不同骨干网络架构下保持一致。

Conclusion: 形态学感知的模糊性学习框架可有效提升晶圆图缺陷识别的诊断支持能力，兼顾准确性、可解释性与成本，且对骨干架构不敏感。

Abstract: Wafer map defect recognition is commonly formulated as a fixed-taxonomy classification problem that assigns each wafer to a single defect class. However, some wafers exhibit morphologies near class boundaries, for which forcing a single prediction may be less informative than providing plausible diagnostic alternatives. This paper proposes a morphology-aware ambiguity learning framework that supports three diagnostic actions: automatic single-class diagnosis, assisted diagnosis with two plausible defect classes, and full review. Using the radial, angular, and geometric characteristics of training wafer maps, the framework constructs a class-level ambiguity matrix representing defect-class pairs with similar morphology and plausible diagnostic alternatives. It guides the model to learn plausible alternative classes rather than treating all incorrect classes equally. During inference, the matrix determines whether an uncertain prediction can be represented by a meaningful two-class diagnostic set or should be escalated for full review. Experiments on WM-811K show that the proposed framework outperforms conventional approaches in defect recognition and diagnostic decision support, providing meaningful two-class alternatives while reserving full review for cases with unresolved ambiguity. Illustrative cost analyses further show the potential cost advantage of the proposed routing strategy. The diagnostic behavior of the framework remains consistent across different backbone architectures.

</details>


### [116] [Chronosphere: Space-Time Tessellation of Local Climate Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21872)
*Daniel Cher,Eric Xing,Kexing Li,Brian Wei,Isaac Corley,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Chronosphere，一种时空神经场，用于学习气候表征。它通过自适应时空环面上的可学习站点镶嵌与共享局部基函数库，实现容量分配的分辨率自适应，在重建气候学任务中匹配或超越现有位置编码器，迁移场景增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理表征学习的核心难题在于环境过程的时空复杂度差异极大，而现有位置编码器通常在全空间固定单一细节层级：球谐等全局基函数均匀分配容量，局部基仅覆盖预定义区域，学习型镶嵌虽能自适应但难以高效表征高频信息。

Method: Chronosphere将可学习站点构成的自适应镶嵌定义在时空环面 $S^2\times S^1$ 上，并与共享的局部基函数库相结合；容量放置位置与各区域细节量均可随数据在空间和时间上自适应调整。模型以重建气候学为训练目标。

Result: 在空间与时间任务上，Chronosphere匹配或领先当前最先进的位置编码器，且在最需要自适应的时空迁移设定下取得最大增益。

Conclusion: 统一全局与局部基的时空自适应表示框架能有效应对气候过程的非均匀复杂度，并在跨时空泛化中展现优势。

Abstract: We introduce Chronosphere, a spatio-temporal neural field that learns representations of climate. A central challenge in geographic representation learning is modeling environmental processes whose spatial and temporal complexity varies widely. Yet existing location encoders typically fix a single level of detail everywhere. Global bases such as spherical harmonics spread capacity uniformly across space and time. Localized bases resolve only predefined regions. Learned tessellations adapt, but are inefficient at representing higher frequencies. Chronosphere unifies these approaches, pairing an adaptive tessellation of learnable sites on the spacetime torus $S^2\times S^1$ with a shared bank of local basis functions. Both where capacity is placed and how much detail each region carries adapt to the data, across space and time. Trained to reconstruct climatology, Chronosphere matches or leads state-of-the-art location encoders across spatial and temporal tasks, with the largest gains under spatial and temporal transfer.

</details>


### [117] [Benchmarking the Explanatory Quality of Open-Weight Vision-Language Models in Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21879)
*Laurent Colbois,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向VLM人脸识别的基准框架，将解释质量作为核心评估维度，定义相关性与忠实性两项标准，并构建结构化输出方法实现量化评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM可用于人脸识别并生成自然语言解释，在法庭等取证场景中人脸比对要求决策透明可审计。但现有评估多关注识别准确率，生成解释的有效性尚未被量化。

Method: 提出基准框架，将解释质量作为核心评估维度。定义两项标准：相关性（依赖身份稳定的面部特征）与忠实性（与可见图像内容一致、无幻觉特征）。通过约束模型输出为结构化解释格式，支持自动化查询与审计，从而量化相关性与忠实性。据此对多个开放权重VLM系列进行基准测试，联合评估人脸验证准确率与解释质量。

Result: 基准测试揭示了所生成解释仍存不足，说明仅看准确率无法全面反映模型性能，需引入解释质量指标。所提基准与开源评测工具为可解释人脸识别系统的规范评测与后续微调提供了基础。

Conclusion: 解释质量应成为VLM人脸识别的核心评估轴；提出的相关性与忠实性指标及开源harness为可解释人脸识别系统的规范评测和未来微调奠定基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have recently been proposed as promising tools for face recognition, as they can produce natural language explanations alongside similarity scores. This capability is considered appealing for face comparisons in forensic contexts, which require decisions to be transparent and auditable. However, existing evaluations of VLMs for that use case focus mostly on recognition accuracy, while the validity of generated explanations remains unquantified. In this work, we introduce a benchmarking framework for VLM-based face recognition that treats explanation quality as a core evaluation axis. We propose two criteria that explanations should satisfy: relevance, i.e., reliance on identity-stable facial features; and faithfulness, i.e., alignment with the visible image content without hallucinated features. We jointly develop a methodology enabling the quantification of relevance and faithfulness of evaluated models, based on constraining model outputs to a structured explanation format that supports automated querying and auditing. Using this framework, we benchmark several families of open-weight VLMs, jointly evaluating face verification accuracy and explanation quality. Our results highlight remaining shortcomings of produced explanations, and emphasize the need for such explanation quality metrics to get a complete picture of model performance. The proposed benchmark and open-source evaluation harness provide a foundation for proper benchmarking and future fine-tuning of explainable face recognition systems.

</details>


### [118] [Catena: A Comprehensive Software Suite for Large-Scale Connectomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21887)
*Samia Mohinta,Pedro Gómez-Gálvez,Shi Yan Lee,Daniel Franco-Barranco,Michael Clayton,Stephan Preibisch,Jan Funke,Albert Cardona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Catena是一个面向连接组学的开源、开发者中心的软件套件，集成神经元与细胞器分割、突触检测、微管追踪与神经递质推断等模块，以可组合的分块流水线、预训练模型和容器化运行时实现可复现、可扩展的电子显微镜连接组图谱构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子显微镜密集标注神经组织体积是连接组图谱绘制的金标准数据，但重建、校对神经元树突/轴突及标注突触需要串联多个软件工具，这些工具往往碎片化、维护不一致或为专有软件，阻碍了可复现性与自动化。因此需要一套整合、开放且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 提出Catena软件套件：1）集成分割（3D神经元与细胞器）、突触检测、微管追踪和神经递质推断等模块；2）采用可组合、分块（chunk-wise）的处理流水线，设计完全文档化、可扩展、可适配；3）通过预训练机器学习模型降低算力与真值数据需求，便于微调；4）模块完全容器化，封装依赖以保证在工作站与集群上执行一致。

Result: Catena整合了开放组件、可共享模型与容器化运行时，提供从电子显微镜体积映射细胞连接组的可复现、可扩展方法；并附带代码与文档（GitHub仓库）。

Conclusion: 通过统一开放组件、可共享模型和容器化运行环境，Catena克服了连接组学工具碎片化、专有化带来的复现与自动化障碍，为基于电子显微镜的连接组图谱绘制提供了可复现且可扩展的途径。

Abstract: The gold standard datasets for mapping connectomes are electron microscopy volumes of densely labeled neural tissue at nanometer resolution. Yet reconstructing and proofreading neuronal arbors and annotating all synapses requires pipelining multiple software tools that are often fragmented, inconsistently maintained, or proprietary, hindering reproducibility and automation. Here, we introduce Catena, an open-source, comprehensive, developer-centric software suite for connectomics that integrates modules for 3D neuron and organelle segmentation, synapse detection, microtubule tracking, and neurotransmitter inference. Catena organizes its modules in composable, chunk-wise processing pipelines in a completely documented, extensible, and adaptable design. We further reduce compute and ground-truth data requirements with pretrained machine learning models, facilitating fine-tuning. Catena ships fully containerized modules that encapsulate evolving dependencies for consistent execution across workstations and clusters. By consolidating open components, shareable models, and containerized runtimes, Catena delivers a reproducible and scalable approach to mapping cellular connectomes from electron microscopy volumes. Code and documentation: https://github.com/Mohinta2892/catena.git

</details>


### [119] [The Role of Radiometric Features in Cross-Site Leaf-Wood Segmentation of LiDAR Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21903)
*Roman Kaharlytskyi,Derek T. Robinson,Roberto Guglielmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战现有叶-木质分割方法排除辐射特征的做法，通过跨站点、跨平台的泛化实验证明：辐射特征（强度、回波次数等）比几何特征具有更强的跨传感器、跨环境泛化能力，并显著提升木质召回率与结构连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叶-木质分割是定量结构模型（QSM）与非破坏性生物量估计的关键步骤。现有分割方法通常刻意排除强度、回波次数等辐射特征，以最大化跨传感器兼容性。作者质疑这一设计选择，认为该假设缺乏跨站点、跨平台泛化能力的实证检验。

Method: 采用跨站点、跨平台泛化评估协议：在公开的 Heidelberg 数据集（地基 TLS，1550nm）上训练，在加拿大安大略省的新数据集（RPA-LS，905nm，稀疏的俯视航测几何）上测试。对比仅用几何特征的模型（包括在高密度 LiDAR 数据集上训练的 SOTA 深度学习模型）与加入辐射特征（强度、回波次数、回波总数）的模型，并分析分割结果的结构连通性。

Result: 仅几何特征方法在稀疏俯视航测数据上泛化失败，F1 ≤ 0.56；引入辐射特征后 F1 提升至 0.61，且木质召回率从 0.16 提升到 0.35，增幅达 119%。此外，仅几何方法常产生破碎的树干和枝条组件，而利用辐射特征可保持更好的结构连通性，得到更连贯的树体结构，更有利于 QSM 重建。

Conclusion: 几何模式具有视角依赖性，容易过拟合特定扫描模式；辐射特征编码的是物理材质属性，能在不同传感器和环境之间泛化。因此排除辐射特征的设计选择值得重新审视，辐射特征对跨平台叶-木质分割和 QSM 重建具有重要价值。

Abstract: Leaf-wood segmentation of individual trees from LiDAR point clouds is essential for quantitative structure models (QSMs) used in non-destructive biomass estimation. Existing segmentation methods typically exclude radiometric features (e.g., intensity, return number) to maximize cross-sensor compatibility. We challenge this design choice by evaluating cross-site and cross-platform generalization: training on the public Heidelberg dataset (terrestrial TLS, 1550nm) and testing on a novel dataset from Ontario, Canada (RPA-LS, 905nm). Results show that geometry-only methods - including state-of-the-art deep learning models trained on high-density LiDAR datasets - fail to generalize to the sparse, top-down geometry of aerial scans, achieving F1 scores <= 0.56. Incorporating radiometric features (intensity, return number, number of returns) improves F1 to 0.61, but more critically, increases wood recall by 119% from 0.16 to 0.35. Furthermore, geometry-only approaches often result in fragmented stem and branch components. We find that leveraging radiometric features preserves greater structural connectivity, resulting in more coherent architectures that are better suited for QSM reconstruction. We demonstrate that while geometric patterns are view-dependent and prone to overfitting scan patterns, radiometric features encode physical material properties that generalize across disparate sensors and environments.

</details>


### [120] [Info3R: Information-Adaptive Test-Time Training for 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21938)
*Sunghyun Baek,Hanna Bae,Minchan Kwon,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出信息自适应的在线测试时训练方法 Info3R，通过信息感知的状态更新与动态状态重置，提升长序列在线 3D 重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Transformer 的在线视频 3D 重建方法在处理长图像流时忽略两个关键信号：每帧输入的重要性，以及模型内部状态的信息饱和度（饱和后状态可塑性下降，难以吸收新观测）。

Method: 提出 Info3R 信息自适应测试时训练方法。包含：(1) 信息感知状态更新，依据每帧的冗余度与信息量调节状态更新强度；(2) 动态状态重置，由状态更新的累积幅度与模型预测置信度触发，并配合 anchor-to-world 对齐，以恢复状态可塑性。

Result: 在相机位姿估计、视频深度估计与 3D 重建任务上取得一致提升，显著缓解长序列性能退化；在 KITTI Odometry 上平均 ATE 比 LongStream 低 1.68 倍。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模帧信息量与状态饱和，可有效维持在线重建模型在长序列上的鲁棒性，验证了信息自适应状态管理与重置机制的有效性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have recently achieved strong performance on 3D reconstruction from images, and recent works extend them to process video streams in an online manner for real-world deployment. However, existing methods overlook two key signals when handling long image streams: the importance of each incoming frame and the information saturation of the model's internal state. In this paper, we propose Info3R, a novel information-adaptive test-time training method for the online 3D reconstruction. We introduce an information-aware state update that modulates the state update strength based on the redundancy and informativeness of each incoming frame. To restore the state's plasticity -- its capacity to incorporate new observations -- we propose a dynamic state reset, triggered by the cumulative magnitude of state updates and the model's prediction confidence and accompanied by an anchor-to-world alignment. Our method achieves consistent improvements on camera pose estimation, video depth estimation, and 3D reconstruction, while substantially mitigating the performance degradation in the long sequence evaluation. Notably, on KITTI Odometry, our method achieves on average 1.68x lower ATE than LongStream, demonstrating its robustness on extended outdoor sequences.

</details>


### [121] [PRIME: Perception Feedback with Situational Memory Embeddings in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22040)
*Erik Deinzer,Naya Baslan,Luca Paparusso,Narunas Vaskevicius,Peter Knott,Luigi Palmieri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出PRIME，一种学习式反馈机制，通过情境记忆让VLA感知查询受下游推理与导航目标调节，在Bench2Drive上取得SOTA驾驶得分82.47。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶VLA模型主要沿感知—推理—规划层级进行前馈推理，早期感知对下游推理与导航目标不敏感，无法根据先前决策优先处理相关视觉线索，感知与推理之间存在割裂。

Method: PRIME引入情境记忆（Situational Memory），通过交叉注意力聚合L步窗口内过去感知、推理、导航目标与预测行为的潜在表示，以意图驱动的方式调节VLA感知查询，实现学习式反馈，仅增加最多29.7M参数（占7.3B基座模型的0.41%）。

Result: 在Bench2Drive闭环基准上，PRIME取得SOTA驾驶得分82.47（较ORION提升4.73），成功率60.00%（提升5.38个百分点），为已发表、基于Think2Drive演示训练的VLA中最高驾驶得分。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级学习式反馈机制将情境记忆注入感知查询，可有效实现意图驱动的感知注意力，在极低参数开销下显著提升闭环驾驶性能。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving operate primarily through feedforward inference across the perception--reasoning--planning hierarchy. While modern architectures maintain temporal recurrence within the perceptual module, early perception remains blind to downstream reasoning and navigation goals, processing visual inputs agnostically without prioritizing cues informed by prior decisions. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces PRIME, a learned feedback mechanism that conditions the VLA perceptual queries on a novel Situational Memory. By aggregating latent representations of past perception, reasoning, navigation goals, and predicted behaviors across an L-step window via cross-attention, PRIME enables intent-driven perceptual attention at minimal computational cost, adding only a maximum of 29.7M parameters (0.41% of the 7.3B-parameter base model). Evaluated on the Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark, PRIME achieves a state-of-the-art Driving Score of 82.47 (+4.73 over ORION) and a Success Rate of 60.00% (+5.38 percentage points), the highest reported Driving Score among published VLAs trained on Think2Drive demonstrations.

</details>


### [122] [Traffic Sign Recognition for Autonomous Driving Using Branched YOLOv2 and Geometric Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22060)
*Arefeh Rezaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于YOLOv2的交通标志识别系统，通过分支预测层实现早期推理以降低计算量，并引入几何模板匹配抑制相似标志的误分类，在自建GTSDB+GTSRB数据集上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通标志识别需同时高效完成标志定位与语义分类，是自动驾驶和高级驾驶辅助系统的关键感知任务。现有方法在计算效率与相似标志区分能力上仍有改进空间，故需在YOLOv2基础上兼顾推理加速与分类精度提升。

Method: 研究两项互补改进：(1)为YOLOv2添加中间预测层构成分支架构，支持对简单样本提前终止推理，并比较整图分支与逐单元分支两种策略；(2)引入几何信息，采用无监督贝叶斯图像分割生成二值表示，在YOLOv2边界框内与类别特定几何模板比对，用于推理阶段或作为训练辅助信号。数据集由GTSDB与GTSRB经无缝克隆和受控图像变换构建，含10类共3,000训练样本与300测试样本。

Result: 选定分支架构运行时间0.647 s、mAP 0.680；基线YOLOv2为0.6607 s、mAP 0.680。推理阶段几何验证将mAP提升至0.713；几何特征训练变体达到0.697 mAP，运行时间0.6608 s。

Conclusion: 分支架构在保持与基线相当精度的同时略微降低推理时间，而几何信息的引入显著提升分类准确率，其中推理阶段几何验证效果最佳，表明几何模板匹配是区分视觉相似交通标志的有效补充手段。

Abstract: Traffic sign recognition (TSR) is an important perception task for autonomous driving and advanced driver-assistance systems, where a system must both localize traffic signs and determine their semantic classes efficiently. This work presents a TSR system based on YOLOv2 for simultaneous detection and classification. Two complementary modifications are studied. First, YOLOv2 is extended with intermediate prediction layers, forming a branched architecture that can terminate inference early for easy cases and reduce computation time. Both whole-image and cell-wise branching strategies are investigated. Second, geometric information is introduced to reduce classification errors between visually similar signs. An unsupervised Bayesian image-segmentation method produces binary representations that are compared with class-specific geometric templates inside YOLOv2 bounding boxes. This information is used either during inference or as an additional signal during training. A dedicated dataset is constructed by combining GTSDB and GTSRB samples using seamless cloning and controlled image transformations. Experiments cover ten traffic-sign classes, with 3,000 training and 300 test samples. The selected branched architecture reports 0.647 s runtime and 0.680 mAP, compared with 0.6607 s and 0.680 mAP for baseline YOLOv2. Geometric verification during inference increases mAP to 0.713, while the geometric-feature training variant achieves 0.697 mAP with a reported runtime of 0.6608 s.

</details>


### [123] [OmniVBench: A Benchmark and Large-Scale Dataset for Omni Reference-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22069)
*Wenxue Li,Peiyan Guan,Haoyang Jiang,Junxian Cai,Hualuo Liu,Chunjie Zhang,Chong Guan,Kai Huang,Songlian Li,Taiyi Wu,Yongjian Yu,Xiaotong Zhao,Alan Zhao,Eric Liu,Xi Chen,Yu Liu,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出OmniVBench基准与Omni-R2V Dataset数据集，用于评测和训练全能参考到视频（omni R2V）生成模型，填补参考类型、控制任务与评测协议方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考到视频（R2V）生成正朝通用化、多能力方向发展，形成omni R2V范式。但现有基准存在两大不足：(1) 测试用例覆盖的参考类型与组合有限；(2) 评测协议主要考察整体参考一致性，忽视参考因子是否被正确保留、解耦与路由。同时，omni R2V训练数据构建成本高，导致训练资源稀缺。

Method: 提出OmniVBench与Omni-R2V Dataset。OmniVBench扩展参考类型、细粒度控制任务与参考组合，覆盖7个任务族、18项细粒度任务（涉及内容、运动、风格、结构、叙事及多参考设置）；引入因子锚定（factor-grounded）评测，含12,172条针对具体样本的检查清单，评估参考因子是否被忠实保留、正确解耦并绑定到目标、以及按指令正确实现。Omni-R2V Dataset基于大规模专业视频语料，含34万条处理后的训练样本，覆盖多样参考类型与多参考组合，并设计任务专用流水线构建参考-目标配对，提供可扩展的数据构建方案。

Result: 对先进开源与闭源R2V模型的广泛评测显示，在OmniVBench上各任务族与评测维度均存在明显性能差距，揭示当前R2V模型的局限。

Conclusion: OmniVBench与Omni-R2V Dataset为omni R2V生成提供了系统的评测基准与工业级训练资源，推动该领域向更通用、更细粒度的参考控制发展。

Abstract: Reference-to-video (R2V) generation is evolving toward increasingly general and versatile reference control, giving rise to the emerging paradigm of omni R2V generation. However, existing benchmarks fall short of these emerging capabilities: their test cases cover limited reference types and compositions, and their evaluation protocols largely assess holistic reference consistency, overlooking whether reference factors are properly preserved, disentangled, and routed. Meanwhile, the high cost of constructing omni R2V training data makes suitable training resources scarce. To address these gaps, we introduce OmniVBench and the Omni-R2V Dataset for evaluating and training omni R2V models. OmniVBench expands R2V evaluation across broader reference types, fine-grained control tasks, and richer reference compositions, covering 7 task families and 18 fine-grained tasks spanning content, motion, style, structure, narrative, and multi-reference settings. We introduce factor-grounded evaluation with 12,172 case-specific checklist items, assessing whether intended reference factors are faithfully preserved, correctly disentangled and bound to their targets, and properly realized according to the instruction. We further introduce the Omni-R2V Dataset, bringing industrial-grade training resources for diverse R2V tasks to the broader research community. Drawing primarily on a large-scale corpus of professional video footage, it comprises 340K processed training samples spanning diverse reference types and multi-reference compositions. We develop task-specific pipelines for reference-target pair construction, offering a practical and scalable recipe for omni R2V data construction. Extensive evaluation of advanced open- and closed-source R2V models reveals clear performance gaps across task families and evaluation dimensions on OmniVBench, highlighting remaining limitations of current R2V models.

</details>


### [124] [MintAct: A Unified Visual Agent for Digital Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22083)
*Mingfei Gao,Rui Tian,Haiming Gang,Bohan Zhai,Le Zhang,Yuanzheng Gong,Di Feng,Ege Özsoy,Kaixin Ma,Vishwesh Kirthivasan,Oğuzhan Fatih Kar,Roman Bachmann,Anders Boesen Lindbo Larsen,Afshin Dehghan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MintAct 是一系列规模为 2B/4B/8B 的视觉语言模型，统一了 UI grounding、跨移动端/桌面/网页的多步导航以及视觉工具使用能力，性能匹配各领域专用模型并在 OSWorld-Verified 上达到 48.9 的最新水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 UI 智能体能力通常被分割为领域专用模型，缺乏一个能同时覆盖 UI grounding、跨平台多步导航和视觉工具使用的统一模型；同时，训练这类模型需要可扩展的环境与强化学习基础设施。

Method: 通过精心设计环境、数据与训练配方，并构建可扩展的环境与强化学习基础设施：环境侧跨异构的按领域后端托管数百个并发实例，同时服务轨迹数据收集与在线 RL；训练侧采用异步框架，显式控制跨域训练分布，并在噪声环境反馈与离策略漂移下保持稳定。

Result: MintAct 在 2B、4B、8B 规模下于多项基准上达到同规模模型中的最先进性能，其中 OSWorld-Verified 得分为 48.9，并匹配各领域专用模型的性能。

Conclusion: 统一的多能力视觉语言模型可在多个 UI 智能体任务上媲美专用模型，表明统一建模与可扩展环境/RL 基础设施的结合是可行且有效的路线。

Abstract: We present MintAct, a family of vision-language models that unifies UI grounding, multi-step navigation across mobile, desktop, and web, and visual tool use, trained at 2B, 4B, and 8B scales. Through careful design of our environments, data, and training recipes, MintAct models match the performance of per-domain specialists across all of these capabilities. To enable this, we develop a scalable environment and reinforcement learning (RL) infrastructure. On the environment side, we host hundreds of concurrent instances across heterogeneous per-domain backends, serving both trajectory data collection and online RL. To enable efficient and scalable RL training, an asynchronous framework keeps explicit control over the cross-domain training distribution and remains stable under noisy environment feedback and off-policy drift. Experimental results show that MintAct achieves state-of-the-art performance (48.9 on OSWorld-Verified) across a wide range of benchmarks at comparable model sizes.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [The Right Tool for the Job: On the Selection of Mitigations for GenAI Privacy Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20884)
*Jonah Bellemans,Qianying Liao,Laurens Sion,Lieven Desmet,Wouter Joosen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 这是一篇立场论文，指出GenAI隐私工程的核心瓶颈不是威胁或缓解技术知识匮乏，而是二者之间缺乏衔接，并将其分解为三个子问题，提出四项建议及研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI已从实验技术演变为现代软件系统的基础组件，但其普及使敏感个人数据保护愈发困难。GenAI不仅放大传统隐私威胁，还引入新的推理型风险（如从看似无害的输入构建详细用户画像）。虽然隐私威胁建模框架开始以更细粒度刻画GenAI特有威胁，缓解技术也不断涌现，但问题空间与解空间基本独立发展，缺少连接二者的桥梁。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）方法：论证GenAI隐私工程的主要挑战在于威胁与缓解之间缺失的衔接，将该缺口分解为三个子问题——(i) 缺乏面向GenAI系统的细粒度威胁到缓解映射；(ii) 解空间假设在GenAI情境下不适用；(iii) 在GenAI约束下难以进行优先级排序。据此推导出四项面向未来缓解选择方法的建议，并提出一种扩展已有威胁到缓解映射方法以适配GenAI特有威胁特征的初步方案。

Result: 提出了三个子问题的分解框架、四项针对缓解选择方法的建议，以及一个扩展既有威胁到缓解映射方法以纳入GenAI特有威胁特征的候选方案，并勾勒出研究议程。

Conclusion: 面向GenAI系统的隐私缓解选择应更加系统化，关键在于构建威胁与缓解之间的细粒度映射桥梁，而非单纯积累威胁或缓解知识；未来研究应围绕该衔接展开。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) has rapidly evolved from an experimental technology into a foundational component of modern software systems. However, as its adoption grows, protecting sensitive personal data becomes increasingly challenging. Specifically, GenAI systems not only amplify traditional privacy threats but also introduce new inference-based risks, such as constructing detailed user profiles from seemingly harmless inputs. In response, privacy threat modeling frameworks are beginning to capture GenAI-specific privacy threats with finer granularity. At the same time, a growing number of mitigation techniques have been proposed to address these threats. However, although knowledge of both threats and mitigations continues to mature, the problem- and solution-space have developed largely independently.
  This position paper argues that the primary challenge in GenAI privacy engineering is not the lack of knowledge about privacy threats or mitigation techniques, but the missing bridge between them. We decompose this gap into three sub-problems: (i) lack of fine-grained threat-to-mitigation mapping for GenAI systems, (ii) inapplicable solution-space assumptions in the GenAI context, and (iii) prioritization difficulty under GenAI constraints. We derive four recommendations for future mitigation-selection approaches, and outline a suggested approach that extends established threat-to-mitigation mapping methods to GenAI-specific threat characteristics. We propose a research agenda toward more systematic privacy mitigation selection for GenAI-based systems.

</details>


### [126] [TPM-Attest: Hardware-Rooted Integrity Attestation as a Kernel-Level Anti-Cheat Alternative for Linux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20909)
*Anudeep Gedela,A. Yaswanth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 TPM-Attest：基于 TPM 2.0 与 Linux IMA 的硬件级远程证明框架，通过用户态 LD_PRELOAD 拦截 EOS SDK 调用、以绑定服务器 nonce 的 TPM quote 控制会话准入，并用带索引前缀的 Merkle 树组织 IMA 日志以防重复叶碰撞。500 次构造篡改会话检测率 100%，增量叶缓存使重复证明延迟低于 3 秒，红队测试阻断全部四种基于文件的攻击向量，并刻画两种已确认绕过条件。实现已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux 上多人 PC 游戏的作弊防护依赖专有 Ring 0 内核模块，其与 Linux 的安全模型、GPL 许可及稳定 ABI 保证在架构上不相容。作者主张不应将这些侵入式模块移植到 Linux，而应完全替换之，寻求无需内核驱动、无需专有代码、无需扫描玩家内存的替代方案。

Method: 1) 以 TPM 2.0 与 Linux IMA 为信任根，实现硬件支撑的远程证明，验证客户端干净启动且仅运行授权软件；2) 通过用户态 LD_PRELOAD 钩子拦截 EOS SDK 调用；3) 以绑定服务器下发 nonce 的实时 TPM quote 作为会话访问门控；4) 在 IMA 日志上构建带索引前缀的 Merkle 树，抵御重复叶碰撞攻击；5) 采用增量叶缓存降低重复证明延迟。

Result: 在 500 次构造篡改会话中检测率达 100%；增量叶缓存将重复证明延迟降至真实 TPM 2.0 硬件上 3 秒以内；针对实际演示游戏的红队评估确认四种基于文件的攻击向量全部被阻断，并精确刻画了两种已确认的绕过条件。

Conclusion: TPM-Attest 表明可用硬件级远程证明在不引入内核驱动与专有代码、不扫描内存的前提下替代 Linux 上的专有反作弊内核模块，并已开源全部实现；其局限在于仍存在两种已确认的绕过条件，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Multiplayer PC gaming on Linux faces a structural problem: the anti-cheat systems that publishers require operate as proprietary Ring 0 kernel modules that are architecturally incompatible with Linux's security model, GPL licensing, and stable ABI guarantees. We argue the right response is not to port these invasive modules to Linux, but to replace them entirely. This paper presents TPM-Attest, a hardware-rooted remote attestation framework that uses the Trusted Platform Module (TPM) 2.0 and the Linux Integrity Measurement Architecture (IMA) to prove, cryptographically, that a client booted cleanly and ran only authorised software -- without any kernel driver, without proprietary code, and without scanning player memory. The system intercepts Epic Online Services (EOS) SDK calls via a userspace LD_PRELOAD hook, gates session access on a live TPM quote bound to a server-issued nonce, and constructs an index-prefixed Merkle tree over the IMA log that is immune to duplicate-leaf collision attacks. Across 500 constructed tamper sessions we achieve a 100% detection rate; incremental leaf caching reduces repeat-attestation latency to under 3 seconds on real TPM 2.0 hardware. A controlled red-team evaluation against a live demo game confirms all four file-backed attack vectors are blocked while precisely characterising the two confirmed bypass conditions. The full implementation is released as open-source software.

</details>


### [127] [(Don't) Trust, but (Don't) Verify: Developers' Attention to Security in AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21020)
*Hamza Khalid,Ronald E. Thompson,Alejandra Sabater,Perucy Mussiba,Kelsey R. Fulton,Daniel Votipka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究通过远程观察实验探究开发者如何评估AI生成代码的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程助手虽广泛使用但常生成不安全代码，现有研究多关注结果安全性，而忽略了开发者评估AI生成代码的过程机制。

Method: 招募100名参与者进行远程观察研究，完成4个C链表任务，每个任务可从5个AI建议中循环选择并编辑，之后完成问卷调查，其中23人参与深度访谈。

Result: （摘要未提供实验结果）

Conclusion: 评估环节是安全使用AI开发的基础，本研究首次隔离并聚焦该阶段，为理解开发者识别漏洞、线索使用及信任影响提供实证基础。

Abstract: AI coding assistants are rapidly transforming software development, but are known to produce insecure code. Prior work has measured whether AI-assisted developers produce secure code, but less is known about how they evaluate AI-generated code: whether they can identify vulnerabilities, what cues they use, and how trust shapes their decisions. This evaluation step is foundational to secure development with AI, whether using auto-complete, chat tools, or AI agents. As a first step, we conducted a remote observational study with 100 participants isolating this evaluation stage. Participants were tasked with producing secure and functional code for four C linked-list tasks. For each, participants were able to cycle through five AI-generated suggestions varying in security and functionality, select one, and edit their choice into a final submission. Participants also completed a post-study survey about their decision-making and perception of AI-generated code's security and 23 completed a more in-depth interview.

</details>


### [128] [Loopjacking: Hijacking Human-in-the-Loop Approval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21081)
*Adithyan Arun Kumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出"Loopjacking"概念，指人类审批的操作与实际执行的操作不一致，分为表示型攻击与审批后状态替换攻击两种变体，并在多款已发布智能体产品中复现该漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类审批常被视为智能体执行关键操作前的最后一道安全边界，但该边界只有在此次呈审的操作与最终被授权或执行的操作相一致时才有意义，而这一绑定关系此前未被系统研究。

Method: 对一组有目的选取的已发布智能体产品进行评测：在七个Agno AgentOS版本（至3.0.9）和12个条件性内存LangGraph Agent Server组合版本（至0.14.0）中复现审批后状态替换攻击；在OpenClaw 2026.2.23中复现表示不匹配攻击（2026.2.24中被拒）；以OpenAI Agents SDK 0.22.0和0.22.2作为负对照，验证序列化续延可保持精确的逐调用绑定并拒绝被篡改的操作B。

Result: 审批后替换在七款Agno AgentOS发布版和12个LangGraph Agent Server组合版本中成功复现；表示不匹配在OpenClaw 2026.2.23中复现、在2026.2.24中被修复；OpenAI Agents SDK作为负对照，能拒绝被篡改的B，保持逐调用绑定。

Conclusion: 完整规范化的审批渲染配合精确的使用时比对，或阻止未经授权的待处理状态变更，可阻断所测试的攻击且不影响正常执行；该结果不用于估计生态系统的普遍性，并与误导性对话、会话走私、动作绑定与授权连续性等既有工作进行区分。

Abstract: Human approval is often treated as the last security boundary before an agent executes a consequential operation. That boundary is only meaningful if the operation presented for review is the operation later authorized or released. We call failures of this binding Loopjacking: a human approves what they understand as operation A, while the implementation uses that decision for a materially different operation B. We distinguish two variants. In a representation-based attack, B is already encoded but omitted or misrepresented at approval time; in a post-approval state-substitution attack, the human sees the correct A and mutable workflow state later replaces it with B.
  We evaluate a purposive set of released agent products. We reproduce post-approval substitution in seven tested Agno AgentOS releases ending at 3.0.9 and in 12 tested versions of a conditional in-memory LangGraph Agent Server composition ending at 0.14.0. We reproduce representation mismatch in OpenClaw 2026.2.23 and its rejection in 2026.2.24. OpenAI Agents SDK 0.22.0 and 0.22.2 provide a negative control: serialized continuation preserves exact per-call binding and rejects mutated B. These results do not estimate ecosystem prevalence. They show that complete canonical approval rendering and exact use-time comparison, or preventing unauthorized pending-state mutation, block the tested attacks while preserving legitimate execution. We separate this contribution from established work on misleading dialogs, session smuggling, action binding, and authorization continuity.

</details>


### [129] [Origin Is All You Need: Provenance-Aware Transformers for Structural Trust-Boundary Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21088)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Jeff Huang,Guofei Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出溯源感知Transformer（Provenance-Aware Transformers），通过将来源标签作为模型内的一等架构信号，在生成过程中强制区分权威与非权威来源，从而防御间接提示注入（IPI），在分布内与分布外均保持鲁棒性且不损失效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer缺乏"来源权威性"的架构概念：检索文档、用户输入与系统指令都经由同一无差别的注意力机制处理，模型只能凭措辞推断哪些应被遵从、哪些应被视为数据，导致间接提示注入（IPI）成为LLM系统的核心安全挑战。

Method: 为每个输入token分配编码其来源的ring ID；在模型中引入来源嵌入（origin embeddings）、可学习的来源注意力偏置（learnable origin attention bias）以及归一化下保持溯源信息的可学习来源缩放（learnable origin scale），从而在架构层面建立权威与非权威来源之间的结构性边界；并提出两阶段微调流水线，在已发布的预训练模型上实例化该架构，使模型习得来源语义与ring约束下的任务行为。

Result: 实验表明，Provenance-Aware Transformers在分布内与分布外均对IPI保持稳健的抵抗能力，同时保持了与基础预训练模型相当的任务效用。

Conclusion: 将溯源暴露为一等的架构信号，能够推动LLM安全对齐从脆弱的模式匹配转向显式的信任分离，为IPI防御提供了新的架构级思路。

Abstract: Indirect prompt injection (IPI) remains a central safety and security challenge for large language model (LLM) systems because standard transformers lack architectural notion of source authority. Retrieved documents, user inputs, and system instructions are all processed through the same undifferentiated attention mechanism, forcing the model to infer from wording alone what should be obeyed and what should be treated as data. We propose Provenance-Aware Transformers, a provenance-aware defense that makes application-supplied source labels actionable inside the model. Each input token is assigned a ring ID encoding its origin, and the model is augmented with origin embeddings, a learnable origin attention bias, and a learnable origin scale that preserves provenance under normalization. The resulting architecture enforces a structural boundary between authoritative and non-authoritative sources during generation. To instantiate this architecture on released pretrained models, we propose a two-stage fine-tuning pipeline to teach the model origin semantics and task behavior under ring constraints. Evaluation shows that Provenance-Aware Transformers maintain robust resistance to IPI both in-distribution and out-of-distribution while preserving utility comparable to the base pretrained model. More broadly, our work shows that exposing provenance as a first-class architectural signal can shift LLM safety alignment from brittle pattern matching toward explicit trust separation.

</details>


### [130] [NetInspector: Measuring and Improving LLM Capabilities for Reliable Intent-Based Networking Policy Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21103)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Hongxin Hu,Guofei Gu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 NetInspector，一个三层代理式框架，通过"先验证后执行"协议将信息检索与推理解耦，使 LLM 在意图翻译与安全策略校验中具备可验证的网络事据支撑，从而显著降低漏报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络规模大、配置异构，人工策略管理难以为继；IBN 可实现高层意图到底层配置的自动翻译，但现有系统依赖静态启发式与固定特征分类器，面对服务定义新增、措辞演化等分布漂移泛化能力差。LLM 具备推理与翻译能力，是 IBN 策略生成的潜在方案，但其在该任务上的可靠性及对安全风险的影响尚不明确。

Method: 作者首先评测微调 LLM 的意图翻译与安全策略违规检测能力，发现其翻译表现优异但在违规检测上存在漏报（false negative）。归因表明根因并非缺乏逻辑推理能力，而是缺乏对网络拓扑与组层级的持续 grounding。据此提出 NetInspector：一个三层代理式（agentic）框架，实施"先验证后执行"（verify-then-act）协议，将信息检索与推理解耦——LLM 专注符号推理，每个策略决策在批准前均由从实时 Environment Layer 检索到的可验证网络事据支撑。

Result: 在覆盖校园、企业与 WAN 拓扑的 2,224 样本合成基准 NetInspector-Bench 上，NetInspector 相比无 grounding 的基线将漏报率（FNR）降低超过 30%，并在语言分布漂移下保持稳健。

Conclusion: LLM 可用于 IBN 意图翻译，但其安全校验的漏报源于缺乏网络拓扑与组层级的持续 grounding，而非推理能力不足；通过将检索与推理解耦、以可验证网络事实支撑决策的 agentic 框架，可显著提升策略安全校验可靠性并增强对分布漂移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Modern networks are large in scale and heterogeneous in configuration, making manual policy management increasingly impractical. Intent-Based Networking (IBN) addresses this by automating the translation of high-level operator goals into low-level network configurations. Yet existing IBN systems rely on static heuristics and fixed-feature classifiers that generalize poorly to distribution shifts such as new service definitions or evolving phrasing in operator requests. Large Language Models (LLMs), with strong reasoning and translation capabilities demonstrated across many domains, are a natural candidate for IBN policy generation. However, it is unclear whether LLMs can be reliably applied to this task, nor whether their use mitigates or worsens the underlying security risk.
  In this work, we show that while fine-tuned LLMs excel at intent translation, they exhibit false negative rates when checking whether a proposed intent violates an existing security policy. The root cause is not a lack of logical reasoning capability, but LLMs lack of persistent grounding in network topology and group hierarchy. Motivated by this finding, we introduce NetInspector, a three-layer agentic framework that enforces a verify-then-act protocol, decoupling information retrieval from reasoning so that the LLM focuses on symbolic reasoning while every policy decision is grounded in verifiable network facts retrieved from a live Environment Layer before approval. On NetInspector-Bench, a 2,224-sample synthetic benchmark spanning campus, enterprise, and WAN topologies, NetInspector reduces FNR by over 30\% relative to ungrounded baselines and remains robust under linguistic distribution shifts.

</details>


### [131] [Toss If Perishable: An Ethnographic Study on Building Scenario-Based Training for Non-Perishable Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21147)
*Francis Hahn,Spencer Cherry,Kumar Shashwat,Laura Araujo Buldrini,Daniel Lende,Xinming Ou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出"非易失知识"概念（独立于工具的调查思维技能），并通过场景驱动训练方法验证其可传授性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC依赖在职培训，聚焦具体工具与流程，工具复杂性会掩盖底层推理过程，阻碍分析师学习调查技能。因此需研究独立于工具的调查思维技能能否被有效传授。

Method: 提出"非易失知识"概念，设计基于真实事件的工具无关场景驱动训练方法，招募高校学生作为被试，开展民族志研究；记录25名学员、5个场次共20小时训练数据，采用扎根理论分析促进或抑制调查思维技能学习的因素。

Result: 通过扎根理论分析识别出影响调查思维技能学习的关键促进与抑制因素。

Conclusion: 结合场景训练、民族志研究与技术分析，为培养业界视为SOC核心能力的推理技能提供了训练路径与实证依据。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) often rely upon on-the-job training focusing on the specific tools and procedures a SOC utilizes. The complexity in tooling can overshadow the underlying reasoning process, hindering an analyst's ability to learn investigative skills. We formulate the concept of "non-perishable knowledge" which corresponds to investigative thinking skills independent of tools. We developed a scenario-driven training method to understand whether such non-perishable knowledge can be imparted through specially designed incident scenarios, where trainees are presented with and solve investigative challenges in a tool-agnostic manner. We designed two such scenarios based on real-world incidents. Human subjects were recruited from a university's student body for ethnographic study to understand how this training method is received by the subjects and how they perform on such tasks. We collected data from 20 hours of documented training with 25 trainees spread across five sessions. Using grounded-theory, we analyzed the data and uncovered factors that inhibit or promote learning of the investigative thinking skills. Our research combines scenario-based training, ethnographic research, and technical analysis to examine how to best train students in the reasoning skills that industry deems vital to SOCs.

</details>


### [132] [X-SPUR: Explainable Surprisal-Based Protocol-Aware Unsupervised Reasoning for Automotive Ethernet Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21217)
*Jisoo Kim,Seonghoon Jeong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出X-SPUR，一个可解释、基于惊奇度、协议感知的无监督车载以太网入侵检测框架，通过因果语言建模检测异常，在TOW-IDS上AUC达0.9987，并消除手工特征工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载以太网承载异构多协议流量，标注攻击数据稀缺，而无监督检测中最强的先验方法AERO仍依赖手工提取的流量特征，泛化性与可解释性受限。

Method: 将原始数据包字段表示为token序列，用因果语言模型学习正常流量模式，以逐token交叉熵惊奇度作为异常分数；引入双模态融合架构（通过加法融合与Hadamard交互结合载荷token嵌入与包间时间）；提出双top-k%的逐协议Z-score校准以处理不同协议族分数分布异质性。

Result: 在TOW-IDS数据集上AUC达0.9987，略高于AERO的0.9969；使用相同架构与超参训练的CarDS模型在第二个车载以太网数据集上保持强性能；逐token惊奇度可将异常分数归因到具体协议字段，提供细粒度解释性。

Conclusion: X-SPUR在无需手工特征工程的前提下取得优于AERO的检测性能，并具备跨数据集迁移能力与字段级可解释性，适用于异构车载网络的可解释安全分析。

Abstract: Automotive Ethernet carries heterogeneous multi-protocol traffic in modern in-vehicle networks, where labeled attack data are rarely available and the strongest prior unsupervised detector still relies on handcrafted traffic features. This article presents X-SPUR, an explainable, surprisal-based, protocol-aware unsupervised reasoning framework that instead represents raw packet fields as token sequences, learns benign traffic patterns through causal language modeling, and detects anomalies from per-token cross-entropy surprisal. To incorporate temporal context, we introduce a bimodal fusion architecture that combines payload-token embeddings with inter-packet timing through additive fusion and a Hadamard interaction. To handle the heterogeneous score distributions of different protocol families, we further propose a dual top-$k$% per-protocol $Z$-score calibration that jointly captures moderately distributed and sparse anomaly signatures. On the TOW-IDS dataset, X-SPUR achieves an AUC of 0.9987. This is marginally higher than the 0.9969 reported for AERO. X-SPUR also eliminates handcrafted feature engineering. We train a separate CarDS model using the same architecture and training hyperparameters. This model retains strong performance on the second automotive Ethernet dataset. Beyond detection, per-token surprisal provides fine-grained explainability by attributing anomaly scores to specific protocol fields, supporting interpretable security analysis in heterogeneous in-vehicle networks.

</details>


### [133] [Combining Exploratory Analysis and Automated Analysis for Anomaly Detection in Real-Time Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21222)
*Ahmed Shah,Ibrahim Abualhaol,Mahmoud Gad,Michael Weiss*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究实时BGP异常监测系统，提出融合自动化分析与探索式可视化分析的原型平台，并探讨二者的互补关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分析师面对海量实时安全信息易不堪重负，往往只关注少量告警，可能遗漏重要事件及其关联。问题核心在于分析师用于检测、探索和响应网络攻击的系统：开发者需构建能呈现多源、多抽象层级信息且直观易用的系统。

Method: 以实时边界网关协议（BGP）流量为对象，测试一个监测可能预示安全威胁的异常的原型系统。该系统实时计算指标与威胁告警，并对告警上下文进行可视化，从而考察探索式分析（可视化）与自动化分析的互补性与交互。

Result: 实现了一个可实时计算指标和威胁告警并可视化告警上下文的原型平台，并揭示了自动化分析与探索式分析之间的互补关系。

Conclusion: 面向学生、安全研究者和开发者，通过实时流系统的开发经验，说明自动化分析与探索式分析的互补作用，可为实时安全监测系统的开发与使用提供参考。

Abstract: Security analysts can become overwhelmed with monitoring real-time security information that is important to help them defend their network. They also tend to focus on a limited portion of the alerts, and therefore risk missing important events and links between them. At the heart of the problem is the system that analysts use to detect, explore, and respond to cyber-attacks. Developers of security analysis systems face the challenge of developing a system that can present different sources of information at multiple levels of abstraction, while also creating a system that is intuitive to use. In this article, we examine the complementary nature of exploratory analysis and automated analysis by testing the development of a system that monitors real-time Border Gateway Protocol (BGP) traffic for anomalies that might indicate security threats. BGP is an essential component for supporting the infrastructure of the Internet; however, it is also highly vulnerable and can be hijacked by attackers to propagate spam or launch denial-of-service attacks. Some of the attack scenarios on the BGP infrastructure can be quite elaborate, and it is difficult, if not impossible, to fully automate the detection of such attacks. This article makes two contributions: i) it describes a prototype platform for computing indicators and threat alerts in real time and for visualizing the context of an alert, and ii) it discusses the interaction of exploratory analysis (visualization) and automated analysis. This article is relevant to students, security researchers, and developers who are interested in the development or use of real-time security monitoring systems. They will gain insights into the complementary aspects of automated analysis and exploratory analysis through the development of a real-time streaming system.

</details>


### [134] [Transcript-Bound Combiners for Downgrade-Resilient Hybrid Post-Quantum Key Establishment: Definition, Proof, and Embedded-Device Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21273)
*Bhanwar Gupta,Sanjeev Rana*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究混合密钥建立中"降级韧性"能否成为组合器(combiner)的局部性质。作者在组合器层给出基于博弈的定义,并证明双向分离:忽略握手转录的混合组合器必然被降级;而将会话密钥与确认标签绑定到转录哈希的组合器可阻止一切此类攻击(除256位转录哈希可忽略项外)。还给出显式的最强链路安全界,并用Cortex-M4测量构建成本模型:转录绑定每方增加一次哈希,约占握手计算11.8%、含射频能耗1.5%,不增加任何消息或线上字节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合密钥建立将后量子KEM与经典Diffie-Hellman并列运行,已被TLS、SSH、IKE标准化(后量子分量为ML-KEM)。但混合KEM仅保护派生密钥,不保护"选择哪些原语"这一协商过程的完整性。完整协议通过握手转录(transcript)认证该协商;而将混合KEM作为独立即插即用原语部署,或在无转录认证的极简握手中使用时,则不继承此保证,主动攻击者可剥离后量子选项。作者由此追问:仅凭密钥调度本身,组合器需要包含什么内容,才能使降级韧性成为其局部属性。

Method: 1) 在组合器层给出基于博弈(game-based)的降级韧性定义;2) 证明双向分离(two-sided separation):忽略转录的组合器被确定性降级,而把会话密钥和确认标签绑定到转录哈希的组合器可阻断所有此类尝试;3) 给出显式的最强链路(strongest-link)安全界;4) 使用由已发表Cortex-M4测量校准的成本模型评估性能。

Result: 分离结论:不绑定转录的组合器可被100%降级;绑定转录哈希的组合器可阻断全部降级尝试,仅余256位转录哈希可忽略的误差项。性能:转录绑定每方仅增加一次哈希,约占握手计算的11.8%,但仅占含射频能耗的1.5%,且不增加任何消息或线上字节。所有数据由通过30项校验门槛的已发布测试工具生成。

Conclusion: 降级韧性可以作为组合器的局部性质:只要密钥调度将会话密钥与确认标签绑定到转录哈希,无需依赖完整协议即可抵抗降级。该方案安全增益显著,而计算与通信开销极小(尤其含射频能耗几乎可忽略),适用于资源受限的IoT场景。

Abstract: Hybrid key establishment runs a post-quantum key-encapsulation mechanism (KEM) alongside a classical Diffie-Hellman primitive, so that the session key stays secure while either component resists attack. This design is now standardized in the Transport Layer Security protocol, Secure Shell, and the Internet Key Exchange, with the standardized module-lattice KEM (ML-KEM) as the post-quantum component. A hybrid KEM secures the derived key, but not the integrity of the negotiation that selects which primitives are used. Full protocols authenticate that negotiation through a handshake transcript; a hybrid KEM deployed as a standalone drop-in primitive, or inside a minimal handshake without transcript authentication, inherits no such guarantee, and an active attacker can strip the post-quantum option. We ask what the key schedule alone must contain to make downgrade resilience a local property of the combiner. We give a game-based definition at the combiner layer and prove a two-sided separation: a combiner that ignores the transcript is downgraded with certainty, whereas one that binds the session key and the confirmation tag to a hash of the transcript blocks every such attempt, up to a term negligible for a 256-bit transcript hash. We also give an explicit strongest-link security bound. Using a calibrated cost model composed from published Cortex-M4 measurements, transcript binding adds one hash per party - about 11.8% of handshake computation but only 1.5% of radio-inclusive energy - and adds no messages or bytes on the wire. Every reported number is produced by a released harness that passes a 30-check validation gate.

</details>


### [135] [Identifying Security Platform Product Abuse with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21303)
*Shaefer Drew,Michael Brautbar,Paul Knight,Edward Raff,Lana Peric-McDermott,Simran Sarin,Nickolas Machado,Hanna Albright,Vitaly Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并部署了首个面向SaaS产品滥用的全系统防御方案，覆盖多模态多数据库数据采集、冷启动稀有事件建模及真实部署约束，实现滥用覆盖率提升35%、月告警量下降30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产品滥用（product abuse）在SaaS行业中虽个体罕见但呈增长趋势。高级威胁行为者可在客户环境内滥用安全平台，或对产品本身进行绕过实验，并利用离地（Living-off-the-Land, LOTL）攻击规避易被检测的恶意软件。现有防御需跨异构数据库整合多模态数据，同时面临此类高级事件的固有稀有性导致的冷启动问题，并受成本、用户行为、性能等实际部署约束限制。

Method: 构建并部署了一个面向SaaS产品滥用的全系统防御框架：跨不同数据库类型采集多模态数据；针对高级事件的内在稀有性设计冷启动学习方案；在成本、用户行为、性能等真实运营约束下进行系统设计。此外引入可解释特征与反事实分析机制，用于对已识别攻击的回顾性评估。

Result: 产品滥用覆盖范围提升35%；月告警数量减少30%；系统能够适应恶意行为者行为的变化；并通过回顾性评估验证了可解释特征与反事实性能对已识别攻击的价值。

Conclusion: 该研究首次提供了面向产品滥用的全系统防御，尤其是在已部署、可运行的生产能力层面，证明在真实运营约束下可同时实现更高覆盖率、更低告警噪声与对攻击者行为演变的适应性。

Abstract: Product abuse is an individually rare, but growing, problem across the SaaS industry. Highly sophisticated threat actors can misuse security platforms within customer environments or conduct bypass experiments on the product itself. Threat actors can leverage living-off-the-land (LOTL) attacks to avoid using cumbersome, frequently detected malware. Remediating this threat requires collecting multiple data modalities across different types of databases, addressing a cold-start problem in the intrinsic rarity of such sophisticated but dangerous events, and designing within the constraints of real-world deployment (e.g., cost, user behavior, performance, etc). To wit, we provide the first study of such a whole-system defense, especially with respect to a deployed and operational capability. Our results show an increase in product abuse coverage by 35\%, a 30\% reduction in monthly alerts, and adaptability to changes in malicious actors' behavior. We review both the constraints we considered in designing the system to meet operational requirements and a retrospective evaluation of the value of explainable features and counterfactual performance on previously identified attacks.

</details>


### [136] [Conformal Privacy Auditing: Calibrated Re-identification Attacks with Statistical Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21340)
*Shuo Huang,Gholamreza Haffari,Xingliang Yuan,Ting Yu,Lizhen Qu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Conformal Privacy Auditing (CPA)，一种无分布假设的校准框架，为每篇发布文档对LLM增强攻击者提供重识别风险的统计证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计只报告特定攻击流水线的成功率，缺乏有限样本统计保证；而差分隐私等训练期保护难以转化为针对单篇自然语言文档的发布期决策。

Method: CPA基于保形预测，在可交换性假设下输出候选身份的保形模糊集，保证以用户指定置信度包含真实身份，并以集合大小导出可解释的泄漏代理。方法同时支持logit访问与仅采样两类攻击者，可统一审计开源模型与专有API模型。

Result: 在多个发布基准与攻击者配置下，CPA实现了校准的覆盖率，并揭示出随着辅助知识、LLM增强与发布机制变化，认证可识别性出现显著变化。

Conclusion: CPA为报告和比较跨攻击者配置、数据集及发布机制的发布期关联风险提供了具有统计基础的依据。

Abstract: Empirical identity leakage from released text is increasingly driven by attackers that combine large language models (LLMs) with auxiliary knowledge to link documents to individuals. Existing audits typically report success rates for specific attack pipelines but lack finite-sample statistical guarantees, while training-time protections such as differential privacy are difficult to translate into release-time decisions for individual natural-language documents. We introduce Conformal Privacy Auditing(CPA), a distribution-free calibration framework that provides a statistical certificate of re-identification risk for each released document against LLM-empowered adversaries. CPA outputs a conformal ambiguity set of candidate identities that is guaranteed to contain the true identity with user-chosen confidence under exchangeability, together with an interpretable leakage proxy derived from set size. CPA supports both logit-access and sampling-only attackers, enabling audits of open-source models and proprietary API models in a unified framework. Across multiple release benchmarks and attacker configurations, CPA achieves calibrated coverage and reveals sharp shifts in certified identifiability as auxiliary knowledge, LLM augmentation, and release mechanisms vary, providing a statistically grounded basis for reporting and comparing release-time linkage risk across attacker configurations, datasets, and release mechanisms alike.

</details>


### [137] [CESBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models on Cryptographic Engineering Security for IoT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21344)
*Wenquan Zhou,An Wang,Jing Liang,Peien Feng,Jingqi Zhang,Yaoling Ding,Liehuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 CESBench——首个面向物联网设备密码工程安全的 LLM 基准，包含 380 道专家撰写题目，覆盖六个子域与四类任务，对 11 个模型评测显示综合得分 54.4%–83.6%，判断理由论证是最弱能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备仅有安全算法不足够：具备物理访问能力的攻击者可直接攻击实现，且部署后缺陷难以修复。LLM 已被用于构建与分析此类实现，但现有基准仅覆盖密码学或通用网络安全，缺乏对"密码工程"这一实现层安全的评测基准。

Method: 构建 CESBench：380 道专家撰写题目，覆盖物联网密码工程安全六个子域（侧信道、故障注入、实现、防护对策、评估、集成）；四类任务对应不同能力：209 道选择题测记忆、67 道判断题要求给出安全判定及理由、63 道场景题要求工程诊断、41 道代码题由 572 个测试用例评分。用 11 个开源权重与闭源 LLM 逐题作答进行验证；选择题与代码题自动评分，判断题与场景题由 LLM 评判，并与另一模型家族的第二评判者及人工重评交叉校验。

Result: 综合得分区间为 54.4%–83.6%。各任务类型最高分：选择题 98.6%、代码题 95.1%、场景诊断 88.4%，而判断题仅 58.8%。跨模型统计中，88.5% 的安全判定结论正确，但其理由论证仅获得 53.4% 的评分要点分。

Conclusion: 最强模型在选择题上已接近饱和、多数代码题可解，而对安全判定进行理由论证仍是最弱能力。基准、提示词与逐题结果均已公开。

Abstract: For Internet of Things (IoT) devices, a secure algorithm alone is not enough: an attacker with physical access can attack the implementation directly, and its flaws are hard to fix once deployed. Large language models (LLMs) are now used to build and analyze such implementations. LLM benchmarks exist for cryptography and general cybersecurity, but none covers cryptographic engineering. In this paper, we present CESBench, 380 expert-written items across six sub-domains of cryptographic engineering security for IoT devices: side-channel, fault injection, implementation, countermeasures, evaluation, and integration. Four task types target different competences: 209 multiple-choice items test recall, 67 judgment items require a security verdict and its justification, 63 scenario items require an engineering diagnosis, and 41 code tasks are graded by 572 test cases. To validate the benchmark, 11 open-weight and proprietary LLMs answer every item. Multiple-choice and code responses are scored automatically, and judgment and scenario responses by an LLM judge, whose scores are checked against a second judge from another model family and human re-scoring. Composite scores range from 54.4% to 83.6%. The top score on each task type is 98.6% for multiple choice, 95.1% for code, and 88.4% for scenario diagnosis, but only 58.8% for judgment. Across models, 88.5% of verdicts are correct, yet their justifications earn only 53.4% of the rubric marks. Multiple choice is near its ceiling for the strongest models and most code tasks are solved, whereas justifying a security verdict remains the weakest competence. The benchmark, prompts, and per-item results are public.

</details>


### [138] [Batched Paillier-Based Hamming-Distance Computation over Binary Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21364)
*Yavor Litchev,Liwen Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文实现了一个基于Paillier的同态加密客户端，通过进位分离编码、查表加密、降指数解密与GPU加速，在批量512位嵌入上取得高吞吐率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态加密支持在加密二进制嵌入上进行外包计算，但大整数运算与数据搬运限制了吞吐率，需要针对性的工程优化来提升批量处理性能。

Method: 融合进位分离二进制编码、基于查表的加密、降指数解密、CUDA/CGBN大整数运算、持久化设备状态以及批量检索集成；给出编码正确性证明，并对比四种CPU与GPU客户端配置，其中查表配置采用280位指数参数。

Result: 在3次热态试验、每批10,000个随机512位嵌入下，查表GPU配置的批量吞吐中位数为43,091次加密/秒与28,983次汉明距离解码/秒；摊薄成本为每条向量0.0232 ms与0.0345 ms，相对CPU基线分别加速453.8倍与200.9倍。

Conclusion: 结合密码学预计算、批量加速器执行与持久化运行时状态可显著提升吞吐率；研究区分了热批量性能与单请求延迟，并指出初始化、传输和检索集成仍存在剩余开销。

Abstract: Additively homomorphic encryption supports outsourced computation on encrypted binary embeddings, but large-integer arithmetic and data movement can limit throughput. We describe a Paillier-based client that combines a carry-separated binary encoding, table-based encryption, reduced-exponent decryption, CUDA/CGBN arithmetic, persistent device state, and batched retrieval integration. We establish the encoding's correctness and characterize four CPU and GPU client configurations. The lookup configuration uses a 280-bit exponent-size parameter. Across 3 warm-state trials on batches of 10,000 random 512-bit embeddings, the lookup GPU configuration achieved median-batch throughputs of 43,091 encryptions/s and 28,983 Hamming- distance decodes/s. Its amortized costs were 0.0232 ms and 0.0345 ms per vector, corresponding to factors of 453.8 and 200.9 relative to the measured CPU baseline. These implementation- specific results demonstrate the throughput benefits of combining cryptographic precomputation, batched accelerator execution, and persistent runtime state. The study distinguishes warm-batch performance from isolated-request latency and identifies the remaining costs of initialization, transport, and retrieval integration.

</details>


### [139] [TrustBOM: A Scalable Architecture for Confidentiality-Preserving SBOMs Across Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21419)
*Van Thang Nguyen,Frederic Rupprecht,Tom Lawrence,Lucca Di Benedetto,Sören Schubert,Amor Rezgui,Sebastian Werner,Maria C. Borges,Stefan Tai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TrustBOM，一种基于零知识非成员证明的保密性SBOM架构，可在不暴露依赖图的情况下证明特定漏洞或受限许可不存在，证明生成随约束数线性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SBOM已成为企业架构中软件供应链治理的关键机制，但组织因担心暴露敏感依赖信息而采用有限，需在保护机密性的同时实现可验证的合规声明。

Method: TrustBOM集成到企业CI/CD工作流，采用零知识非成员证明，依据消费者定义的政策约束进行选择性证明；架构设计使证明生成复杂度随断言约束数量线性增长，而非随SBOM规模增长。

Result: 实证评估显示线性性能，在通用硬件上平均每个约束的证明生成时间为0.9秒，表明在企业平台生态中部署具有可行性。

Conclusion: TrustBOM为保密性SBOM提供可扩展方案，兼顾依赖信息保护与供应链合规验证，具备大规模企业部署的可行性。

Abstract: Software Bills of Materials (SBOMs) have emerged as a key mechanism for software supply chain governance in enterprise architectures. However, their adoption across organizations remains limited due to concerns about exposing sensitive dependency information. To address this limitation, we propose TrustBOM, a scalable architecture for confidentiality-preserving SBOMs integrated into enterprise CI/CD workflows. TrustBOM enables software providers to attest that specific vulnerabilities or restricted licenses are absent from their software without revealing the underlying dependency graph. This is achieved using zero-knowledge non-membership proofs, which are applied selectively based on consumer-defined policy constraints. The architecture ensures that proof generation scales linearly with the number of asserted constraints rather than with the size of the SBOM, enabling efficient operation in large-scale enterprise environments. Empirical evaluation demonstrates linear performance, with an average proof generation time of 0.9 seconds per constraint on commodity hardware, indicating the feasibility of deployment in enterprise platform ecosystems.

</details>


### [140] [DEFEAT: Stitching Fragmented File I/O Contexts for Early Ransomware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21426)
