*Muhammad Ejaz Ahmed,Hyoungshick Kim,Mohsen Ali Alawami,Alsharif Abuadbba,Seyit Camtepe,Surya Nepal,Junaid Qadir*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 DEFEAT 框架，将碎片化文件 I/O 事件重构为 File Event Gadget（FEG），以属性控制流图（ACFG）建模并用 GNN 无监督聚类，实现首文件即检测的高精度勒索软件识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 勒索软件将文件操作分散到临时与中间文件，割裂了连接单个 I/O 事件与整体加密活动的语义上下文。现有检测器基于孤立文件流推理：模式匹配方法依赖刚性事件序列，学习方法需跨大量文件累积统计证据，均难以应对这种碎片化。

Method: DEFEAT 把因果相关的文件事件分组为 FEG（跨多个动态创建文件的语义连贯单元），范围限定于单一用户资产的文件操作上下文，无需全系统插桩，区别于溯源图。每个 FEG 建模为 ACFG，经图神经网络嵌入后做无监督聚类，使分析人员可标注整个行为簇而非单样本。

Result: 在覆盖 67 个勒索软件家族、97,816,471 条文件 I/O 事件的语料上，检测准确率达 99.2%，优于 UNVEIL、RWGuard、Peeler 等最先进方法 6.57%–7.56%；标注工作量减少 94%。

Conclusion: DEFEAT 以单次文件加密为粒度：每个 ACFG 恰对应一个 FEG（一个用户资产上下文），故首个文件操作完成即可分配簇标签，实现首个加密文件的即时检测。

Abstract: Ransomware increasingly fragments its file operations across temporary and intermediate files, scattering the semantic context that links individual I/O events to an overarching encryption campaign. This fragmentation defeats existing detectors that reason over isolated file streams -- whether pattern-based methods that match rigid event sequences or learning-based methods that require accumulating statistical evidence across many files. We present DEFEAT, a framework that reconstructs this fragmented, scattered context by grouping causally related file events into File Event Gadgets (FEGs), semantically coherent units that capture the full intent behind sequences of file operations spanning multiple dynamically created files. Unlike provenance graphs (system-wide causal graphs that record relationships among all OS entities, such as processes, files, sockets, and registry keys, across the entire system), FEGs are scoped to the file-operation context of a single user asset, enabling lightweight, targeted analysis without whole-system instrumentation. Each FEG is modelled as an attributed control flow graph (ACFG) and embedded via a graph neural network for unsupervised clustering, enabling analysts to label entire behavioural clusters rather than individual samples, reducing annotation effort by 94%. Evaluated on a corpus of 97,816,471 file I/O events spanning 67 ransomware families, DEFEAT achieves 99.2% detection accuracy and outperforms state-of-the-art methods including UNVEIL, RWGuard, and Peeler by 6.57 to 7.56%. The framework operates at the granularity of a single file encryption: because each ACFG represents exactly one FEG (one user asset context), a cluster label can be assigned as soon as the first file operation completes, enabling detection at the first encrypted file.

</details>


### [141] [HE-Guardrail: A Homomorphic Guardrail Against Jailbreak Attacks for Encrypted Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21484)
*Byeongseo Min,Yongwoo Lee,Young-Sik Kim,Yongjune Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文识别出同态加密大语言模型（HE-LLM）推理中的关键安全漏洞：恶意客户端可提交越狱等对抗性提示，而加密保密性使服务器无法检查提示或响应，导致攻击不可见；为此提出 HE-Guardrail，在全密文下评估护栏并同态控制响应是否返回，基于 Llama Guard、JBShield 与 GradSafe 三种护栏实例化，结果能在密文域高度复现明文护栏决策，并呈现不同的安全—效率—效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态加密被广泛用于隐私保护机器学习，客户端将加密输入提交给服务器，服务器可在不接触明文的情况下评测大语言模型。然而该范式存在被忽视的安全盲区：保护良性客户端机密性的加密特性，同时使服务器无法审查进入的提示与生成的响应，恶意客户端因此可发起越狱等对抗性攻击，且攻击过程对服务器完全不可见，难以检测或阻断。

Method: 提出 HE-Guardrail 框架，在密文域内完整执行护栏机制，并同态地控制目标模型响应是否返回给客户端。具体地，将护栏判定与响应门控均以同态运算实现，并以三种代表性护栏进行实例化：Llama Guard、JBShield 与 GradSafe，从而在不解密的前提下完成对抗提示的识别与拦截。

Result: 实验表明，HE-Guardrail 在密文域中的决策与对应明文护栏高度一致，能够有效复现其判定行为；三种实例化方案在安全性、效率与效用上表现出各自不同的权衡特征。

Conclusion: HE-LLM 推理场景中，加密带来的机密性与服务器侧可审计性之间存在天然张力，构成现实安全漏洞；HE-Guardrail 证明护栏可被迁移至密文域执行，在保持隐私的同时实现对恶意提示的同态拦截，为隐私保护推理的安全防护提供了可行路径，后续可在安全—效率—效用之间按需选择具体护栏实例。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) has emerged as a promising approach to privacy-preserving machine learning (PPML), enabling computation directly over encrypted data. In HE-based PPML, a client submits an encrypted input to the server, which evaluates models such as large language models (LLMs) without access to the underlying plaintext. However, we identify a critical security vulnerability in this setting: HE-LLM inference is vulnerable to malicious clients that submit adversarial prompts, such as jailbreak attacks. The same confidentiality that protects benign clients also prevents the server from inspecting incoming prompts or generated responses, making adversarial attempts difficult to detect or block and potentially allowing successful attacks to remain entirely invisible to the server. To address this vulnerability, we propose HE-Guardrail, a framework that evaluates guardrail mechanisms entirely over encrypted data and homomorphically controls whether the target-model response is returned to the client. We instantiate HE-Guardrail with three representative guardrails - Llama Guard, JBShield, and GradSafe. Our results show that HE-Guardrail closely reproduces the decisions of the corresponding plaintext guardrails in the encrypted domain, with distinct security-efficiency-utility trade-offs.

</details>


### [142] [ServeGuard: Verifiable, Bounded-Residual Confinement of Operator-Invisible Channels Without Revealing the Certified Read Factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21515)
*Dominik Dahlem,Rui Vieira*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 ServeGuard：一种供应链原语，让发布者以零知识证明交付"携带证明的适配器"，从结构上消除监控器盲区内的后门通道，而非事后检测，消费者无需信任发布者即可验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方适配器以不透明权重矩阵形式分发，接收方无法在不信任发布者或不检查其核心资产（权重）的情况下判断适配器是否隐藏后门。对于"载荷放置在安全监控器结构性盲区"这一重要类别，检测作为防御是不健全的：任何只经由已声明监控器分解的检测器在其盲子空间上不变，且良性适配本身也使用该子空间，导致良性适配器与后门适配器在所有盲子空间统计量上重叠。因此需要改变思路，从"检测"转向"结构性消除"。

Method: 发布者构造适配器，使其仅通过监控器覆盖的方向读取输入，并以零知识方式证明这一点而不泄露其所证明的读取因子。证书开销低：昂贵部分（识别监控器盲区）是公共基模型的确定性函数，故只需证明一个线性恒等式；所服务的残差即基模型自身公共下限，而非证明者选择的容差。产物 ServeGuard：发布者交付携带证明的适配器，消费者或监管者无需读取被证明的读取因子、无需信任发布者，即可验证该适配器相对于已声明监控器不含有此类隐藏通道；并在准入时通过类型守卫将保证绑定到实际服务的适配器字节。

Result: 在来自四个模型家族、最多 7B 的八个检查点上评估：监控预算是架构性的，测得的前沿在分组查询（GQA）检查点上于 value-path rank 处饱和，但在多头（MHA）检查点上未饱和。在 0.5B 模型上，受限对良性适配几乎无代价，使监控器质量成为安全杠杆。

Conclusion: 通过以可证明的结构性消除替代事后的盲区后门检测，ServeGuard 提供了一种可验证的适配器供应链原语，其证明廉价、绑定实际服务字节，且安全性由架构与监控器质量决定。

Abstract: Third-party adapters for open-weight language models ship as opaque weight matrices; a recipient cannot check whether an adapter hides a backdoor without trusting the publisher or inspecting the weights, the publisher's core asset. For one important class (payloads placed where a safety monitor is structurally blind), detection is unsound as a defense: every detector that factors through the declared monitor is invariant on its blind subspace, and honest and backdoored adapters overlap on every blind-subspace statistic we evaluate, because benign adaptation uses that subspace too. Rather than detect this channel, we make it structurally \emph{absent} and prove that we did. The publisher builds the adapter to read the input only through directions the monitor covers and proves this in zero knowledge, revealing nothing about the read factor it certifies. The certificate is cheap because the expensive part, identifying the monitor's blind spot, is a deterministic function of the \emph{public} base model, so only one linear identity is proved; the served residual is the base model's own public floor, not a prover-chosen tolerance. The result is \emph{ServeGuard}, a supply-chain primitive: the publisher ships a \emph{proof-carrying adapter} whose proof lets a consumer or regulator verify, without the certified read factor and without trusting the publisher, that the adapter carries no hidden channel of this class relative to the declared monitor; an admission-time typing guard binds the guarantee to the adapter bytes admitted at serving time. Across eight checkpoints up to 7B from four families, the monitoring budget is architectural: the measured frontier saturates at the value-path rank on grouped-query checkpoints but not on multi-head ones. On a 0.5B model confinement is nearly free for benign adaptation, making monitor quality the security lever.

</details>


### [143] [Critical sets of Latin squares based on autoparatopisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21532)
*Manuel González-Regadera,Raúl M. Falcón,María Dolores Frau*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将拉丁方临界集用于秘密共享方案，利用自仿射群轨道解决信息持有者缺失问题，并提出计算具有给定仿射自同构群的临界集这一更一般问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于临界集的秘密共享方案中，若某些信息片段被多个临界集共用，一旦持有者缺失就无法恢复秘密。需要一种方法来克服这种对特定持有者的依赖。

Method: 利用拉丁方自仿射群（autoparatopism group）描述的条目轨道；提出计算含指定仿射自同构群的临界集问题；证明临界集仅依赖于该仿射自同构的共轭类和拉丁方的主类；以不超过六阶的拉丁方为例计算临界集的最小和最大规模。

Result: 确定了不超过六阶拉丁方中与自仿射自同构相关的临界集的最小与最大规模，并将该方法实现于一种新的秘密共享方案设计中。

Conclusion: 自仿射群轨道方法可有效解决秘密共享中信息持有者缺失的问题，且临界集规模由仿射自同构共轭类与拉丁方主类决定。

Abstract: In cryptography, critical sets of Latin squares have particularly been implemented to design secret sharing schemes. A main problem in these cryptographic protocols arises from absent holders of pieces of information that are common to different critical sets, because they become indispensable to recover the secret. This paper solves this problem by making use of the orbits of entries described by the autoparatopism group of the Latin square under consideration. To this end, we introduce the more general problem of computing critical sets of Latin squares having a given paratopism in their autoparatopism group. These critical sets depend only on the conjugacy class of the autoparatopism and the main class of the Latin square under consideration. Based on this fact, as an illustrative example, we determine the smallest and largest sizes of critical sets associated with autoparatopisms of Latin squares of order up to six. We implement this approach in the design of a new secret sharing scheme.

</details>


### [144] [Et Tu, MacBook? Unprivileged Keystroke Inference and Context Profiling via the Built-in IMU Side Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21569)
*Jiaji He,Yi Shi,Junfeng Cai,Chang Liu,Yongqiang Lyu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示 Apple MacBook 内置 IMU 的侧信道漏洞，提出首个非特权侧信道攻击 BRUTUS，可推断按键、桌面环境与用户身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MacBook 为设备姿态与运动感知在内置机身上集成了 IMU，但该 IMU 会无意中捕获用户交互和环境引起的细微振动。这些信号构成一个此前未被探索的新型侧信道，且可通过非 root 权限的 IOKit 驱动及两个无内容系统元数据接口（HIDIdleTime、CGEventSource）被访问，因此亟需研究其泄漏范围与安全影响。

Method: 作者首先发现经 IOKit 驱动实现非 root 访问 IMU 数据，并结合 HIDIdleTime 与 CGEventSource 增强泄漏；随后对 IMU 数据进行严格表征，揭示三个维度的泄漏：按键身份、桌面表面、用户行为。基于此提出 BRUTUS——首个针对 Apple MacBook 内置 IMU 的非特权侧信道攻击，利用 IMU 信号并以语言模型辅助重建。

Result: BRUTUS 在按键恢复上达到 89.1%–97.5% 的字符级准确率；借助语言模型可对部分句子实现 100% 准确重建；在用户识别与环境画像中，无需标签即可正确发现用户和环境 profile，并将后续片段正确归入对应 profile。

Conclusion: 该工作表明内置 IMU 会泄漏按键、环境与用户身份等多维敏感信息，凸显对内置 IMU 传感器访问进行严格监管的紧迫必要性。

Abstract: Recent generations of Apple MacBooks embed an inertial measurement unit (IMU) within their unibody chassis for device orientation and motion sensing. However, this IMU inadvertently captures not only intended device-level information but also subtle physical vibrations from user interactions and the surrounding environment. These signals establish a novel, previously unexplored side channel. We uncover a vulnerability allowing non-root access to IMU data via an IOKit driver, alongside two content-free system metadata interfaces (HIDIdleTime and CGEventSource) that further enrich the side-channel leakage. Through rigorous characterization of the IMU data, we reveal that the leakage spans three core dimensions: (1) keystroke identity (which key is typed), (2) desk surface (where the laptop is placed), and (3) user behavior (who is typing). Leveraging these findings, we introduce BRUTUS, the first comprehensive unprivileged side-channel attack targeting built-in IMU sensors on Apple MacBooks. BRUTUS achieves a character-level accuracy of 89.1% to 97.5% in key recovery. Furthermore, aided by language models, it can successfully reconstruct certain sentences with 100% accuracy. For user identification and environment profiling, BRUTUS correctly discovers user and environment profiles without labels and correctly assigns subsequent segments to their corresponding profiles. Ultimately, this work highlights the urgent necessity of strictly regulating access to built-in IMU sensors.

</details>


### [145] [Micro-Collaborative Poisoning: A Distributed Attack on RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21573)
*Pedro Pereira,Eva Maia,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出\"微协作投毒\"（Micro-Collaborative Poisoning）：将虚假目标主张拆分到多篇局部合理的文档中，而非集中于单一恶意段落，并在108种RAG配置下评估其攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过外部知识源为LLM输出提供事实依据，但这一依赖也带来了投毒攻击面。既有直接投毒方式依赖单篇恶意文档，易被文档级审查发现，因此需要研究更隐蔽的分布式投毒威胁。

Method: 提出微协作投毒攻击：把虚假目标主张分散到多篇各自看似合理的文档中，形成跨文档的弱对抗信号。在108种RAG配置上进行评估，变量包括数据集、检索器架构、检索深度（top-k）、数据库构成、被投毒数据库数量以及生成器模型，并做文档级投毒可见性分析。

Result: 攻击效果并非由单一主导毒文档驱动，而是来自检索来源中弱对抗信号的累积。增大top-k、投毒多个数据库会增加这些信号同时出现在检索上下文中的概率；干净数据库的多样性、更强的检索器可降低其影响。文档级可见性分析表明该威胁难以通过孤立文档检查发现，其显式投毒特征弱于直接投毒。

Conclusion: 微协作投毒是一种隐蔽且有效的RAG安全威胁，其危害源于跨文档信号累积而非单点恶意内容，因此需要超越单文档审查的防御视角，关注检索上下文的整体一致性与来源多样性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves large language models by grounding outputs in external knowledge sources, but this dependency also creates a surface for poisoning attacks. This paper introduces Micro-Collaborative Poisoning, a distributed attack in which a false target claim is divided across multiple locally plausible documents instead of being concentrated in a single malicious passage. We evaluate the attack across 108 RAG configurations by varying dataset, retriever architecture, retrieval depth, database composition, number of poisoned databases, and generator model. The results indicate that Micro-Collaborative Poisoning is not driven by a single dominant poisoned passage, but by the accumulation of weak adversarial signals across retrieved sources. Increasing top-$k$ and poisoning multiple databases make it more likely that these signals will appear together in the retrieved context, while clean database diversity and stronger retrievers can reduce their influence. The document-level poisoning visibility analysis further shows that this threat is difficult to expose through isolated document inspection, since Micro-Collaborative Poisoning achieves downstream influence while leaving a weaker explicit poisoning signature than direct poisoning.

</details>


### [146] [CIPL: A Channel-Aware Framework for Recoverable Privacy Leakage in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21686)
*Tao Huang,Guosen Wu,Guolong Zheng,Jiayang Meng,Chen Hou,Xu Yang,Xuechao Yang,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CIPL（通道反转隐私泄露）框架，以通道感知方式在黑盒条件下评估LLM智能体隐私泄露，揭示存储标签无法决定信息可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体隐私评估多局限于记忆、检索或工具调用等单一组件，难以区分"内部暴露"与"外部观察者实际可恢复的信息"，导致评估口径不一致、跨管线不可比。

Method: CIPL将目标建模为敏感源→选择→组装→执行→观察→抽取六个阶段，在统一协议下度量从"被选中的敏感单元"到"攻击者可恢复输出"的转化；覆盖记忆型、检索中介型、工具中介型目标，并补充BrowserUse实时智能体案例；另采用分层语义审计。

Result: 记忆型目标接近饱和的参照情形；检索中介型泄露常为部分泄露；工具中介型与实时智能体泄露强度高度依赖观察面、提示-通道对齐度、检索深度与提供方行为；分层语义审计可发现精确匹配遗漏的攻击者可用披露。

Conclusion: CIPL为跨异构智能体管线比较"内部敏感依赖如何转化为外部可恢复泄露"提供了统一框架，表明存储层面的标注不足以判定实际泄露风险。

Abstract: Privacy leakage in LLM agents is commonly evaluated within individual components such as memory, retrieval, or tool-use pipelines, which makes it difficult to distinguish internal exposure from information that an external observer can actually recover. We present CIPL (Channel Inversion for Privacy Leakage), a channel-aware evaluation framework for black-box privacy leakage in LLM agents. CIPL represents a target through sensitive source, selection, assembly, execution, observation, and extraction stages and evaluates the transition from selected sensitive units to attacker-recoverable output under a shared protocol. Experiments across memory-based, retrieval-mediated, and tool-mediated targets, together with a BrowserUse live-agent case study, show that storage labels alone do not determine recoverability. Memory targets form a near-saturated reference case, retrieval-mediated leakage is frequently partial, and tool-mediated and live-agent leakage varies strongly with observation surface, prompt-to-channel alignment, retrieval depth, and provider behavior. A stratified semantic audit further identifies attacker-useful disclosures that canonical exact matching misses. CIPL therefore provides a common framework for comparing how internal sensitive dependence is realized as externally recoverable leakage across heterogeneous agent pipelines.

</details>


### [147] [TERMon: Detecting Persistent Behavioral Threats in Edge AI via Hardware-Native Ternary Runtime Monitor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21713)
*Arish Sateesan,Edlira Dushku*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TERMon是一种轻量级硬件运行时监控器，通过观察推理行为而非重执行或形式化验证来检测边缘AI加速器异常。其将类条件可信行为表示为三值模式，并与温度计编码指纹并行匹配，检测有害权重损坏时按行为影响程度成比例报警。在PYNQ-Z2 FPGA上实现，无需BRAM或DSP，决策延迟两周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI加速器广泛部署于安全关键环境，模型输出可能控制执行器、门禁或报警。模型损坏、分布偏移和对抗输入仍可产生格式良好且高置信度的预测，导致运行时故障难以察觉，因此需要轻量级的运行时异常检测机制。

Method: 提出TERMon硬件运行时监控器，通过观察推理行为而非重执行或形式化验证来检测异常。将类条件可信行为表示为硬件高效的三值模式，与温度计编码指纹并行匹配；三值编码可精确复现对应的未量化范围判定。在PYNQ-Z2 FPGA上实现流水线设计。

Result: TERMon对有害权重损坏的检测与其行为影响成比例；但在严格假阳性工作点下，分布外与对抗输入基本无法通过所监控特征区分。实现于PYNQ-Z2 FPGA，流水线设计无需片上块RAM或DSP，决策延迟为两周期。

Conclusion: TERMon以极低硬件开销实现了对权重损坏引发的异常推理的有效检测，但对分布外与对抗输入的检测能力有限，表明基于行为特征的监控在安全关键部署中仍需与其他机制配合。

Abstract: Edge AI accelerators are increasingly deployed in safety-critical environments, where model outputs may control physical actuators, make access-control decisions, or trigger alarms. In these settings, runtime failures often remain undetected because model corruption, distribution shift, and adversarial inputs can still produce well-formed, confident predictions. This paper presents TERMon, a lightweight hardware runtime monitor that detects such anomalies by observing inference behavior rather than re-executing or formally verifying the model. TERMon represents class-conditional trusted behavior as hardware-efficient ternary patterns that are matched in parallel against a thermometer-encoded fingerprint. The ternary encoding reproduces the corresponding unquantized range decision exactly. TERMon detects harmful weight corruptions in proportion to their behavioral impact, while out-of-distribution and adversarial inputs are largely not separable using the monitored features at a strict false-positive operating point. We implemented TERMon on a PYNQ-Z2 FPGA, and the pipelined design requires no on-chip block RAM or DSPs and has a two-cycle decision latency.

</details>


### [148] [A Framework to Quantify the Probability of Future Cyber Loss Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21717)
*Siem Peters,Martin Eian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出LEFSA概率框架，将损失事件频率估计重构为机器级网络损失事件预测与分层聚合，验证了操作遥测的预测价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络风险量化受限于操作数据稀缺以及损失事件频率难以量化，亟需可扩展、可解释且可落地的风险估计方法。

Method: LEFSA从操作安全遥测中估计经校准的机器级CLE概率，并在考虑机器间依赖的前提下跨基础设施层级聚合，支持机器、服务、业务流程及组织层面的风险估计。

Result: 基于23家组织使用Microsoft Defender for Endpoint的专有MDR遥测评估，XGBoost预测性能最强，平均AUC达0.90，各评估期校准误差均较低。

Conclusion: 操作安全遥测对未来CLE发生具有显著预测信息，概率化机器级建模与分层聚合可作为定量、数据驱动网络风险管理的可行基础。

Abstract: Cybersecurity risk quantification remains challenging due to limited operational data and difficulties in quantifying Loss Event Frequency (LEF). This paper introduces the Loss Event Frequency Security Analyser (LEFSA), a probabilistic framework that reformulates LEF estimation as machine-level Cyber Loss Event (CLE) prediction combined with hierarchical infrastructure-level aggregation. LEFSA estimates calibrated machine-level CLE probabilities from operational cybersecurity telemetry and aggregates them across infrastructure layers while accounting for machine-level dependencies. This provides a foundation for scalable, explainable, and operationally applicable cyber risk estimation at the level of machines, services, business processes, and the entire organization. The framework was evaluated using proprietary Managed Detection & Response telemetry from 23 organizations using Microsoft Defender for Endpoint. XGBoost achieved the strongest predictive performance, with a mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.90 and consistently low calibration error across evaluation periods. The results demonstrate that operational cybersecurity telemetry contains substantial predictive information for future CLE occurrence, supporting probabilistic machine-level modeling and hierarchical aggregation as a promising foundation for quantitative, data-driven cyber risk management.

</details>


### [149] [Verifiable Computation with Trusted Execution Environments and On-Chain Digital Rights Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21728)
*Bingle Stegmann Kruger,Co-Pierre Georg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于TEE与数字权利代币（DRT）的架构，使数据所有者可将私有数据汇入数据池，并通过分布式账本发放受限计算权利，让第三方分析者在TEE内对封存数据执行开源代码并获取结果而无法访问原始数据。本文主张以"事前控制"为核心的隐私观，并以WASM/Python任务在Solana上记录兑换的参考实现验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护方案多聚焦于数据发布后或访问过程中的保护，而数据所有者难以在数据被处理之前保留对处理方式的事前控制权。作者由此提出以"控制"为中心的隐私视角，希望通过技术手段让数据创造者继续掌控其数据如何被组合与计算。

Method: 架构设计结合三类技术：（1）可信执行环境（TEE）实现数据的封存与安全计算；（2）数字权利代币（DRT）作为窄范围的计算权利凭证，将特定开源代码与某一数据池绑定；（3）分布式账本用于DRT的发行与兑换记录。分析者获得的是在TEE内对合并封存数据执行指定开源代码的权利，并仅接收执行结果，而非底层数据。参考实现以WebAssembly（WASM）/Python作业运行于封存数据集之上，并在Solana上记录兑换。

Result: 参考实现成功演示了在封存数据集上运行WASM/Python作业并在Solana上记录兑换的流程，从而说明了该平台在技术上的可行性及其固有局限。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种以数据所有者事前控制为核心的隐私架构，借助TEE、DRT与分布式账本实现受限计算权利的发行与兑换，使分析者能获得计算结果而无法接触原始数据，为隐私保护数据协作提供了可行路径并揭示了其局限。

Abstract: We present an architecture that enables data owners to combine private data into data pools using Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) and manage these pools by issuing narrowly scoped computational rights, encoded as Digital Rights Tokens (DRTs), to third-party data analysts. Each DRT binds specific open-source code to a pool and is issued and redeemed on a distributed ledger. Data analysts can obtain the right to execute open-source code on the combined sealed data inside a TEE and receive the result from this code execution, but not the underlying data. We argue for a control-centric view of privacy in which creators retain ex ante control over how their data is processed. A reference implementation runs WebAssembly (WASM)/Python jobs over sealed datasets and records redemptions on Solana, illustrating the feasibility and limitations of the platform.

</details>


### [150] [CASCADE Against Jailbreaks: Combination Across Stages with Controlled Attack-Defense Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21793)
*Jiale Luo,Eric Han*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究LLM越狱防御在流水线不同阶段（输入改写、输出守卫等）内部及跨阶段的组合策略，在统一的直接、黑盒、单轮攻击威胁模型下，标准化攻击成功率定义与查询预算，跨19种攻击与15种防御给出分层防御的实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对LLM越狱攻击的防御分布在流水线不同阶段，但各阶段该部署何种防御、如何组合尚不明确；既有实证研究因攻击成功率定义与实验设置不一致而彼此割裂，且大多孤立评估单个防御，缺乏可比的系统性结论。

Method: 提出决策框架：在统一威胁模型（直接、黑盒、单轮攻击）下，通过原则化的攻击成功率公式与受控查询预算标准化评估，并制定明确的公平性规则；据此在同一流水线内及跨阶段系统地组合评估防御。

Result: 在19种攻击与15种防御的组合实验中，未发现任何单一防御在所有情况下最优；但精心选择的防御组合能够以极小的效用损失获得显著的安全提升。

Conclusion: 防御不存在普适最优解，分层组合是可行路径；本文为分层防御流水线的构建提供实践建议，并确立了可复现、可比较的评估规范。

Abstract: Defenses against jailbreak attacks on Large Language Models (LLMs) operate at different pipeline stages, such as input modification or output guard, but it remains unclear which defenses to deploy at each stage and how to combine them. Prior empirical studies, fragmented by inconsistent attack-success-rate definitions and experimental settings, have evaluated defenses largely in isolation. Here we present the first systematic study, to our knowledge, of defense combinations both within and across pipeline stages, under a consistent threat model of direct, black-box, single-turn attacks. Our decision framework standardizes evaluation through a principled attack-success-rate formulation with controlled query budgets, together with explicit fairness rules. Across 19 attacks and 15 defenses, we find that no single defense is universally best, but well-chosen combinations achieve substantial safety with minimal utility degradation, yielding practical recommendations for layered defense pipelines.

</details>


### [151] [Watermarkable Multi-Draft Speculative Sampling via Poisson Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21858)
*Yanxiao Liu,Sicheng Wan,Zhan Gao,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于泊松过程的多草稿投机采样算法，在提升推理效率的同时可嵌入无偏水印，且不损失投机接受率，是首个兼具水印强度与采样效率的多草稿、草稿器不变投机采样方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署中推理效率与输出溯源（可通过投机采样与水印分别实现）都很重要，但已有研究表明二者结合十分困难甚至可能不可行，因此需要新方法来改善这一根本性权衡。

Method: 提出基于泊松过程的多草稿投机采样算法，构建精确的"无通信列表耦合"方案，由此获得草稿器不变性；在此基础上嵌入无偏水印，同时不降低投机接受率。

Result: 算法自身具有强采样效率，可自然支持水印；实验验证其在水印强度与采样效率两方面均表现优异。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现多草稿、草稿器不变的投机采样方案，同时保持水印强度与采样效率，改善了效率与溯源之间的权衡前沿。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of tasks, motivating two important aspects of deployment: inference efficiency and output provenance, which can be tackled by speculative sampling and watermarking, respectively. However, recent works have shown that combining these two goals is highly nontrivial and can be potentially impossible. In this work, we develop a novel multi-draft speculative sampling algorithm based on Poisson processes that improves the frontier of this fundamental trade-off. The proposed algorithm has strong sampling efficiency on its own and, more interestingly, is naturally watermarkable: we can embed an unbiased watermark without degrading speculative acceptance. Moreover, our algorithm is based on an exact list-coupling-without-communication scheme, which yields a drafter invariance property that benefits both sampling and watermarking. It is the first multi-draft, drafter-invariant speculative sampling scheme that maintains both watermark strength and sampling efficiency, and we experimentally verify its strong performance in both aspects.

</details>


### [152] [SFPF: Spatio-Frequency Polarization Fingerprint for Anomalous Wireless Device Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21873)
*Xiaoxuan Huang,Jinlong Xu,Daoyuan Shen,Meng Zhang,Dong Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出空频极化指纹（SFPF），通过在多个频率和观测方向上联合表征复极化响应来检测被替换的无线设备硬件，仿真与实测均优于传统射频指纹和极化指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署无线设备可能被未授权替换硬件，而替换件仍保留通信功能、凭证与逻辑身份，导致异常设备难以检测。传统射频指纹（RFF）在替换硬件与合法硬件高度相似时区分度不足；单方向构造的极化指纹（PF）会遗漏空间非均匀的极化变化。

Method: 提出空频极化指纹（SFPF），在多个频率和观测方向上联合表示复极化响应，传统PF视为其固定方向切片。从硬件相关的模态激励、定向远场辐射与极化投影推导SFPF的形成过程；通过一阶灵敏度分析表明同一硬件变化引起的响应随频率和方向变化，从而论证联合空频采集的必要性。

Result: 电磁仿真验证了非均匀的空频灵敏度，并在相同观测预算下，SFPF相较PF在归一化距离、Fisher分数和类间/类内比上分别提升17.7%、45.8%和11.3%。实验显示SFPF在0–20 dB范围内持续优于RFF和PF；在15–20 dB时，SFPF对异常设备的F1分数达87.3–90.4%，AUROC达85.4–95.5%。

Conclusion: SFPF通过联合空频极化响应提升了异常设备检测性能，是比RFF和PF更有效的硬件一致性校验指纹，适用于受控测量条件下的定期巡检。

Abstract: Periodic inspection of deployed wireless devices is necessary because unauthorized hardware replacement may preserve communication functions, credentials, and logical identity, making anomalous devices difficult to detect. Such inspections are conducted under controlled measurement conditions to verify that each device remains consistent with its enrolled hardware state. Conventional radio-frequency fingerprint (RFF) may provide insufficient separation when replacement hardware closely resembles legitimate hardware, while a polarization fingerprint (PF) constructed at one observation direction may miss spatially nonuniform polarization changes. This paper proposes the spatio-frequency polarization fingerprint (SFPF), which jointly represents complex polarization responses over multiple frequencies and observation directions; conventional PF is its fixed-direction slice. We derive SFPF formation from hardware-dependent modal excitation, directional far-field radiation, and polarization projection. A first-order sensitivity analysis shows that the response to the same hardware change varies with both frequency and direction, motivating joint spatio-frequency acquisition. Electromagnetic simulations confirm the nonuniform spatio-frequency sensitivity and show that, under the same observation budget, SFPF improves normalized distance, Fisher score, and the inter-/intra-class ratio over PF by 17.7%, 45.8%, and 11.3%, respectively. Experiments show that SFPF consistently outperforms RFF and PF over 0--20~dB. At 15--20~dB, SFPF achieves anomalous-device F1 scores of 87.3--90.4% and AUROC values of 85.4--95.5%.

</details>


### [153] [End-to-End Hard-Label Cryptanalytic Model Extraction Using Efficient Sign Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21941)
*Akira Ito,Takayuki Miura,Yosuke Todo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对Eurocrypt 2025 Carlini等人提出的ReLU-MLP硬标签模型提取中的符号恢复瓶颈，提出一种无需专用查询的新符号恢复算法，实验精度更高，并首次在MNIST/Fashion-MNIST训练模型上实现全黑盒端到端提取，标签一致率超98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN参数是宝贵资产，硬标签设置下仅能观察最终标签，是最困难的模型提取场景。已有工作（Eurocrypt 2025）虽实现多项式时间硬标签提取ReLU-MLP，但其符号恢复步骤需要大量查询和计算，难以在黑盒下实现，导致对已训练深层ReLU MLP的全黑盒端到端演示仍属空白。

Method: 提出基于与现有方法完全不同原理的新符号恢复算法，无需为符号恢复设计专用查询，从而可与其余提取步骤统一在黑盒条件下实现。

Result: 符号恢复精度高于现有方法；对已训练模型也能高效完成符号恢复；结合各步骤实现全黑盒端到端提取，在MNIST与Fashion-MNIST上、宽度16、4或6个隐藏层的模型上达到98%以上标签一致率。

Conclusion: 该符号恢复算法使硬标签模型提取的全部步骤均可在黑盒下实现，首次完成对已训练深层ReLU MLP的端到端黑盒提取，验证了硬标签提取方法的实用性与威胁性。

Abstract: The importance of deep neural networks (DNNs) is widely recognized, and the parameters obtained through training are regarded as valuable assets. Recently, attacks that extract these parameters using only oracle queries to a DNN have been actively studied at IACR conferences. The hard-label setting is the most challenging setting for model extraction, where an adversary can observe only the final output label, such as "dog" or "cat." At Eurocrypt 2025, Carlini et al. proposed polynomial-time hard-label extraction of ReLU-based MLPs. However, one step of this attack process, i.e., sign recovery, requires a large number of queries and substantial computation. Implementing this step in a black-box setting remains difficult. Consequently, a fully black-box end-to-end demonstration on trained deep ReLU MLPs has remained a challenge. In this paper, we propose a new sign-recovery algorithm based on a completely different principle from the existing method. Our method requires no dedicated queries for sign recovery. In our experiments, it achieves higher sign-recovery accuracy than the existing method. Consequently, it enables efficient sign recovery even for trained models. With our sign-recovery algorithm, all steps of hard-label model extraction can be implemented in a black-box setting. By combining these implementations, we demonstrate end-to-end model extraction from models trained on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST, with width 16 and 4 or 6 hidden layers, achieving over 98% label agreement.

</details>


### [154] [Provisional Reachability: Containing Agents by Making Every Crossing Revocable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21957)
*Yoshiaki Takashita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在一个已确立的"每周期比特数"泄漏单位下，提出以"暂存+概率审计+撤销"为核心的防御机制，推导出每窗口泄漏上界 L(r)≈c/(er)，并刻画了保密衰减率阈值 μ>g/L 使 L 比特秘密不可达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作确立了防御者必须阻断的量具有"比特/周期"单位，但未给出具体方案。本文旨在真正"设定该速率"，即构造并量化一种能将该泄漏速率压低的机制。

Method: 将每个跨越项暂存一个周期，以概率 r 独立审计，若发现异常则撤销该窗口；在对抗者选择 k 次跨越、每次携带 c 比特的模型下最大化期望泄漏 kc(1-r)^k，得到最优 k*=1/ln(1/(1-r)) 与上界 L(r)~c/(er)。进一步引入秘密每周期以比例 μ 衰减的假设，分析持有量的收敛与可达性；并对比欺骗性防御（表面名称说谎）、拒绝提供参考、入口与指称加密等手段。

Result: 上界为对抗者选择上的上确界，故方案可公开；仿真与理论吻合至 7.7 个标准误。仅暂存无法阻止秘密在每次运行中组装。加入 μ 衰减后持有量收敛至 g/μ，当 μ>g/L 时 L 比特秘密不可达，阈值尖锐（0.9μ* 时 100% 运行组装成功，2μ* 时 0%，共 20,000 窗口）。欺骗性手段失败：名称说谎的表面在 18/18 个读者上保持准确率；拒绝提供参考则无人愿承诺。入口加密仅隐藏 0.10 比特功能信息，指称加密隐藏 3.00 比特中的 2.64 且随机准确率，但 100% 读者仍认为其为普通 Python。审计率方差应被消除（收益对 r 凸），激活预算则应加入方差（存活率乘性，固定均值下灭绝率 70%→100%）。整体将泄漏从 100,000 降至 59 比特，因子达 1,704，保留 12% 合法工作量；将窗口绑定到调用者可恢复至 100% 且不增加泄漏，代价是界限变为按主体计。对 65 个只读工具，暂存后每调用仍余 2,400 比特，因子为 10 而非无穷。

Conclusion: 该机制给出了可公开、仿真可验证的泄漏速率上界，并借由秘密衰减使大秘密在阈值外不可达；但其保护是速率而非总量，单一手段存在结构性残余泄漏（如只读工具仍为 10 倍而非无穷），需通过绑定主体等配置在泄漏与合法功能间取折衷。

Abstract: A companion paper found that what a defender must block over time has units: bits per period [Takashita, 2026a]. This paper sets it. Hold every crossing in escrow for one period, audit each held item independently with probability r, and revoke the window if any audit catches something. An adversary crossing k times, each carrying c bits, expects kc(1-r)^k, maximised at k* = 1/ln(1/(1-r)), a bound of L(r) ~ c/(er) per window. The bound is a supremum over the adversary's choice, so the scheme may be public; simulation matches it to 7.7 standard errors. It is a rate, not a total: escrow alone still lets the secret assemble in every run. But if the secret decays at a fraction mu of held bits per period, holdings converge to g/mu at any horizon, so an L-bit secret is unreachable once mu > g/L -- an error threshold in Eigen's sense, sharp where the closed form puts it (100% of runs assemble at 0.9mu*, 0% at 2mu*, over 20,000 windows). Deception that needs the adversary to reason badly fails: a surface whose names lie left accuracy at 18 of 18, and 100% at three reader strengths. Withholding reference works, and differently: no reader would commit at all. Keying the entry points hides 0.10 bits of what a module does; keying the denotation hides 2.64 of 3.00 at chance accuracy, while 100% of readers still call it ordinary Python. Variance must be removed from the audit rate, where loot is convex in r, and added to the activation budget, where survival is multiplicative: extinction 70% to 100% at a fixed mean. End to end the stack takes the leak from 100,000 to 59 bits, a factor of 1,704, leaving 12% of legitimate work standing; keying the window to the caller restores that to 100% at no cost in leakage, at the price of a bound that is per principal. Of 65 read-only tools, escrow leaves 2,400 bits per call: a factor of 10, not infinity.

</details>


### [155] [The Supersingular Isogeny Problem in Time and Memory $p^{1/3+o(1)}$, Unconditionally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22018)
*José Luis Delgado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个求解超奇异椭圆曲线 $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ 问题的 Las Vegas 算法，在无光滑性启发式假设下达到 $p^{1/3+o(1)}$ 的期望时间与内存复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ 问题可归约到超奇异自同态环问题与同源问题，具有重要密码学意义。此前无条件指数为 $2/5$，Wesolowski 在关于小次数分解的假设下达到 $1/3$，因此需要一个不依赖启发式假设、无条件达到 $1/3$ 指数的算法。

Method: 算法预先固定一族由小素数乘积构成的次数。利用已知计数结果，得到从曲线到其 Frobenius 共轭曲线的大量该次数的同源；通过碰撞估计证明这些同源分布在足够多不同曲线上，使得随机游走能以高概率到达其中一条曲线。在该曲线上，算法将次数拆分为两部分，枚举两个较短同源列表，并匹配它们的目标以获得一条到共轭曲线的同源，将其与 Frobenius 复合即得所需自同态。

Result: 得到 Las Vegas 算法，期望时间与内存为 $p^{1/3}\exp\bigl(O(\sqrt{\log p\,\log\log p})\bigr)=p^{1/3+o(1)}$，且分析不依赖光滑性启发式假设，属无条件结果。

Conclusion: 该工作无条件地将 $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ 问题的复杂度指数改进至 $1/3$，与 Wesolowski 的条件性结果指数持平，同时通过去启发式假设增强了结果的严格性。

Abstract: Given a supersingular elliptic curve $E/\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$, the $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ problem asks for a non-scalar endomorphism of $E$. By known reductions, solving this problem also solves the supersingular endomorphism ring and isogeny problems. Wesolowski obtained exponent $1/3$ under an assumption on the factorization of a small degree, whereas the previous unconditional exponent was $2/5$. We give a Las Vegas algorithm, analyzed without a smoothness heuristic, with expected time and memory \[ p^{1/3}\exp\bigl(O(\sqrt{\log p\,\log\log p})\bigr) = p^{1/3+o(1)}. \]
  The algorithm fixes in advance a family of degrees that are products of small primes. Known counting results provide many isogenies of these degrees from curves to their Frobenius conjugates, and a collision estimate shows that the isogenies occur on sufficiently many distinct curves for a random walk to reach one of them. From such a curve, the algorithm splits a degree into two parts, enumerates two lists of shorter isogenies, and matches their targets to obtain an isogeny to the conjugate, whose composition with Frobenius gives the required endomorphism.

</details>


### [156] [APort Vault: Benchmarking AI Agent Payment Authorization with the Open Agent Passport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22076)
*Uchi Uchibeke*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: APort Vault 是一个面向工具型 AI 智能体支付授权能力的基准，基于公开夺旗活动中人类编写的 4,371 条攻击，在 14 个模型、5 种策略配置和 2 条回放轨道上完成 225,964 次评估，并对比有无确定性前置检查（OAP 规范）的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准常以单一聚合数字衡量安全表现，无法反映模型与策略配置的差异，也无法分离"拒绝支付"与"正确授权"两种机制，因此需要一个多事件、多配置、可复核的支付授权评估基准。

Method: 采用人类在公开夺旗活动中针对实时支付智能体编写的 4,371 条攻击，跨 14 个模型（8 家机构）、5 种策略配置、2 条回放轨道进行回放；设置有无确定性前置检查（实现 Open Agent Passport, OAP 规范）两组对照；每次评估记录五个独立事件，并对模型、提示、轨道进行匹配三元组比较。

Result: 请求率在配置间差异远大于模型间：L1 为 10.9%、L2 为 3.0%、L3 为 0.1%、L4 为 79.4%。在 1,293 条 L4 提示上各模型请求率为 71.2%–84.3%，809 条（62.6%）被全部 14 个模型请求。授权边界上，L2–L4 向非许可收款方转账：仅模型为 140/76,842，加前置层后为 0/69,297；匹配三元组为 105 对 0，零违规覆盖 790 个源会话，单会话上界 0.38%。该零并非靠拒绝支付取得：前置层执行 25,370 笔支付，策略在评估的 25,640 次转账调用中拒绝 187 次，其中 148 次为禁止收款方。

Conclusion: 支付授权基准必须拆分多事件评估并区分策略配置，否则聚合指标难以通过复核；OAP 确定性前置检查可在不牺牲正常支付的前提下将越权转账降至零，相关评估数据、层级护照、评分代码与分析脚本已开源发布。

Abstract: APort Vault is a benchmark for payment authorization in tool-using AI agents. It replays 4,371 attacks written by humans against a live payment agent during a public capture-the-flag event, across 14 models from 8 labs, five policy configurations and two replay tracks, with and without a deterministic pre-action check implementing the Open Agent Passport (OAP) specification. 225,964 evaluations completed. We report five distinct events per evaluation, because collapsing them is how an agent benchmark produces a number that does not survive review.
  Requests are common and their rate differs far more across configurations than across models, though each attack exists at exactly one configuration so policy and attack cohort vary together: 10.9% of model-alone evaluations at Level 1, 3.0% at Level 2, 0.1% at Level 3, 79.4% at Level 4. On the 1,293 Level 4 prompts, each evaluated on every model, request rates run from 71.2% to 84.3%, and 809 prompts (62.6%) elicited a request from all fourteen models, each ending in a successful payment to the level's allowlisted recipient.
  The authorization boundary is where the conditions diverge. At Levels 2 to 4, transfers to recipients the passport did not permit number 140 of 76,842 with the model alone and 0 of 69,297 behind the layer, and 105 against 0 on 68,970 matched model, prompt and track triples. The zero spans 790 source sessions, giving a per-session upper bound of 0.38%. It was not obtained by refusing payments: 25,370 payments executed behind the layer, while the policy denied 187 of the 25,640 transfer calls it evaluated, 148 of them for a forbidden recipient.
  We release the 225,964 evaluations, the level passports, the scoring code and the analysis script at huggingface.co/datasets/aporthq/vault-benchmark-v1 .

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Extreme classification: beating chance with one training example from each class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20897)
*Kevin Bleakley,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究一个最小分类问题：能否在 P≠Q 时严格优于随机猜测。构造随机核规则使期望精度为 1/2+MMD_k²/4，并由可数二元问题族得到特征核；给出 R 上的确定性次序规则与可数生成空间上的确定性无分布规则；证明在未知不平衡先验下无通用规则，且自适应选择目标类时除公平硬币外任何规则都可能严格劣于随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在仅有少量标签观测、目标样本来自两个未知分布的极简设定下，是否存在普适地严格优于随机猜测的分类规则，从而刻画该统计问题的可学习性与不可能性边界。

Method: 分析最近邻规则对多元高斯分布的表现；构造固定随机化核规则，将期望精度与核最大均值差异 MMD_k² 联系起来；借助可数族可测二元问题构造可数生成可测空间上的特征核；在实数轴上证明确定性次序规则优于随机；通过可测编码将结果推广至可数生成可测空间；并考察未知不平衡类先验与自适应目标类选择下的下界。

Result: 1-最近邻对均值不同且共正定协方差阵的多元高斯分布成功；但对实轴上光滑密度也可能严格劣于随机。所构造的随机核规则期望精度恰为 1/2+MMD_k²(P,Q)/4；实数轴确定性次序规则对任意不同 Borel 概率测度优于随机；确定性无分布规则在任意可数生成可测空间（含可分度量空间）优于随机。不存在对任意不同分布对与未知不平衡先验均有效的规则；自适应目标类选择下，除公平硬币外任何规则对某有限支撑分布对严格劣于随机。

Conclusion: 该问题在温和条件下（如可数生成可测空间）可实现优于随机的分类，且精度可由 MMD 精确刻画；但普适性受限于类先验信息与自适应选择，说明先验平衡性或选择机制是严格优于随机的关键。

Abstract: We study a minimal classification problem: Given independent labeled observations $X\sim P$ and $Z\sim Q$ from two unknown distributions $P,Q$, and given an independent target $Y$ drawn with equal probability from $P$ or $Q$, can one classify $Y$ strictly better than chance whenever $P\neq Q$? The one-nearest-neighbor rule succeeds for every pair of multivariate Gaussian distributions with distinct means and a common positive-definite covariance matrix but can perform strictly worse than chance even for smooth densities on the real line. We construct a fixed randomized kernel rule whose expected accuracy is exactly $1/2+\operatorname{MMD}_k^2(P,Q)/4$, and obtain characteristic kernels on countably generated measurable spaces from countable families of measurable binary questions. We also prove that a deterministic order rule on $\mathbb R$ beats chance for every pair of distinct Borel probability measures. A measurable encoding then gives a deterministic distribution-free rule which beats chance on every countably generated measurable space, in particular every separable metric space. Finally, we show that no rule works for every distinct pair of distributions and every unknown unbalanced class prior; under adaptive target-class selection, every rule other than a fair coin is strictly worse than chance for some finitely supported pair.

</details>


### [158] [Complex Problem Solving in Large Language Models: A Statistical Control Survey and Diagnostic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20973)
*Jiazhang Cai,Tao Wang,Ruidong Zhang,Siyuan Li,Terry Ma,Luyang Fang,Haoran Lu,Huimin Cheng,Yingchuan Zhang,Shushan Wu,Rui Xie,Lin Tang,Chao Huang,Rongjie Liu,Ziyu Liu,Meizhi Yu,Yongkai Chen,Yifan Zhou,Zeliang Sun,Chang Liu,Zhen Xiang,Wei Xiao,Zixin Rao,Xinyi Liu,Yutong Hu,Mengrui Zhang,Jing Zhang,Weidi Luo,Jincheng Yu,Zhengliang Liu,Weihang You,Hanqi Jiang,Yi Pan,Junhao Chen,Xinliang Li,Tianming Liu,Wenxuan Zhong,Ping Ma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该综述将大语言模型的复杂问题求解（CPS）重新表述为对潜在解状态的序贯估计-决策问题，把推理视为候选状态转移的生成器，把过程控制视为评估、选择与调节机制，并据此组织方法、解读指标、提出"控制匹配/失配"的诊断假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将LLM的复杂问题求解归因于更强的推理能力或更长的生成，但早期步骤误差放大、提示脆弱性、以及无法修正错误承诺等失败现象无法仅由知识缺失或表达能力不足解释，因此需要一个能同时刻画推理与控制、区分误差与不确定性来源的统一分析框架。

Method: 将CPS建模为控制器在潜在解轨迹上的信念维护与决策过程：随含噪中间证据更新信念，并决定提交、验证、分支、回滚或弃权以最小化期望损失；据此把现有方法归纳为五个组件——显式状态表示、转移结构化、验证与约束执行、搜索与回滚、不确定性管理；同时按所估计的统计量重新解读评价指标；并区分系统性/随机性/不可约误差与认知/偶然不确定性，提出"问题-控制匹配"与其失效形式"控制失配"的诊断假设。

Result: 该框架澄清了现有方法各自估计与控制了什么、哪些环节仍处于失控状态；给出可检验的诊断预测：干预措施在针对观测失败所涉及的误差或不确定性成分时最为有效；并指出例如增加采样只能降低采样变异性，无法改变共享的系统性误差。

Conclusion: 可靠验证、定向恢复、校准不确定性以及预算匹配的评估是当前的核心开放问题；该视角为CPS方法的设计与评测提供了统一的理论坐标。

Abstract: Complex problem solving (CPS) with large language models (LLMs) is often framed as a matter of stronger reasoning or longer generation. Yet early-step error amplification, prompt brittleness, and failures to revise incorrect commitments are difficult to explain by missing knowledge or expressive capacity alone. This survey interprets CPS as a sequential estimation-and-decision problem over a latent solution state. A controller maintains a belief about an unobserved solution trajectory, updates it as noisy intermediate evidence arrives, and decides whether to commit, verify, branch, roll back, or abstain to minimize expected loss. Reasoning supplies candidate transitions and interpretations, whereas process control shapes and evaluates those proposals and regulates subsequent transitions and observations. Within this framework, we organize existing methods around five components: explicit state representation, transition structuring, validation and constraint enforcement, search and rollback, and uncertainty management. We also interpret evaluation metrics according to the statistical quantities they estimate. The framework further yields a diagnostic hypothesis: interventions should be most effective when they target the error or uncertainty component implicated by an observed failure. We distinguish systematic, stochastic, and irreducible error together with epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty, and call this alignment problem-control fit and its failure control mismatch. For example, additional sampling may reduce sampling variability while leaving a shared systematic error unchanged. This perspective clarifies what current methods estimate and control, what remains uncontrolled, and why reliable validation, targeted recovery, calibrated uncertainty, and matched-budget evaluation are central open problems.

</details>


### [159] [A Smoothed Discrepancy Principle for Random Feature Methods and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21017)
*Mike Nguyen,Nicole Mücke*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对非参数回归中的谱正则化方法，提出基于偏差原理的多尺度早停规则，并借助随机特征近似实现计算加速与完全自适应的极小极大最优学习率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典早停方法（如偏差原理）通常无法在所有光滑度水平上实现完全自适应，且在大数据集上计算代价较高；同时神经网络的宽度选择与训练停止时间往往依赖光滑度或容量等先验知识。

Method: 在非参数回归框架下，构建适用于一般核估计器的多尺度早停规则（基于偏差原理）；进一步引入随机特征近似以降低大数据集上的计算成本，并数据驱动地选择随机特征数量；利用随机特征与神经正切核（NTK）机制下神经网络的联系，将停止时间与网络宽度联合选择。

Result: 所提方法在正确设定情形下对所有光滑度水平实现完全自适应；随机特征近似在保持极小极大最优统计保证的同时降低计算成本；可同时自适应选择最优停止时间与所需随机特征数量；并给出数据驱动的网络宽度建议。理论上证明，同时选择的宽度与停止时间可使神经网络在无需光滑度或容量先验知识的情况下达到极小极大最优学习率。

Conclusion: 该工作将谱正则化的自适应早停、随机特征近似与神经网络容量选择统一起来，提供了数据驱动、计算高效且统计最优的方案。

Abstract: We study data-driven early stopping for spectral regularisation methods in the classical non-parametric regression setting. Building on the discrepancy principle, we propose a multi-scale stopping rule that applies to general kernel estimators and show that, unlike previous approaches, it achieves full adaptivity over all smoothness levels in the well-specified case. A key contribution of our work is an extension based on random feature approximations, which reduces computational cost on large datasets while preserving minimax-optimal statistical guarantees. Our procedure not only selects an optimal stopping time but also provides a fully data-driven choice of the number of random features needed to achieve optimal rates. Through the established connection between random features and neural networks in the neural tangent kernel regime, our method further yields a principled, data-driven recommendation for the network width. We prove that the resulting simultaneously chosen width and stopping time allow neural networks to attain minimax-optimal learning rates without prior knowledge of smoothness or capacity parameters.

</details>


### [160] [Triply-Scalable Equivariant Gaussian Process Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21085)
*Tim Steinert,David Ginsbourger*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究等变高斯过程（GP）在大规模科学问题中的可扩展性问题，通过建立GP等变性与条件化之间关系的结果，构造等变稀疏GP，并实例化出一类无积分等变核，实现"三重可扩展"的等变高斯过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程能提供有原则的概率预测并编码先验知识（包括等变性），但计算代价限制了其在大规模科学问题中的应用；等变神经网络虽常用却缺乏GP所具备的不确定性量化能力（如分子研究中很重要）；高维输入和大对称群进一步要求可扩展性。

Method: 建立关于GP等变性与条件化相互作用的理论结果，并借助合适的均值函数与协方差核获得等变稀疏GP；用一类灵活的无积分等变核实例化该框架；提出稀疏变分GP（SVGP），并开发无矩阵等变全GP实现（结合精确Kronecker约简与预处理共轭梯度求解）；同时比较等变稀疏GP中诱导点选择的不同方法。

Result: 在合成SO(2)等变场以及基于量子化学模拟的N-甲基甲酰胺电偶极矩预测任务上，实现了准确且具备不确定性感知的预测，计算成本仅为经典GP推断的一小部分。

Conclusion: 等变稀疏高斯过程框架（含SVGP与无矩阵全GP实现）可在保持等变性与不确定性量化的同时显著提升可扩展性与数据效率，适用于SO(2)等变向量场与分子性质预测等科学任务。

Abstract: Gaussian processes (GPs) provide principled probabilistic predictions while encoding prior knowledge, including equivariances. Yet, their use in large-scale scientific problems is limited by computational cost. Equivariant neural networks are common but typically lack the uncertainty quantification offered by GPs, which is valuable in applications such as molecular research. High-dimensional inputs and large symmetry groups further demand scalability. We establish results pertaining to the interplay of GP equivariance and conditioning and leverage them to obtain equivariant sparse GPs through suitable mean functions and covariance kernels. We instantiate this framework with a flexible class of integration-free equivariant kernels, yielding scalable and data-efficient GP inference. In particular, we introduce triply scalable equivariant Gaussian processes.
  We employ equivariant sparse variational Gaussian processes for $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant vector fields and molecular property prediction. Alongside the SVGP, we develop a matrix-free equivariant full-GP implementation that combines an exact Kronecker reduction with preconditioned conjugate-gradient solves, enabling fast and scalable evaluation of the full joint predictive density. We further compare different approaches for selecting inducing points in the equivariant sparse GP models. Our test cases include synthetic $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant fields as well as the prediction of electric dipole moments of N-methylformamide based on quantum chemistry simulations, achieving accurate, uncertainty-aware predictions at a fraction of the computational cost of classical GP inference.

</details>


### [161] [Diagonalized Attention for Individualized Regression: Latent-Row Localization and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21320)
*Borui Peng,Liwei Lin,Feifei Wang,Long Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出面向矩阵值协变量的个体化稀疏回归框架，引入对角化注意力机制，用query-key分数定位样本特定的信号行，用value矩阵做回归；理论证明单头/多头模型可高概率恢复潜在信号行并给出预测风险界，实验验证了预测与定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代文本与图像表示常为矩阵值（行对应token、patch等局部特征）。预测信息通常是稀疏但样本特异的，而经典稀疏回归假设共同支撑集，难以适应这种异质性。

Method: 形式化矩阵值协变量下的个体化稀疏回归：每个观测有自身关注的行，回归效应在总体间共享。提出对角化注意力机制，query-key分数用于定位样本特定信号行，value矩阵用于下游回归。参数量与样本量无关，且无需响应即可为新观测识别关注行。

Result: 建立存在性定理：在合适的分数分离与集中条件下，单头与多头对角化注意力模型以高概率恢复潜在信号行，并给出预测风险界。仿真在回归与误设定分类中，不同样本量、维度和信号基数下均展现良好的预测与定位能力。真实情感分析显示分类精度提升且token选择可解释。

Conclusion: 该理论为注意力评分机制如何定位异质矩阵值数据中的样本特定信号提供了统计学解释。

Abstract: Modern text and image representations are often matrix-valued, with rows corresponding to tokens, patches, or other local feature vectors. Predictive information is often sparse but sample-specific, making classical sparse regression methods with a common support poorly suited to this heterogeneity. This paper formalizes an individualized sparse regression framework for matrix-valued covariates in which each observation has its own rows of interest, while the associated regression effects are shared across the population. To estimate this model, we introduce a diagonalized attention mechanism that uses query--key scores to localize sample-specific signal rows and a value matrix for downstream regression. The proposed method has a parameter dimension independent of sample size and can identify rows of interest for new observations without their responses. We establish existence theorems showing that, under suitable score-separation and concentration conditions, single-head and multi-head diagonalized attention models recover the latent rows with high probability, yielding prediction risk bounds. Our theory therefore provides a statistical explanation of how attention-based scoring localizes sample-specific signals in heterogeneous matrix-valued data. Simulations demonstrate strong prediction and localization in regression and misspecified classification across varying sample sizes, dimensions, and signal cardinalities. Real sentiment analyses show improved classification accuracy and interpretable token selection.

</details>


### [162] [Sparse Identification for Automatic Large-Scale Screening: A Constraint-Aware Framework with Ultra Fast Decoding Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21321)
*Jianing Li,Li Chai,Yingcheng Lai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: LoSc：一种超快速、准确且有理论保证的大规模群体检测框架，通过逻辑解码算法在O(klogn)次混合检测内识别所有阳性样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情早期需通过大规模筛查识别少数感染者，但试剂与检测能力有限。现有群体检测方法要么计算复杂度过高，要么识别精度低，且缺乏在样本使用约束和稀释效应等硬约束下的稀疏识别理论分析。

Method: 提出Logic Screening（LoSc）方法：引入具有简单选择策略的新型解码算法；解码仅依赖逻辑运算，可直接硬件实现；在池化设计中显式建模稀释效应和样本使用约束，并建立理论保证以指导最优池化配置。

Result: 仅需O(klogn)次混池检测即可识别所有阳性；解码仅需逻辑运算，实现超快计算；理论保证指导最优池化配置；大量仿真验证了其有效性、高效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: LoSc为自动大规模筛查提供了快速且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: In the early stages of a pandemic, identification of a small number of infected individuals through large-scale screening is critical for pandemic control, yet remains challenging under limited reagents and testing capacity. Existing group testing methods suffer from either high computational complexity or low identification accuracy. Even worse, no available methods provide theoretically rigorous analysis for sparse identification with hard constraints caused by the sample usage constraint and the dilution effect existing ubiquitously in practical applications. In this article, we propose the Logic Screening method (LoSc), an ultra fast, accurate, and theoretically grounded framework for large-scale screening. LoSc introduces a novel decoding algorithm with a very simple selection strategy, achieving identification of all positives with only O(klogn) pooled tests. The decoding relies only on logical operations, enabling direct hardware implementation and yielding ultra fast computational implementation. Moreover, LoSc explicitly incorporates dilution and sample usage constraints into pooling designs, and establishes theoretical guarantees to guide optimal pooling configurations. Extensive simulations confirm the superior effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability. We believe LoSc offers a fast and reliable solution for automatic large-scale screening.

</details>


### [163] [Brownian Heads for Deep ReLU Representations: Activation Mass and the Cost of Same-Sample Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21422)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari,Nicole Mücke*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究深度ReLU表示后接有界范数预测器（Brownian heads，位于加性/Levy-Brownian RKHS中）的条件经验Rademacher复杂度，重点刻画"同一样本上选择特征并拟合预测器"所引入的选择成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度表示学习通常在同一样本上既选择隐藏特征又拟合最终预测器，然而在特征选择之后再做固定特征分析会忽略选择成本。作者希望显式分离"已实现尺度"与"选择复杂度"，弥补现有分析对选择代价刻画不足的缺陷。

Method: 针对固定表示，论文基于激活质量（观测隐藏向量平均范数）推导精确对偶恒等式与紧界；在同一样本选择下，表示上确界诱导出二次Rademacher过程，并用Brownian layer-cake与Gaussian投影恒等式将其约化为逐坐标或有符号投影阈值迹，从而分离实现尺度与选择复杂度。对输入两两不同的样本，构造显式标量ReLU族以匹配有限迹与VC速率。

Result: 推导出固定表示下的精确对偶恒等式与紧界；将二次Rademacher过程约化为坐标/符号投影阈值迹形式；显式标量ReLU族在已实现迹-包络水平上与有限迹及VC速率在通用常数内匹配；通过induced-norm收缩得到矩形、秩亏ReLU网络的架构级界；实验验证了紧界与速率，展示固定激活质量下的选择间隙，并评估了Brownian heads的预测可行性。

Conclusion: 研究表明显式建模选择成本对深度表示泛化分析至关重要，Brownian head框架能够以迹-包络形式清晰分离实现尺度与选择复杂度，并为ReLU网络给出紧的架构级复杂度界，理论与实验一致支持其有效性。

Abstract: Deep representation learning often selects hidden features and fits the final predictor on the same sample, so fixed-feature analysis performed after selection can omit selection cost. We study the conditional empirical Rademacher complexity of deep ReLU representations followed by bounded-norm predictors in additive or Lévy-Brownian RKHSs, termed Brownian heads. For a fixed representation, we derive an exact dual identity and sharp bounds in terms of activation mass, the average norm of the observed hidden vectors. Under same-sample selection, the representation supremum induces a quadratic Rademacher process. Brownian layer-cake and Gaussian-projection identities reduce it to coordinatewise or signed projected threshold traces, separating realized scale from selection complexity. For samples with pairwise-distinct inputs, explicit scalar ReLU families match the finite-trace and VC rates up to universal constants at the realized trace-and-envelope level. Induced-norm contraction also yields architecture-level bounds for rectangular, rank-deficient ReLU networks. Experiments verify the sharp bounds and rates, exhibit a selection gap at fixed activation mass, and assess the predictive feasibility of Brownian heads.

</details>


### [164] [Improving the Predictive Performance of Bootstrap Aggregating by Dirichlet Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21454)
*Quoc Viet Le,Joonha Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文基于Breiman关于降低树间相关性可提升随机森林的观察，提出Dirichlet-Multinomial Bagging Random Forest (DM)与Dirichlet-Weighted Random Forest (DW)两种变体，通过浓度参数α调制样本重加权，并给出理论判据指导轻量调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Breiman曾指出，在不削弱单棵树性能的前提下降低树间相关性可改进随机森林。本文受此原理启发，试图通过调制样本重加权来进一步降低树间相关性，从而提升随机森林性能。

Method: 提出两种变体：DM（Dirichlet-Multinomial Bagging Random Forest）与DW（Dirichlet-Weighted Random Forest），二者均通过浓度参数α>0调制样本重加权；同时给出一个简洁的理论判据，用于判定变体何时与标准随机森林行为无异，并据此设计轻量调参策略。

Result: 在公开分类基准的受控评测中，DM与DW表现出一致竞争力，且常优于其他随机森林基线，额外运行时间可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过Dirichlet机制调制样本重加权以降低树间相关性，可在几乎不增加运行成本的前提下提升随机森林性能，理论判据亦为调参提供了有效指导。

Abstract: We revisit Breiman's observation that reducing inter-tree correlation without weakening individual trees can improve random forests. Building on this principle, we introduce two variants: Dirichlet-Multinomial Bagging Random Forest (DM) and Dirichlet-Weighted Random Forest (DW). Both modulate sample reweighting via a concentration parameter $α>0$. We provide a simple theoretical criterion that clarifies when these variants behave indistinguishably from standard random forests, and we use it to guide a lightweight tuning strategy. In a controlled evaluation on public classification benchmarks, DM and DW are consistently competitive and often stronger than other random-forest (RF) baselines, with negligible additional runtime.

</details>


### [165] [How Many Posterior Samples? Calibrated Stopping for Adaptive Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21813)
*Vincent Corlay,Andriy Enttsel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究面向分类的自适应感知中的“停止层”，即在给定后验样本及其类别投票的情况下，如何把投票转化为类别判定。研究表明常见的阈值式插入规则（plug-in）并不提供置信保证；作者转而校准固定样本规则与有限时域序贯规则以满足给定的误判概率，并分析精确截断（curtailment）以降低后验采样成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分类导向的自适应感知中，后验样本既能指导下一步感知方向，其类别标签又充当对候选类别的投票并决定是否继续感知。然而，如何由这些投票做出最终判定（停止层）本身缺乏严格的置信保证，尤其是阈值式规则容易被误用为置信保证，因此需要研究可校准、可控误判概率的停止规则。

Method: 作者聚焦停止层，不改变后验采样器与感知方向。首先指出插入式阈值规则的缺陷：当真实投票质量恰等于阈值时，该规则约有一半概率做出判定，故阈值本身并非置信保证。随后提出并校准两类替代规则：固定样本规则与有限时域序贯规则，使其满足预设的误报概率；并研究精确截断（exact curtailment），即当固定池规则的最终判定已被强制确定时提前停止。进一步推导单轮判定概率如何沿感知路径决定后验样本成本与分类精度。

Result: 在 MNIST 上，结合 DDRM 与固定的 PCA 引导探测序列，截断最多可节省 62% 的后验样本。在匹配工作点下，序贯停止在所评估的规则中成本降低最多。在高精度情形下，同一序贯规则可以用更多后验样本换取更少的测量次数。

Conclusion: 阈值式插入规则不能作为置信保证；通过将固定样本规则和有限时域序贯规则校准到指定误判概率，并结合精确截断，可在不改变采样器与感知方向的前提下显著降低后验采样成本；序贯停止在成本效率上最优，并可在高精度场景中实现后验样本与测量次数之间的权衡。

Abstract: In classification-oriented adaptive sensing, posterior samples characterize uncertainty at the current measurement state and can serve two roles: they may guide the next sensing direction, while their class labels provide votes for the candidate classes and determine whether sensing should continue. We focus on the stopping layer that turns these votes into a declaration, without modifying the posterior sampler or sensing directions. A natural plug-in rule declares when the observed vote share exceeds a threshold. We show that this threshold is not itself a confidence guarantee: when the underlying vote mass equals the threshold, the plug-in rule declares about half the time. As alternatives, we calibrate a fixed-sample rule and a finite-horizon sequential rule to a prescribed false-declaration probability, and study exact curtailment, which stops a fixed-pool rule once its final verdict is forced. We then derive how one-round declaration probabilities determine posterior-sample cost and classification accuracy along a sensing path. On MNIST with DDRM and a fixed PCA-guided probe sequence, curtailment saves up to 62% of posterior samples. Among the evaluated rules at matched operating points, sequential stopping reduces the cost the most. At a high accuracy, that same sequential rule can trade more posterior samples for fewer measurements.

</details>


### [166] [Riemannian Simultaneous Inference for Tangent Vector Field Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21910)
*Xiaotian Chang,Yangdi Jiang,Qirui Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究无边界黎曼流形上的非参数切向量场回归，提出核估计量并建立同时置信管推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同点的响应属于不同切空间，直接平均无意义，且现有方法缺乏对回归场的整体（同时）不确定性刻画。

Method: 核估计量先平行移动邻近响应至目标切空间，再做体积校正的局部平均；推导一致二阶偏差、有限带宽协方差与随机速率；将切向范数写成单位切丛上的上确界，通过精确协方差白化得到相关长度在底流形上为O(h)、在纤维上为O(1)的单位方差高斯场，结合局部协方差几何得到显式内在常数的Gumbel极限，并与高斯逼近、交叉拟合协方差估计结合构造同时置信管；进一步讨论带宽选择与高阶偏差校正。

Result: 获得Gumbel型极值极限与可行同时置信管，模拟支持推断流程，全球风数据重构展示管的截面可刻画空间变化的不确定性。

Conclusion: 该框架为流形上切向量场回归提供了理论上严谨且可行的同时推断工具。

Abstract: We consider nonparametric tangent vector field regression on a Riemannian manifold without boundary. Because responses at different points lie in different tangent spaces, the proposed kernel estimator first parallel transports nearby responses to the target tangent space and then forms a volume-corrected local average. We first derive its uniform second-order bias, finite-bandwidth covariance, and stochastic rate. For simultaneous inference, the tangent norm is written as a supremum over the unit tangent bundle. Exact covariance whitening gives a unit-variance Gaussian field whose correlation length is of order $h$ along the base manifold and of order one along the fibre. Its local covariance geometry leads to a Gumbel limit with an explicit intrinsic constant. Combining this limit with Gaussian approximation and cross-fitted covariance estimation yields a feasible simultaneous confidence tube for the regression field. We further discuss improved finite-sample inference with bandwidth selection and high-order bias corrections. Simulations on various manifolds support the proposed inference procedure. A randomized reconstruction of global wind data illustrates how the tube's cross-sections describe spatially varying uncertainty.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Schedule optimization for tau-leaping in masked discrete diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21960)
*Cecilia Secchi,Giacomo Zanella*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文分析掩码离散扩散模型中tau-leaping采样的因式分解误差ε_fact，通过引入依赖密度ρ刻画其积分表示，导出有限K下的递归平稳方程与唯一最优解，并在N,K→∞联合极限下显式刻画最优平滑调度，区分ρ收敛与退化两种情形对误差阶的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码离散扩散模型常用tau-leaping加速采样，每步并行揭示多个坐标，但用乘积分布替代联合条件分布会引入因式分解误差ε_fact，即使在预测器完美学习时也存在。需要理论理解该误差的结构及调度（denoising schedule）如何影响它，以指导调度选择并揭示随机块大小相对于确定性规划的代价。

Method: 针对N个坐标、K步采样的标准采样器（随机块大小由去噪调度决定），用依赖密度ρ给出ε_fact的精确积分表示，刻画条件依赖随已揭示坐标比例的变化；构造ρ的估计器并量化估计误差对调度选择的影响；对有限K优化问题推导递归平稳方程，并在单调性条件下刻画唯一最优解；在N,K→∞联合极限下解析地刻画最优极限平滑调度，并比较随机块大小与确定性规划的代价。

Result: 得到ε_fact以分布相关依赖密度ρ表示的精确积分形式；建立ρ的估计器并分析估计误差对调度的影响；推导有限K优化问题的递归平稳方程并在单调性下证明唯一最优解；在联合极限下给出最优极限平滑调度的显式刻画及随机块大小相对确定性规划的成本；当ρ_N一致收敛到严格正连续剖面时，优化固定平滑调度只能改善主导常数而不能改变ε_fact的N/K标度；当ρ_N退化时，合适调度可相对均匀调度改善渐近阶。基于平稳过程和可交换混合的算例验证了两种机制。

Conclusion: 该工作为掩码离散扩散采样的因式分解误差提供了依赖密度视角的精确刻画，明确了最优去噪调度的结构及其极限行为：在依赖剖面正则收敛时调度优化仅影响常数项，而在依赖退化时调度可改变渐近阶。这为调度选择与估计器设计提供了理论依据，并揭示了随机块大小的内在代价。

Abstract: Masked discrete diffusion models are commonly accelerated using the so-called tau-leaping discretization method, which reveals several coordinates in parallel at each sampling step. The sampler replaces the joint conditional law of each revealed block by a product distribution, incurring a factorization error $\varepsilon_\text{fact}$ present even with perfectly learned predictors. We analyze the standard sampler on $N$ coordinates with $K$ sampling steps, whose random block sizes depend on a denoising schedule. Our analysis uses an exact integral representation of $\varepsilon_\text{fact}$ in terms of a distribution-dependent dependence density $ρ$, which records how conditional dependence evolves as the revealed fraction of coordinates grows. We develop estimators for this profile and quantify how estimation errors affect schedule selection. We derive recursive stationarity equations for the finite-$K$ optimization problem and, under a monotonicity condition, characterize its unique optimizer. In the joint limit $N,K\to\infty$, we obtain an explicit characterization of the optimal limiting smooth schedule and quantify the cost of random block sizes relative to a deterministic planner. When $ρ_N$ converges uniformly to a strictly positive continuous profile, optimizing over fixed smooth schedules can improve the leading constant but not the $N/K$ scaling of $\varepsilon_\text{fact}$. By contrast, if $ρ_N$ degenerates, suitable schedules can improve the asymptotic order relative to the uniform schedule. Examples based on stationary processes and exchangeable mixtures illustrate these two regimes.

</details>


### [168] [The Manifold Hypothesis under Unknown Gaussian Noise:Conditional Certificates and Consistent Dimension Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21311)
*U jin Choi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究带噪数据在显式辨识与正则条件下能对流形假设作出何种推断，提出群体残差证书框架，整合多视角局部化、高斯集中、隶属不确定性与群体迁移，给出覆盖尺度的推论及几何维数的几乎必然恢复结果，并改进样本复杂度条件、控制错误维数输出、处理未知噪声并刻画整数谱计数极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究噪声数据能否以及在何种辨识与正则条件下验证流形假设，明确流形几何维数的可识别性与统计推断极限，并厘清点估计、有限尺度覆盖与证书输出三者的区别。

Method: 构建群体残差证书，结合独立视角局部化、高斯集中、隶属不确定性与群体迁移；复用已有的可矫正性判据导出覆盖尺度结论；利用实际球协方差极限将谱交叉点与几何维数对应；采用双质量证书控制错误几何维数输出；对单次观测在仿射支撑或已知坐标界约束下给出噪声区间与一致的 高斯相关维数估计；借助Ahlfors正则性关联指数与Hausdorff维数；并用Cantor集精确计算检验整数谱计数与相邻半径斜率的极限。

Result: 在局部光滑流形与正Hölder密度下，实际球协方差极限可将谱交叉点识别为几何维数，并证明重复观测下几乎必然的最终恢复；复用精确局部化平均将充分点样本条件从 Nr^{d+4}≫log N 改进为 Nr^d≫log N，复制条件为 kr^2≫log N；双质量证书可在声明类界下控制错误几何维数的输出；单次观测在未知高斯噪声情形下可获得噪声区间与一致的Gaussian相关维数估计；Ahlfors正则性将该指数与Hausdorff维数及齐次光滑类的几何维数等同；精确Cantor计算刻画了整数谱计数与相邻半径斜率的极限。

Conclusion: 带噪数据在适当辨识与正则条件下可对流形假设给出可证明的维数识别与覆盖保证，样本复杂度较已有结果显著改善，并能控制错误维数输出、处理未知噪声，同时明确整数谱方法的适用边界；可复现实验进一步区分点估计、有限尺度覆盖与证书输出三类目标。

Abstract: We study what noisy data can establish about the Manifold Hypothesis under explicit identification and regularity conditions. A population residual certificate combines independent-view localization, Gaussian concentration, membership uncertainty, and population transfer. Existing rectifiability criteria then yield a covered-scale consequence. For a local smooth manifold with positive Hölder density, the actual-ball covariance limit identifies the spectral crossing with geometric dimension. We prove almost-sure eventual recovery under repeated observations. Reusing accurate localization averages improves the sufficient point-sample condition from $Nr^{d+4}\gg\log N$ to $Nr^d\gg\log N$, with replication $kr^2\gg\log N$. A two-mass certificate controls incorrect geometric-dimension emissions under declared class bounds. For single observations with unknown Gaussian noise, affine-support or known coordinate-bound restrictions provide noise intervals and consistent Gaussian correlation-dimension estimators. Ahlfors regularity identifies this exponent with Hausdorff dimension and with the geometric dimension of a homogeneous smooth class. Exact Cantor calculations delineate the limits of integer spectral counts and adjacent-radius slopes. We credit established local PCA, rectifiability, concentration, binomial inference, and deconvolution results before specifying our constructions. Reproducible experiments distinguish point estimation, finite-scale coverage, and certificate emission.

</details>


### [169] [Parameter estimation for graphon-interacting particle systems from discrete observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21710)
*Chiara Amorino,Matteo Sfragara*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究图on加权平均场交互粒子系统中漂移与扩散系数的联合参数估计，基于伪似然对比函数，证明了估计量在Δ_n→0与N→∞下的一致性，并在NΔ_n→0下建立渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典交互粒子系统的参数估计多假设同质交互，极限为i.i.d.结构；而实际异质交互由graphon加权平均场刻画时，极限粒子仅独立但非同分布，带来显著分析困难，本文旨在填补该异质情形下漂移与扩散系数联合估计的理论空白。

Method: 基于固定时间区间[0,T]的离散观测，利用图on加权平均场刻画异质交互，构造基于伪似然的对比函数（contrast function）进行参数估计。

Result: 证明所得估计量在离散化步长Δ_n→0且粒子数N→∞时具有一致性；在附加条件NΔ_n→0下建立渐近正态性，表明尽管极限分布不满足同分布假设，graphon结构仍可被严格处理。

Conclusion: 该工作为graphon加权异质交互粒子系统的参数估计提供了严格的渐近理论，拓展了平均场交互模型统计推断的适用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the joint parameter estimation of drift and diffusion coefficients for heterogeneously interacting particle systems. Unlike the homogeneous setting, the interactions are governed by a graphon-weighted mean-field framework, which introduces significant analytical complexity: while homogeneous systems yield i.i.d. limits, our setting results in particles that are independent but non-identically distributed in the limit. Based on discrete observations of the system over a fixed time interval $[0, T]$, we propose a contrast function based on a pseudo-likelihood approach. We prove the consistency of the resulting estimators as the discretization step $Δ_n \to 0$ and the number of particles $N \to \infty$. Furthermore, we establish asymptotic normality under the additional constraint $N Δ_n \to 0$, demonstrating that the underlying graphon structure can be rigorously handled despite the lack of identical distribution in the limit.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Layerwise Decoupling for Stable Structured Sparsification of Fully Connected Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21126)
*Charles Kulick,Armenak Petrosyan,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦的逐层结构化稀疏方法，对预训练网络全连接层逐层剪枝，证明其等价于特定联合惩罚，并表现出比耦合方法更稳健的剪枝特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合惩罚所有层的方法在结构化剪枝中存在正则强度可用范围窄、易发生灾难性过度剪枝的问题，需要更稳健的逐层稀疏方案。

Method: 提取浅层两层子网络，对内层权重归一化，对外层权重矩阵施加结构化组惩罚，按层序贯处理以剪除神经元并缩减每层宽度；对任意正齐次激活函数证明该约束解耦目标在最优处等价于内、外层权重上的特定联合惩罚，从而得到简洁的投影与近端形式。

Result: 该解耦重构比耦合方法更稳健：数值实验中正则强度的可用范围更宽，灾难性过度剪枝率低于所测联合基线，同时保持相当的精度。

Conclusion: 结论在受控分类与稀疏恢复研究中得到验证，并在高维PINN压力测试及OPT-1.3B前馈层中考察了适用范围。

Abstract: We propose a decoupled, layerwise method for structurally sparsifying the fully connected layers of pretrained neural networks. Rather than penalizing all layers jointly, our approach extracts shallow two-layer subnetworks, normalizes the inner weights, and applies a structured group penalty to the outer weight matrix of each block, processing layers sequentially to prune neurons and reduce the width of each layer. We prove that the constrained decoupled objective is equivalent at optimality to a specific joint penalty on the inner and outer weights, for any positively homogeneous activation, and thus admits a clean projected and proximal formulation. Our central finding is that this decoupled reformulation is more robust than coupled methods. In numerical experiments it provides a wider usable range of the regularization strength and a lower rate of catastrophic over-pruning than the tested joint baseline while maintaining comparable accuracy. We establish these properties in controlled classification and sparse-recovery studies, and examine their scope in a high-dimensional PINN stress test and in the feed-forward layers of OPT-1.3B.

</details>


### [171] [Multi-Domain Clustering via Measure Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21664)
*Rafael Pereira Eufrazio,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Charles Casimiro Cavalcante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于测度量化的多域聚类通用框架，通过最小化各域概率测度与原型测度间的概率度量（如Sinkhorn散度、MMD）学习共享簇原型，并支持最近质心或最优传输两种分配策略及小批量优化，在多域基准上优于基线且可扩展至数十万样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚类（如K-means）以质心为基础，主要面向单一数据域。面对来自多个域的样本时，需要一种能够学习共享簇原型并兼容不同域分布的多域聚类方法。

Method: 给定多个域的样本，学习一组共享的簇原型，通过最小化每个域的概率测度与原型测度之间的概率度量（Sinkhorn散度或最大均值差异MMD）来拟合。数据点分配采用最近质心或最优传输两种方式，后者以协作策略耦合域内所有样本。采用小批量优化策略，使拟合与分配均可扩展，降低内存与计算开销。

Result: 在涵盖图像、音频与传感器数据的5个多域基准上，基于Sinkhorn的方法持续优于经典聚类及多域聚类基线，且在扩展到数十万样本时仍保持该优势。

Conclusion: 基于测度量化的多域聚类框架有效统一了共享原型学习与多域分配，Sinkhorn方法在多个基准上表现稳定优越并具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental task in data analysis, typically addressed through centroid-based methods such as K-means. In this work, we present a general framework for multi-domain clustering via measure quantization: given samples from multiple domains, we learn a shared set of cluster prototypes by minimizing a probability metric, such as the Sinkhorn divergence or the Maximum Mean Discrepancy, between each domain's probability measure and the measure of prototypes. Data points are then assigned to clusters either via nearest centroid, or via optimal transport, a collaborative strategy that couples all samples within a domain. A mini-batch optimization strategy makes both fitting and assignment scalable, reducing memory and computational cost while preserving clustering performance. Experimental results on 5 multi-domain benchmarks spanning image, audio and sensor data show that our Sinkhorn-based method consistently outperforms classical and multi-domain clustering baselines, and that this advantage persists when scaling to hundreds of thousands of samples.

</details>


### [172] [Geometric Mean Pooling for Equal-Weight Multiplicative Coarse-Graining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21876)
*Ang-Kun Wu,Fangdi Wen,Jingtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何均值池化（GMP），一种结合特征符号乘积与特征幅值几何均值的带符号池化算子，在乘法组合型任务上优于平均/最大池化，但在图像和分子任务上效果依赖表征与参数化，属互补性归纳偏置而非通用替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均池化具有加性偏置、最大池化具有极值偏置，二者均缺乏对"联合符号+乘法尺度"信息的刻画能力；受量子多体物理中局域到全局组合的启发，作者希望设计一种无学习参数、能保留乘法统计特性的池化算子。

Method: 提出几何均值池化（GMP）：将特征符号的乘积与特征幅值的几何均值相结合，构成带符号池化算子；进一步构造非重叠的层次化GMP，以保持对应的全局乘法统计量；在合成序列任务、迭代粗粒化、图像分类与分子亲脂性回归上进行评估。

Result: 在合成任务上，GMP比平均/最大池化更准确地恢复基于乘积的信号，并在测试的乘法输入噪声水平下保持预测性能；在图像与分子数据上，其效果依赖表征形式、目标参数化方式以及局部/全局池化的位置。

Conclusion: GMP可作为一种互补的、依赖任务机制的归纳偏置，适用于等权乘法组合合理的场景，而非标准池化算子的通用替代方案。

Abstract: As an alternative to the additive and extremal biases of average and max pooling, we introduce Geometric Mean Pooling (GMP), a signed pooling operator that combines the product of feature signs with the geometric mean of feature magnitudes. Motivated by local-to-global composition in quantum many-body physics, GMP retains both joint sign information and a characteristic multiplicative scale without introducing learnable pooling parameters. We show that non-overlapping hierarchical GMP preserves the corresponding global multiplicative statistic and evaluate it on synthetic sequence tasks, iterative coarse-graining, image classification, and molecular lipophilicity regression. On the synthetic tasks, GMP recovers product-based signals more accurately than average and max pooling and maintains predictive performance under the tested levels of multiplicative input noise. On image and molecular data, however, its effectiveness depends on the representation, target parameterization, and placement of local and global pooling. These results position GMP as a complementary, regime-dependent inductive bias for tasks in which equal-weight multiplicative composition is plausible, rather than as a universal replacement for standard pooling operators.

</details>


### [173] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"稀疏先验"类与"稀疏维度"概念，证明在k-稀疏先验下分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为Ω(√(k/n))，并给出TV距离下匹配的上界，表明合适的先验可克服对样本数n的维数灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI中，从n个样本学习d维分布的理论保证随维度以O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})退化（虽为minimax最优）。作者假设现有界过于悲观，因为仅靠光滑性假设不足以刻画实际应用中分布的固有结构，因此需要引入能捕捉这种结构的先验假设。

Method: 引入"稀疏先验"类，并定义"稀疏维度"作为所有分布空间上先验稀疏性的度量；在此基础上分析k-稀疏先验下的分布学习，推导贝叶斯风险下界，并在温和附加假设下推导TV距离的上界；同时证明贝叶斯设定下分布学习与学习采样的统计等价性。

Result: 在常见距离度量下，k-稀疏先验的分布学习取得Ω(√(k/n))的贝叶斯风险下界；在温和附加假设下，针对TV距离得到匹配的上界（渐近于n,k最多差对数项）。由于分布学习与学习采样的统计等价性，该结果同样适用于学习采样。

Conclusion: k仍可依赖于维度d或某个内在维度概念，但结果表明在合适的先验下学习能够克服关于样本数n的维数灾难，说明现有基于光滑性假设的悲观界可被结构性先验假设改进。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [174] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个端到端商业智能（BI）评测基准 BI-Bench，发现现有前沿大模型准确率不足 50%，并设计工具增强的 BI-Agent 及后训练框架，最多带来 40 个百分点的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 BI 流程需用户完成识别相关表、数据转换、构建连接关系等繁琐准备步骤，才能回答业务问题，复杂且耗时。鉴于大语言模型在数据处理上的强大能力，作者希望研究其能否端到端回答 BI 问题，从而免去人工准备环节。

Method: 1）从公开来源收集真实 BI 项目，并人工从用户仪表盘中抽取（问题，标准答案）对，构建 BI-Bench 基准；2）设计工具增强的 BI-Agent，将 BI 工作流分解为结构化数据上的子任务（如搜索、连接、转换），并跨 BI 阶段编排专门的数据管理方法；3）提出后训练框架，从真实 BI 项目中合成训练轨迹，对 BI-Agent 进行监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL）训练。

Result: 即使是前沿大模型在 BI-Bench 上表现也很差，准确率低于 50%。BI-Agent 相比原生大模型可获得最高 40 个百分点的准确率提升；经过后训练的 BI-Agent 还能再获得最高 30 个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在复杂 BI 工作流中结合工具增强推理与领域特定后训练具有重要意义，并为未来研究指明了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [175] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ETA 提出一种端到端可训练的弹性阈值注意力架构，通过从查询表示预测动态上下文阈值，在训练中用乘性抑制代替删除，实现稀疏训练与硬件加速解码，同时保持稠密模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码时，海量 KV 缓存引发严重的内存带宽瓶颈。稀疏注意力虽通过选择性加载缓解该问题，但僵化的启发式规则会丢弃必要上下文，导致模型质量下降。因此需要一种既能实现硬件加速解码、又不牺牲稠密模型质量的方案。

Method: 提出 Elastic Threshold Attention（ETA），一种端到端可训练架构。ETA 从查询表示直接预测动态、上下文相关的阈值，使模型在困难检索或推理步骤分配类稠密上下文，同时剪枝常规 token。训练时采用乘性抑制（multiplicatively suppresses）将低于阈值的 logits 平滑压向零，而非直接删除，从而避免表示坍缩，形成分布式概率储备库。推理时硬剪枝无信息 KV 块，并吸收粗粒度 GPU 块选择带来的附带 token。还实现 Triton 自定义解码核，用缓存几何-概率界在 O(1) 时间筛选 KV 块，并提供离线校准算法冻结逐头常量阈值。

Result: 1.45B 预训练 ETA 模型在语言建模、常识推理、长上下文检索任务上媲美稠密注意力，训练稀疏度约 85%、活跃解码密度约 38%。Triton 解码核在最长 512K token 序列上相对 FlashAttention-2 实现最高 2.5 倍墙钟解码加速。离线校准额外减少 27% 注意力计算量。

Conclusion: ETA 通过可学习的动态阈值与乘性抑制训练策略，消除了初始 token 上的局部注意力汇，兼顾稀疏效率与稠密质量，并借助定制内核与离线校准进一步降低推理开销，为长上下文高效解码提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens.
